## Open questions (resolve when reached; record the answer here)

1. **D-K3** — Should Left at the start edge in Free mode jump to the previous cell (mirror of Right at end edge)? Default: no (`LEFT_EDGE_LEAVES_CELL = false`).
2. **D-X1** — Is a marked cell's own trailing number counted? Default: no, it is a label only.
3. **A6** — Is the inferred undo scope (bill-wide undo/redo + trash + revisions + draft recovery) what the owner meant by "undo, redo and related features"?
4. **D-C1** — A row with qty/rate but no item name: counted as an item and flagged "missing name" (default) — confirm.
5. Should unnamed adjustments (total with no name) be allowed? Default: `partial`, not totalled.
6. Professional-print visual layout: content is specified; arrangement needs owner review (Phase 34).
7. Shortcut rebinding UI: not in scope unless the owner asks.
8. Party sync between branches: out of scope for v1 (bills carry party snapshots); revisit after Phase 60 if wanted.
9. Does the owner want an item-name autocomplete from past bills (not a catalogue)? Not requested; not planned.
10. (Milestone 8) Calculator on-screen keypad in v1? Default: no.
11. (Milestone 8) Privacy: `docs/reference/legacy_import/Bills_JSON.zip` holds real party names and phone numbers; remove it before sharing the repo/zip outside the owner's control.
12. (Milestone 8) Old branches cannot see new optional snapshot fields; all branches should be updated before relying on extra cells / round-off sync.
13. (Milestone 9) Esc rules [A]: Dashboard = nothing; History/Customers/Settings -> Dashboard; opened bill -> History; Create -> guard then Dashboard. Owner said 'yes' to the recommended options; confirm when Phase 94 starts.
14. (Milestone 9) Title bar [A]: keep native Windows buttons, colour via titleBarOverlay (not a fully custom bar). Scrollbars [A]: thin and rounded.
15. (Milestone 9) Extra-column Down key [A]: only in extra columns, Down at the bottom-most row adds a tail row.
16. (Milestone 9) New themes need a preview/palette sign-off from the owner before the final values are frozen (Phases 99–102).
17. (Milestone 10) Item sorter [A]: item rules (merges/splits) sync between branches; item-name autocomplete while typing a bill is NOT included (open question for the owner); milestone runs before Phase 69; no AI model in v1 (optional local embedding model only if the rules leave too many leftovers).
18. (Milestone 10) The owner must judge grouping quality on the real bills; the sandbox only measured counts (see `docs/ITEM_SORTER_SPEC.md` section 1).
19. (Milestone 11) Create always opens blank [A]: supersedes the Phase 70 resume/New-button; Save/Discard guard on leaving stays; crash-recovery draft only at app start.
20. (Milestone 11) Delete row / reorder [A]: row selected by clicking the row number or Shift+Space; Delete removes the item cells, rows below move up, extra-column cells stay on their row positions; reorder keys Alt+Up/Down; both subject to the shortcut-conflict test.
21. (Milestone 11) Dashboard tiles [A]: six tiles (Weather, USD/INR, EUR/GBP/AED, Gold & Silver, Clock & date, Indian holidays) in a symmetric grid; separate 'Today' section under the existing tiles; free no-key sources; network only from the main process.
22. (Milestone 11) Notifications [A]: identical toasts replace each other, clear on page change, max 2 visible; the owner has not yet listed the unrelated notices seen on Esc.
23. (Milestone 11) Owner must confirm on the real Windows PC: clipboard (Phase 119), round-off paths (120), HD image width (121), live APIs (125). Sandbox work on these is NOT verification.
24. (Milestone 12) Sync replaces the encrypted postbox completely [A]; one Supabase login per computer, created by the generator query; branch name and PO prefix chosen in Settings, both unique per organisation; no Main role, no read-only branch (not requested).
25. (Milestone 12) "Latest wins" [A]: later skew-corrected `edited_at` decides a conflict, the server version chain orders everything else; the losing version is kept as a revision. Confirm this is what the owner means by "latest version wins".
26. (Milestone 12) Readable bill and customer data (real names, phones) will sit on Supabase [A: accepted]; protection is Auth + RLS + sign-ups disabled + TLS. Owner to confirm.
27. (Milestone 12) Users are created from SQL by writing into Supabase's `auth` tables [A, unproven]; fallback is dashboard-created logins plus `qt_attach_seat`. Free-plan limits (Realtime connections / messages, 7-day pause, no backups) came from third-party pages in a chat and must be re-read on Supabase's own page in Phases 128 and 138.
28. (Milestone 13) Print position: owner paper test needed (Phase 144 item 3). A Settings 'Vertical adjust (mm)' nudge is [A], only if the driver adds a fixed shift that cannot be turned off.

## Standing gaps (not confirmed)

- **Print position (Phase 144, owner report 2026-10-12):** an A4 printout on the owner's printer starts about 65-70 mm down the sheet with about 95 mm blank below (looks vertically centred; side margins look like the designed 10 mm, no shrinking). Source reading finds only a 10 mm top gap and no centring, so the shift is added after the template (Electron print options or the printer driver / Windows setup). Phase 73's "driver" diagnosis was never confirmed on paper. Unresolved until the owner's paper test (Save PDF vs Microsoft Print to PDF vs real printer) is recorded.
- **Milestone 12 (planned 2026-10-12, nothing built or run):** every server item is unproven until the owner runs it on a real Supabase project: `supabase/sql/11`-`16` (not written yet), `91_selftest_v2.sql`, the `qt_create_org` generator (writes Supabase's `auth` tables from SQL; may need the dashboard fallback), Auth sign-ups disabled, RLS isolation, Realtime on the free plan, pause/restore behaviour. The Free-plan numbers in the design came from third-party pages quoted in a chat. The real two-computer cutover (1 927 bills / 12 893 customers) has not been tried. See `docs/SUPABASE_MIGRATIONS.md` (M12 section) for the checklist.

*(Append here, in plain words, every behaviour a session could not actually run or see — real rendering, real printing, real clipboard paste into WhatsApp, real Supabase. Never write "verified" for something that wasn't run.)*

- **Phase 68 (performance, not measured on the real app):** nothing was timed on a release build, in Electron or on Windows; real start-up time (new log line `Start-up timing ...`), real keystroke-to-paint (Phase 24 harness, still never run in Chromium), PDF / image time inside Electron, big-list rendering in a real window and the **one-hour renderer + Chromium memory soak** are all owed to the owner (steps in `docs/reference/qa/PERF_ACCEPTANCE.md`). Sandbox numbers are in the Performance log.
- **Phase 1:** `npm run dev` / `electron-vite build` were **not run** (owner said skip builds). The placeholder window rendering "Quot3 — loading" and the main-process `sqlite_version()` log from `src/main/index.ts` are therefore **unconfirmed**; owner should run `npm install && npm run dev` locally. What WAS proven: `better-sqlite3` loads in Electron 44.6.0 (xvfb) via a standalone script.
- **Phase 1:** `electron-rebuild` from source could not be tested (sandbox blocks electronjs.org headers). Not needed with N-API prebuilds, but untested on Windows (`prebuilds/win32-x64.node` is shipped in the package; not executed here).
- **Phase 3:** `electron-vite build` / `npm run dev` still not run (owner: skip builds). The visual/behaviour proof used an esbuild bundle of the real main/preload/renderer + Tailwind CLI CSS in Electron under xvfb (screenshots of both themes inspected). Not exercised: Vite HMR path, the production meta-CSP plugin, Tooltip hover/focus timing, Dialog focus trap / Esc / focus-return, Toast auto-dismiss, ConfirmDialog (rendered code only; no interaction test), a real Windows font stack (Segoe UI Variable) — screenshots used the Linux fallback font.
- **Phase 3:** Active theme is in-memory in main (resets each launch) until Phase 5 persists `ui.theme`. Design-preview is reachable only via `#/dev/design-preview` (temporary hash switch in `main.tsx`; Phase 4 replaces it with the router).
- **Phase 5:** The real `openDatabase()` call inside `src/main/index.ts`, the `app.getAppPath()/src/main/db/migrations` lookup, the new IPC channels (`settings:*`, `app:getDbInfo`) over the real bridge, and the Dashboard dev line ("DB schema v1/1 · writable · next PO Q3-0001") were **not run** (no build/dev, owner: skip). The DB layer itself is proven by Vitest against real temp-file SQLite under **Node 22**, not under Electron's ABI in this phase (Phase 1 proved the binding loads in Electron). Also unconfirmed: behaviour on Windows paths/file locks (WAL files, `VACUUM INTO` target on Windows).
- **Phase 4:** **Nothing in the new UI was run or seen** — no `electron-vite build`, no `npm run dev`, no Electron window, no screenshots (owner: skip builds). Proven only by `npm run typecheck` + Vitest on the pure logic (`shortcuts.ts`, `routes.ts`, `commandSearch.ts`). Unconfirmed: SideRail/Dashboard/palette/cheat-sheet rendering and styling, real keyboard flow (Ctrl+0..4, Ctrl+K, F1, Esc back, rail arrow keys), focus-to-page-region after navigation, focus return after closing the palette, Tooltip on rail items. Owner: run `npm run dev` and walk it by keyboard.
- **Phase 4:** The Phase 1–2 placeholder ("Quot3 — loading" + version/data-dir line from `window.quot3.app.getInfo()`) is no longer rendered by the renderer; `getInfo` is still wired and tested in main, it just has no on-screen consumer until a later phase (About/Settings).
- **Phase 2:** `electron-vite build` / `npm run dev` still not run (owner: skip builds). The headless proof used an **esbuild bundle of the real `src/main/index.ts` + `src/preload/index.ts`** with a tiny test HTML page, not the React renderer. So the Vite-built renderer page showing data dir + version, and the build-time `<meta>` CSP injection plugin in `electron.vite.config.ts`, are **unconfirmed** (owner: `npm run dev`).
- **Phase 2:** Not exercised: single-instance lock (second launch focusing first window), `app:openDataFolder` opening Explorer, `will-navigate` blocking (only `window.open` → null and CSP fetch block were observed), the packaged (non-dev) CSP header path, Windows `%LOCALAPPDATA%` resolution on a real Windows machine (unit-tested with injected env only).
- **Phase 1:** `npm install` on Windows (full lifecycle incl. `electron` binary download via its own postinstall) not run on Windows; the sandbox install used `--ignore-scripts` then ran Electron's `install.js` manually.

- **Phase 9:** The grid is read-only and **visually unseen**: nothing was rendered in Electron/Chromium, `electron-vite build` / `npm run dev` were not run (owner: skip). Proven only by typecheck + Vitest (pure view-model in node; React render + render-count counters in **jsdom**). Unconfirmed: the real look of `grid.css` (sticky header, 26px rows, ellipsis, zebra, footer tints, horizontal scroll with many extra columns), **≥ 20 visible rows at 1366×768** (arithmetic says 26px rows fit ~25+ under a ~40px dev bar, but not seen), the `/dev/grid` route + palette entry "Open grid (dev)" (also reachable at `#/dev/grid`), and real scroll/paint performance (no measurement — Phase 24 owns that). jsdom does not run CSS layout, so row height is asserted only as "no inline height; comes from the token".

- **Phase 11 (READ THIS — owner tests this phase hardest):** nothing was run in a real Chromium/Electron: no build, no dev server, no screenshots (owner: skip). The pipeline is proven in **jsdom** only, by dispatching synthetic `keydown` / `beforeinput` / `mousedown` / `mouseup` events and reading `getSelection()` offsets. jsdom has **no contenteditable behaviour and no layout**, so these are UNCONFIRMED in the real app: (a) that `contenteditable="plaintext-only"` on the span really accepts native typing/Backspace/Delete/selection in Free mode; (b) the **mouse path** — `mousedown` on the text sets contenteditable + focuses the span *without* preventDefault and relies on the browser placing the caret at the pointer, then `mouseup` reads that caret → Free; clicking cell padding → Append at end; double-click word select; (c) that `beforeinput` really fires with `historyUndo`/`historyRedo` on Ctrl+Z/Y in a plaintext-only span and `preventDefault` suppresses native undo; (d) the visible active-cell outline (solid = Append, dotted = armed, dashed + tint = Free), the caret being visible in an **empty** cell (`min-width: 2px`), end-of-text scroll in narrow cells (`scrollLeft = scrollWidth`), `scrollIntoView` vs the sticky header (no `scroll-margin` set yet); (e) IME composition (keydown `Process` is passed through untouched — untested); (f) focus return after leaving the grid / window switch (focusout only commits; the cell stays active). If any of (a)–(c) misbehave, the single place to fix is `components/grid/gridController.ts`; the reducer needs no change.

- **Phase 13:** nothing seen in a real browser — the corner-flag marker, `title` tooltip, right-click menu position/focus (`position: fixed` inside the grid root) and Ctrl+Backspace handling in real Chromium are jsdom-simulated only. Owner: `npm run dev` → `#/dev/grid`, type a Total, right-click it, press Ctrl+Backspace.

- **Phase 15 (READ THIS — unseen in a real browser):** nothing ran in Chromium/Electron. Unconfirmed: (a) that `contenteditable="plaintext-only"` keeps styled child `<span class="q3-run">` nodes intact while the user types/deletes across run boundaries (native typing inside a span should inherit its style; the commit diffs the final text into the runs either way); (b) caret/selection restore after `renderRuns` repaints the spans (`setSelectionRange` walks text nodes; jsdom-proven only); (c) focus return after the palette/toolbar; (d) the look of red/bold/highlight (`--rt-red`, `--rt-highlight` tokens) in both themes; (e) palette position (`position: fixed` at the cell's bottom-left) near screen edges; (f) Ctrl+H / Ctrl+B / Ctrl+Shift+C/K/R not being grabbed by Electron/Chromium before the page (no app menu exists yet, so none expected). If (a)/(b) misbehave, the single place to fix is `components/grid/gridController.ts` (`formatActive`, `paint`) + `gridDom.ts` (`renderRuns`, `placeBoundary`).

- **Phase 16 (READ THIS — unseen with a real clipboard/mouse):** nothing ran in Chromium/Electron. jsdom only: synthetic `copy`/`cut`/`paste` events with a fake `clipboardData`, synthetic `mousedown`/`mousemove`/`mouseup`. Unconfirmed: (a) that Chromium puts the custom `application/x-quot3-grid` flavour on the OS clipboard via `clipboardData.setData` AND hands it back in the paste event (if it doesn't, a Quot3→Quot3 paste silently falls back to plain TSV: values arrive, formatting does not); (b) a real Excel block pasting cleanly (Excel's CRLF + trailing CRLF, quoted multi-line cells) — the parser is unit-tested against those shapes, not against a live Excel; (c) the palette actions: Copy/Cut use `document.execCommand('copy'|'cut')`, Paste uses `clipboard:readGrid`; neither channel call has been exercised over the real bridge, and Electron 44's async `clipboard.read()` custom-flavour lookup (`web application/x-quot3-grid`) is a best guess — `readGrid` may only ever return `tsv`; (d) mouse-drag range: that the browser's native text-selection drag across contenteditable spans is fully suppressed by `removeAllRanges()` + `user-select:none` and that `mousemove` targets the hovered cell while a drag started inside a contenteditable span; the drag range does not auto-scroll; (e) the status-line paste report text/placement. If (a)/(d) misbehave the single place to fix is `components/grid/gridController.ts` (`onCopy`/`onPaste`/`onMouseMove`).

- **Phase 24 (READ THIS — the gate's key number is missing):** (a) **Real keydown→paint was never measured.** `/dev/perf` (`pages/dev/PerfDev.tsx`) is written to do it (rAF + MessageChannel after each synthetic key, paced at 240 Hz via `setTimeout`, `long-animation-frame` observer) but it was **never loaded in Chromium** — typecheck + a jsdom mount test only. Unconfirmed: that the page runs, that `setTimeout` pacing really reaches ~4.17 ms (browser timer clamping), that `flush: fn => fn()` is enough (the controller uses `flushSync`; `switchFormat` too), and that synthetic `KeyboardEvent`s drive the same path as real keys (they do in jsdom). On a 60 Hz display a key landing mid-frame is measured to that frame (up to 16.7 ms) — judge p95 against the **logic** row too, or use a 240 Hz display. (b) The jsdom logic numbers are not a proxy for the budget in either direction. (c) The cell-element cache added to `gridController.ts` (see Phase 24 log) is jsdom/suite-proven only; if arrow/Tab ever lands on a stale cell in the real app, the single place to look is `cellEl()` (clear `cellCache` there). (d) Gate items not provable here: 20 rows at 1366×768, append-caret hand-test, Red+Bold in the real caret model, long-text layout — all inherited Phase 9/11/15/23 standing gaps, now owed to one owner session.

(b) `electronPdfFiles.ts` (Windows Save dialog with overwrite confirmation, remembered folder, `fs.writeFile`, `shell.openPath`) is untested code; a file open in another program (EBUSY) is reported through `PRINT_FAILED` by design but never seen. (c) The dialog's new "Save PDF" button / S key, the toast and the palette actions were proven in jsdom with a fake bridge only. (d) A real PDF opened in a real viewer (page look, ₹ glyph in Adobe/Edge) was not seen — only `pdftotext`/`pdffonts`/`pdfinfo` output.

## Known issues / carried forward

- **From the 2026-10-12 handoff (NOT Milestone 12 scope; each is a separate task):** (1) v1 decrypt pause between two real computers, root cause never confirmed (superseded by M12, which removes the code path). (2) Updater crash in the installed build: `Cannot set properties of undefined (setting 'autoDownload')` at `updaterPort.ts` (`mod.autoUpdater` undefined; probably ESM/CJS interop, fix unconfirmed). (3) Renderer `TypeError ... reading 'toLowerCase'` in `normalizeKey` / `eventToKeys` / `matchShortcut`. (4) False "previous run did not close properly (unclean-exit)" notice after Start fresh. (5) Dashboard weather "operation was aborted" and metals "fetch failed" (real APIs, Phase 125). (6) Image capture: the "legacy" tier fails (771 px) while the "zoom" tier works (2157 px vs 2156 expected). (7) Windows test failures: `security.test.ts` (POSIX fake path through `pathToFileURL`), `conflicts.test.ts` property test times out at 5000 ms, `harness.test.ts` soak seed 11 convergence mismatch (passed 3/3 on Linux, maybe a flake). (8) `electron-builder.yml` needs a `publish:` block for auto-updates. Privacy: `Bills_JSON.zip` holds real names and phone numbers; backups are full private SQLite copies; never commit a license key or password.
- **Doc inconsistency noticed during Milestone 12 planning (flagged, not changed):** the Phase table above still shows rows 92-106, 114 and 115 as ⬜ and the Milestone overview shows Milestone 9 as "Planned", although the Phase log has DONE entries for them (and the Current state says Milestone 10 and 11 are complete). The next session that touches those rows should reconcile them against the Phase log.
- **Milestone 12 planning:** nothing in `supabase/sql/11`+ exists yet; the v1 SQL (01-06, 90) is unchanged and its tables stay in the owner's project until the owner runs `99_drop_v1.sql` (Phase 142).
- **Phase 46:** (1) **"Today" / "this month" use `bill_date`** (the date on the bill, which defaults to the local date when created), live bills only, all branches. A bill dated in the future counts in neither today nor "this month so far" (it still appears in Recent bills). (2) **Top customers** = all-time total billed (live bills), top 5, bill-less customers skipped, ties by name; Enter goes to the Customers page (not straight to that customer's bills). (3) **Recent bills** = newest 8 by bill date then creation time; Enter opens the bill on Create through the unsaved-changes guard. (4) **Drafts banner** is the existing Phase 29 `DraftRecovery` (it uses `drafts:list`); `dashboard:stats` therefore has **no `drafts` field** (contract updated). (5) **Last backup** = mtime of the newest `*.db` in `%LOCALAPPDATA%\Quot3\backups\` (until Phase 50 only pre-migration backups exist); "No backup yet" when none. (6) **Sync status is a placeholder**: always `off`, shown as "Sync: off". (7) **Sparkline** is a plain SVG polyline scaled to the week's largest day (no axis numbers; every dot has a title and the SVG has an aria-label with all seven values). (8) Refresh on mount and window focus only (no push from bill saves). (9) The dev DB-info line and DraftRecovery were kept as they were.
- **Phase 48:** (1) **Round-off toggle NOT built.** `computeBill(format, rows, { roundOff })` exists but none of its callers (`gridModel`, `billModel`, `billTsv` x2, `printModel`) passes it, so a Settings toggle would have changed nothing or made the grid and the print disagree. It needs one shared "bill options" read in all those places - its own small phase; say if wanted. (2) **Print margins NOT editable.** `PAPER_ENGINE_RULES.md` section 7 says margins are fixed by the template; A5 capacity, the overflow measure and `@page` all depend on them. The Print page says so. (3) **Default paper size is not a control:** the same rules file says A5 is the default and the rules pick A4 when needed; the page states that rather than offering a setting that would fight the rules. (4) **Logo:** PNG / JPEG only (checked by bytes, max 512 KB, 16-2000 px); SVG and GIF refused. Stored as `assets/company-logo.png` or `.jpg`; the setting holds only the file name; `printService` turns it into a data URL (a settings value is capped at 64 KB, so the image cannot live there). (5) **PO prefix:** 1-8 chars `A-Z 0-9 - _ /`, saved upper-case; "unique vs known branches" = the branches table (one local branch until sync; the DB also has a unique index). Changing it never touches saved bills or the counter. (6) **Behaviour ranges:** trash 1-365 days (read at launch), left-unlock presses 1-3 (live in the grid), autosave 1-30 s (live, from the next change). A hand-edited out-of-range value reads as the default. (7) **Silent printing** is refused at save when there is no printer name (the print code already ignored it then). (8) **Preview** in Settings uses the real Professional template with the legacy sample bill and the typed values (not the bill you have open). Entry points: sidebar / Ctrl+4, four palette actions "Settings: ...", and an Import parties button on the page.
- **Phase 47:** (1) **Built without the owner's format (the phase said to stop and ask; the owner told this session to commence anyway).** The mapping step makes the importer layout-independent; if the real file has e.g. address split over several columns, or a combined "name + city" cell, that needs a follow-up. (2) **Duplicate rule = same normalised name** (names are unique among live parties, and bills look parties up by name); phone is used to explain ("phone differs") and to warn when a NEW name shares a phone, never to block. The brief said "name_norm + phone" - flip if the owner wants same name with a different phone to import as a second party (would need the unique-name rule relaxed everywhere). (3) **Update mode never blanks a field**; the first of repeated names in the file wins. (4) **One column per field** (no joining of several columns). (5) **No `.xls`** (ExcelJS cannot read it) - the message tells the owner to Save As .xlsx; `.csv` is read with ExcelJS' CSV reader (UTF-8, comma). (6) **Caps:** 10 MB, 20,000 rows. (7) **Undo** soft-deletes the created parties (bills keep their snapshot and `party_id`) and restores the pre-import values of updated ones from `party_imports.before_json`; a party edited after the import is KEPT and counted, not reverted. Only the batch's own undo exists - there is no "undo any older import" UI beyond the done-screen button (`partyImport:list` is ready for it). (8) **Entry points:** Ctrl+Shift+J / palette "Import parties from Excel" and an *Import* button on Customers; Phase 48's Settings page should add its own button calling `usePartyImportStore.getState().start()`. (9) **Migration `0001_party_import`** was written by hand (drizzle-kit not run): `meta/_journal.json` has the entry but there is **no `0001_snapshot.json`** - generate one next time `drizzle-kit generate` is run, or ignore (the runner only reads the journal + SQL). (10) `package.json` / `package-lock.json` changed ONLY by adding `exceljs@4.4.0`.
- **Phase 45:** (1) **Link between a customer and its bills is `bills.party_id`** (set by Phase 27's save by normalised name). Count / last bill / total cover LIVE bills only (trashed bills excluded; restoring a bill brings it back). The total is summed with decimal.js in `shared/customers/customerList.ts`, never in SQL; a bill whose cached total is unreadable counts but adds nothing. (2) **Delete = soft delete** (`parties.deleted_at`, one `audit_log` row `party`/`delete`). The customer's bills keep `party_id` and their party snapshot untouched and stay in History. **If the same name is later typed on a bill or added again, a NEW customer is created and the old bills stay with the deleted one** (they do not re-attach; there is no customer restore UI - owner may ask for one). (3) **Editing a customer never rewrites old bills** (they keep the snapshot they were saved with); a renamed customer still counts its old bills (linked by id), whose History rows keep showing the old name. (4) **Duplicate names are refused** on add and rename (normalised, case / spacing insensitive) with an inline message; keeping a customer's own name is fine. (5) **Search is client-side** over the loaded list: name + phone through the Phase 26 party index (a numeric query = phone digits) and address substring; the contract's `query?` on `customers:list` was dropped. The best match is highlighted so Enter works right after typing. (6) **Inline edit**: Enter / F2 / double-click edits the highlighted row in place; Insert / "Add customer" opens a pinned row above the list; Enter saves, Esc cancels, Tab next field; address is a one-row textarea (Shift+Enter = new line). A new customer clears any search that would hide it. (7) **Keys** (new scope `customers` in `shared/shortcuts.ts`): Ctrl+F search, Insert add, Enter / F2 edit, Right = bills, Delete = delete (asks first); registered for the palette / cheat-sheet; Ctrl+F and Insert are handled by the page, the rest by the list itself. Palette entries off the Customers page just navigate there. (8) **Bills view** reuses `components/history/BillList` (it gained an optional `onEscape`, used so Esc / Left go back to the customer list instead of leaving the page); Enter opens the bill in Create through the unsaved-changes guard. Bills are paged with the History cursor (200 per page). (9) `parties:delete` from the old plan was never added (replaced by `customers:delete`). (10) The list refreshes on mount and when the window regains focus (not while an editor is open); it is not live-pushed from bill saves, so a bill saved on Create shows in the counts the next time the page is opened. (11) `partyService.upsert` (used by bill saves) is unchanged; it still never edits a stored party from a bill.
- **Phase 44:** (1) **Key:** Ctrl+Shift+V = *Revisions* (global scope: the open bill on Create, the highlighted bill on History; from any other page it opens History; pressing it again closes the panel). It was unused in the registry; Chromium/Electron may treat Ctrl+Shift+V as "paste as plain text" in text fields, so the grid's own paste is unaffected only if the global layer sees the key first - **untested in the real window**. (2) **Every stored revision is the state AFTER a save** (that is what Phase 27 writes; revision 1 = the first save), so the newest row equals the bill as it stands (`isCurrent`). `UNDO_REDO_RULES` §5 says "stores the previous persisted state"; the two are the same set of snapshots seen from either side, so no change to Phase 27 was made. After delete + restore the bill's number moves up without a row, so then **no row is marked Current** (compare mode "since" still works against the live bill). (3) **Two comparisons**, switched with **C**: *What this save changed* (revision vs the next lower stored one; the oldest stored revision says there is nothing earlier) and *Changes since* (revision vs the bill as stored now). Inline diff only; no side-by-side layout. (4) **Rows are matched by id**; an added or removed row is shown as such, a row whose content changed lists each changed field (item name, name formatting, qty, rate, typed total, GST %, each extra-column cell and mark, highlight); `moved` = order changed among rows both versions share. Blank padding rows are ignored. A row whose id was regenerated would show as removed + added (rows keep their ids across saves today). (5) **Restore** = `revisions:restoreToDraft` returns the revision's content under the bill's CURRENT PO number / branch / revision plus the stored bill as baseline; the renderer calls `billStore.loadDraft(draft, baseline)`, so the bill opens in Create **dirty** (Save / Discard prompt, autosave draft, Ctrl+Z history starts empty), PO number unchanged, and saving it is an ordinary edit (revision + 1, a NEW revision row; old rows untouched). Restoring the current version is refused with a message. If the bill open on Create has unsaved edits, the normal Save / Discard / Cancel prompt comes first (the panel stays open under it). Restoring a revision while a DIFFERENT saved bill is open on Create goes through the same prompt, then replaces that bill in the editor. (6) **Party link is not restored**: the party snapshot fields come back, `partyId` stays the bill's current link (the server decides the link by name on save, Phase 27). (7) **Cap = newest 50 by count.** The Phase 27 prune cut at `latest - 50`, which keeps fewer than 50 once a delete / restore has bumped `revision` without writing a row; it is now count-based (`pruneRevisions(billId, keep)`, signature changed, one caller). Older rows past the cap are gone for good. (8) **List robustness**: the list fields are read with `json_extract` guarded by `json_valid`, so one damaged snapshot shows blank fields in the list instead of breaking it; opening / restoring that one gives `DB_ERROR`. (9) Revisions are listed only for live bills (a trashed bill is `NOT_FOUND` - restore it from Recently deleted first); a purged bill's revisions go with it (FK cascade, Phase 43). Create has no button for Revisions (the bottom bar was not touched); use Ctrl+Shift+V or the palette.

- **Phase 43:** (1) **Key change:** Ctrl+Shift+D is now **Recently deleted** (History scope, `UNDO_REDO_RULES` §4); the Phase 25 `create.focusDate` moved to **Ctrl+Shift+E** (it was not in the frozen KB §8 map). Owner may prefer another key for either: edit `shortcuts.ts` only. (2) **Undo delete** = a bar above the status line with an *Undo delete* button, plus **Ctrl+Z** on the History page, for 10 s (`UNDO_DELETE_MS`). A toast cannot hold a button (Phase 38 note), hence the bar. Ctrl+Z inside the search / date fields stays the field's own undo. A second delete replaces the offer (only the newest delete can be undone this way; older ones are still restorable from Recently deleted). The offer is a module-level timer: it survives leaving History but Ctrl+Z only acts while History is open. (3) **The view replaces the Dates + Bills panels** (no tab strip); search, filters and the normal bar are hidden while it is open; Esc, Ctrl+Shift+D or *Back to bills* return. Esc is consumed (not "Back") unless a confirm dialog is open. Leaving the History page closes the view. (4) **Keys inside the view:** Enter = Restore, Delete = Delete forever (confirm; Cancel focused first), Ctrl+Shift+Delete = Empty trash (dialog; the red button stays disabled until the typed word is `EMPTY`, any case; Enter in the box confirms only when it matches), Space / Shift+arrows / Ctrl+A multi-select. History's Ctrl+O (Open PDF) is ignored while the view is open. (5) **Restore bumps `revision` again** (delete +1, restore +1) so a sync tombstone is superseded; it does not write a `bill_revisions` row or an audit row (nothing in the plan asks for either). Deleting still writes no audit row (Phase 42 decision). (6) **Purge** = hard delete of trashed bills only (a live bill id is reported `missing`, never purged); rows and revisions cascade, the bill's drafts are removed explicitly (no FK). `po_counters` is untouched so PO numbers are never reused. Purge / empty / auto-purge each write ONE `audit_log` row (`bill` + `purge` / `empty_trash` / `auto_purge`, detail = ids, count, and for auto-purge the retention and cutoff). Nothing purged = no audit row. (7) **Auto-purge runs only at launch** (writable DB), like the draft sweep; retention = setting `trash.retentionDays` (default 30, clamped 1..3650, garbage → 30), read live; there is **no Settings screen for it yet** (Phase 48/49). The "Purges in" column uses the same retention. (8) **Sync:** restore calls the same outbox hook as save/delete (no-op until Phase 54); purge sends nothing (the tombstone already went out). Purging a bill that another branch has not yet pulled the tombstone for is a Phase 57 concern. (9) The trash list is unvirtualised (30-day retention keeps it small) and loads every trashed bill each time it opens. (10) The status line (Restored / Deleted forever / Emptied...) goes through `SaveFlow` toasts like the other History actions. (11) After the LAST live bill is deleted History shows its "No saved bills yet" empty state, which hides the bottom bar; Ctrl+Shift+D and Ctrl+Z still work there but there is no button to reach Recently deleted until a bill exists - the palette entry "Show / hide Recently deleted bills" also works.

- **Phase 41:** (1) **Enter on a bill does nothing but print a status line** ("…arrive in the next phase"); Open/Edit, Open PDF, Print, Copy image, Delete, the bottom action bar and search are Phase 42 (`openBillForEdit` must go through `requestLeave` there; `print:openPdf` takes the bill's own PDF path). (2) **The left panel lists dates only (no "All dates" row)**; `history:listBills` with no `date` returns every bill newest first and pages by cursor, ready for Phase 42's search. `query` is NOT accepted yet (the strict schema rejects it). (3) **Order inside a day = creation time, newest first** (legacy shows 737 above 732); a bill edited later keeps its place. Cross-branch ties break by id. (4) **The legacy screenshot's Current / Create Backup / Refresh Backups / branch-code buttons and Auto-refresh tick are not built** (backups: Phase 50; branch filter is a disabled select showing the local branch name until sync, Phase 59; Refresh is a button and the palette action "Refresh the bills list"; no auto-refresh). (5) **History does not refresh by itself**: the page loads on every visit and on Refresh; a bill saved while the page is open (not possible today) or arriving by sync (`bills:remoteChanged`, Phase 56) will need a subscriber. (6) **Selecting a date by arrow keys loads that day at once** (no debounce; stale answers are dropped by a request counter); a day with > 200 bills loads further pages when the highlighted row or the scroll window is within 20 rows of the loaded end. (7) Palette labels are "Focus the dates list" / "Focus the bills list" / "Refresh the bills list" — my first wording "History: …" outranked "Go to History" in palette search (caught by `routes.test.ts`). (8) The totals shown are the cached `totals_json.grandTotal`; a bill whose cache is unreadable shows `—`, a blank party shows `—`. (9) Dates are shown `DD-MM-YY` in the list and sorted by the stored ISO date; the Dates panel is a fixed 190 px, the bill list columns are 110 / 100 / flexible / 150 px (first guesses, owner may tune in `grid.css` `.q3-hist*`). (10) `useHistoryStore()` is read whole in `History.tsx`, so the page re-renders on any store change (fine for one page; row components are not memoised — the virtual window keeps it small).
- **Phase 42:** (1) **Open PDF = save-as-PDF then open it**: bills do not store a PDF path, so Ctrl+O / the button opens the normal print dialog in Professional mode with the Save PDF step auto-run (system Save dialog), then opens the file in the OS viewer. If the extra-columns warning needs acknowledging the dialog waits for Y first. The Save-PDF palette entries on History save without opening. (2) **Print / Copy image / Open PDF / Edit need exactly one bill**: with several selected they report "N bills selected - select one bill to ..."; Delete and the TSV copy take the whole selection. (3) **Bulk export = the list as TSV on the clipboard** (Ctrl+Shift+T with 2+ bills selected: Bill No, Date, Party, Total); with one bill it copies the whole bill exactly like Create. No file export yet (nothing in the plan asks for a format). (4) **Soft delete only**: `bills:delete` sets `deleted_at`, bumps `revision`/`updated_at`, keeps rows and PO numbers; it does NOT write a `bill_revisions` row or an audit row and there is **no Undo toast, no trash view, no restore** (all Phase 43). The confirm dialog text already says "moved to Recently deleted". Deleting the bill that is open on Create clears the Create session and its draft. (5) **Search results span all dates** (the Dates panel loses its highlight; arrowing in Dates or clicking a date clears the search). The date range filter and the branch rule still apply. Cap 500 hits with a "refine the search" note. (6) **Search semantics**: every space-separated word must match something; party typos are fuzzy per word (>= 4 letters, one slip in 4-5 letters, two in 6); amounts match exactly or by whole-rupee part, never by prefix (so `12` finds PO sequence 12 but not 1200). A 2-letter transposition like `smrat` for `smart` is NOT found (Fuse counts it as two edits); `smert` is. (7) **Filters persist while you stay in the app** (the store is module-level); the Clear button / palette "Clear search and date filter" resets. (8) **Keys**: Enter / Delete / Ctrl+A / Space / Shift+arrows work while the bill list has focus; Ctrl+F and Ctrl+O work from anywhere on the page (both call `preventDefault`). Enter in Dates still just moves to the bills. Print/image keys are the global ones. Registry scope for the History keys is `history` (the global hotkey layer in `AppShell` has no active scopes, so the History page listens itself). (9) **The old Phase 41 behaviour "Enter on a bill = status line only" is gone**; its test was rewritten. (10) The search box, From/To and Clear sit on one row between the header and the panels; the action bar is below the status line. Layout values (28 px controls) are first guesses in `grid.css` `.q3-hist__tools` / `.q3-hist__actions`.

- **Phase 40:** (1) **The owner's "80 rough / 70 pro rows on ONE A4 page" is now honoured by shrinking type** (tiers 9 → 8 → 7 → 6.2 → 5.5 pt, cell padding 0.3 → 0.1 mm), chosen by measurement; the alternative (accept 2 pages) was Phase 35's decision and is reversed here because the engine's own `pages` said 1 while the real PDF said 2. Owner may flip: lower `A4_MAX_ROWS` or drop tiers (`printConstants.ts`). (2) The fit runs for every one-page A4 decision (incl. forced A4) and costs 1-5 hidden-window measurements; multi-page A4 and A5 are never squeezed. (3) `print:decide` now returns `density`; the dialog preview renders with it. (4) If even tier 4 overflows, the bill flows onto a second page and `decision.pages` still says 1 (not seen in the matrix; logged, not handled). (5) The matrix uses plain amounts `₹17226.00` (no thousands separators), as Phase 33 decided.
- **Phase 39:** (1) **No acknowledgement for images**: an extra-columns/A4 warning guards paper, not a screen image; the image uses the same size decision as print (rules + measured overflow, saved `paperOverride` counts) and reports the paper in the result. (2) **Crop box = union of header + table** (not the sheet), padding 12 CSS px on all sides; the offscreen window is printable width + 2 × padding because a capture rect cannot leave the viewport (found on the first real-Chromium run: right padding was being clipped). (3) **Scale 3, else 2 when the bill is tall; > 16,000 output px refuses with `IMAGE_FAILED` and a clear message** (no silent downscale); ~16k px at scale 2 = about 8,000 CSS px ≈ 250+ rows. (4) Test tolerance: ink margins ≤ padding + 1 CSS px (half-pixel table borders). (5) No toast spinner; the toast shows the real pixel size (e.g. `Image copied - 1,542 × 1,068 px - paste into WhatsApp`). (6) Image copy needs an open bill with content ('Nothing to copy yet' otherwise). (7) Samples for owner review: `docs/reference/qa/image-*.png`.
- **Phase 38:** (1) **"Open PDF" is a palette action (`output.openPdf`, "Open last saved PDF") with no global key**, remembering only the last PDF saved *this session*; History's per-bill "Open PDF" (Phase 42) should call `print:openPdf` with its own path (the channel and the service method already exist and are tested). A success toast cannot hold a button, so no "Open" link appears in it. (2) **No global shortcut for Save PDF** — the frozen KB §8 map has no PDF key, so it is the print dialog's **S** key and the palette actions `output.pdfRough` / `output.pdfPro` (they open the dialog and save as soon as the decision is in; if the decision needs the extra-columns acknowledgement they wait for Y then S). Owner may want a global key: add it in `shortcuts.ts` only. (3) The PDF **uses the same decision as printing** (rules → measure → re-decide), asks for the acknowledgement the same way, and a forced 4/5 size applies; the bill's saved `paperOverride` counts as forced as in Phase 37. (4) The Save dialog is opened BEFORE the PDF is rendered, so cancelling costs nothing; cancelling keeps the print dialog open and says nothing. (5) Default name = `<PO number, else party name, else Quotation>-<YYYY-MM-DD>.pdf`; illegal Windows characters → `-`, stem capped at 60 characters, reserved device names prefixed `_`. A never-saved bill has no PO, so its name starts with the party. (6) A chosen path without `.pdf` gets it appended; `savePath` over IPC must be absolute (drive, UNC or POSIX) and end in `.pdf`, and `print:openPdf` only opens existing `.pdf` files (so the renderer cannot make main open or write arbitrary files). (7) The page count in the result is read from the PDF bytes (`countPdfPages`), checked against `pdfinfo` for three fixtures — it assumes plain page dictionaries as Chromium writes them. (8) The remembered folder is per session (first save starts in Documents). (9) The literal-threshold guard (Phase 36) flagged a `80` I first used as the file-name cap; it is 60 now — keep numbers 40/30/80/70 out of print files. (10) The Phase 33 note stands: the print template writes plain amounts (`₹17226.00`, no thousands separators).
- **Phase 32:** (1) The integration suite is **store/page-level in jsdom** (real `Create` page + hotkeys + guard + stores → fake bridge → REAL `billHandlers`/`partyHandlers`/`billService` on a temp-file SQLite). It is not Playwright-Electron (no display was used), so real caret, focus, layout, clipboard and window-close behaviour remain the standing gaps listed per phase. (2) In that flow items/adjustment/extra-column text/Mark are entered through store actions (`setCellText`, `toggleMark`) — the grid's own key pipeline is covered by the Phase 11–22 suites; the QA drives party autocomplete (typed + ArrowDown/Enter), Alt+1/Alt+2, Ctrl+Alt+=, Ctrl+S, Esc, Ctrl+N and the guard dialog (S / D) through real events. (3) **`tsconfig.integration.json` is new** (DOM + node types) and is part of `npm run typecheck`; the test is excluded from `tsconfig.node.json`. Any future test that mixes renderer and main code must be added to `tsconfig.integration.json` `include` (and excluded from node config) the same way. (4) The historical-bill fixture lives in `shared/__fixtures__` and is imported by `main/services/seedHistory.ts` (so it ships in the main bundle; ~3 KB). Dates are fixed (anchor 2026-10-08, 3 days apart), so "last 7 days" style Dashboard stats against these bills will drift as real time passes. (5) `QUOT3_SEED_HISTORY=1 npm run dev` seeds once into a database that has **no bills at all** (also skipped if trashed bills exist); it also seeds the 30 parties via the existing dev party seed. Delete `%LOCALAPPDATA%\\Quot3\\quot3.db` to re-seed. (6) React prints "update not wrapped in act(...)" warnings in this suite (async bridge promises resolving after `flush`); harmless, assertions are on settled state. (7) Defect hunt result: no production defect was found by this suite; the only failures were test-authoring mistakes (party index `loaded` flag, wrong total arithmetic, `computeBill` field names).

- **Phase 31:** (1) **Address and date are not copied** (spec: party name, contact, transport lines; no company block) — add them in `billToRows` if wanted. Phone/Transport lines are written as `Phone<TAB>value` / `Transport<TAB>value`, only when filled. (2) Numbers are plain strings (`1250000.50`, no ₹, no commas) so Excel treats them as numbers. (3) Footer rows follow the grid exactly (single `Total`, or Items total / adjustments / Adjustments total / Round off / Final total; GST format: adjustments in the Amount column only). (4) A partial row is copied as shown; blank padding rows are skipped. (5) An empty bill copies nothing and shows "Nothing to copy yet". (6) The HTML flavour is a bare `<table>` of `<td>` cells (no styling). (7) The typed-but-uncommitted cell is flushed before copying (same as Save), which may add an undo entry for that typed text only. (8) Golden outputs live inline in `billTsv.test.ts`. (9) Copy is independent of a running Save.
- **Phase 30:** (1) **Shortcut change:** Ctrl+Shift+P was "Focus party name" (Phase 25) and is now **Print — Professional** (KB §8, frozen); focus-party moved to **Ctrl+Shift+F** (tests updated). Owner may prefer another key for focus-party — rebind in `shortcuts.ts` only. (2) The five output actions (Print Rough/Professional, Copy image Rough/Professional, TSV) are **stubs**: button, key and palette entry exist and raise a warn toast `X is not built yet (arrives in Phase N)`; phases 31 (TSV), 37 (print) and 39 (image) must set `phase: null` in `shared/outputActions.ts` and replace the stub branch of `stores/outputActions.ts` `runOutput`. (3) The stub buttons are `aria-disabled` (not `disabled`) deliberately so the tooltip with the reason still shows and a click reports it. (4) `Toolbar.tsx` from the phase's key-files list was **not created**: the compact toolbar above the grid already exists as `FormatToolbar` + `ExtraColsToolbar` (Phases 15/21/22) inside `.q3-create__tools`; a third wrapper would duplicate it. (5) Save/output buttons render only where `BottomBar` gets `actions` (the Create page); `/dev/grid` keeps the Phase 18 bar. (6) Buttons stay out of the Tab order (Phase 18 decision) — keyboard path = the shortcuts + palette (Phase 67 reviews the final order). (7) The F1 sheet lists every entry of `allShortcuts()` grouped by action group, so the new five appear automatically; no per-row test of the sheet DOM. (8) "Next/prev custom format Alt+3.." in KB §8 needs no code: formats declare their own `shortcut` and the registry generates it (Phase 20). (9) Save button while a save is in flight is protected by `runSave`'s own busy flag (not disabled visually).
- **Phase 29:** (1) **Text still being typed in the cell you are in is NOT in a draft**: cells commit to the store on leaving the cell (Phase 11), the autosave deliberately does not flush the live cell (that would commit mid-typing), so a crash loses at most the cell in progress plus up to 2 s of earlier changes. Making drafts per-keystroke needs Phase 11's commit model changed — not done. (2) The recovered bill is **dirty against a baseline**: an empty bill (never saved) or the stored bill (`bills:getForEdit`; an edit whose bill has since vanished falls back to the empty baseline). **Revert to last saved** therefore goes back to that baseline, not to the draft. (3) Recovering an edit does not check the revision: if the saved bill changed elsewhere meanwhile, Save reports `CONFLICT_REVISION` (nothing overwritten). (4) Recovery opens the draft but leaves its row in the database until Save / Discard / undo-to-clean (so a second crash is still recoverable); it is hidden from the banner while open. (5) Drafts of other sessions are never merged: two recoverable drafts are two banner rows. (6) A draft that cannot be parsed is never offered and never deleted until the 14-day sweep. (7) Cleanup runs **only at launch**; a draft untouched for 14 days inside one long-running session is not swept until the next start. (8) Palette action `draft.recover` has no key (Dashboard banner buttons are in the Tab order; Enter recovers). (9) `drafts:save` failures are silent (retried on the next change); there is no "draft could not be saved" indicator. (10) The History page (Phases 41–42) will call `openBillForEdit`; a draft for that bill and a manual open do not know about each other. (11) The `/dev/*` pages are not autosaved against (they have no open Create bill, or are the same bill store) — `/dev/grid` loads a fixture bill into the shared store and WOULD be autosaved as a draft once it is dirty; owner may see a stray draft after playing in `/dev/grid`.
- **Phase 28:** (1) **Save from the dialog does not "continue" after a missing party name**: the dialog closes, focus jumps to Party name and the pending navigation is dropped — press Esc/Ctrl+S again (KB §6 says "then continues"; kept simple). (2) **No UI opens a saved bill yet**: `openBillForEdit(id)` (`stores/createSession.ts`) works but History (Phases 41–42) must call it — wrap it in `requestLeave` first when a bill is open. (3) The guard covers leaving the **Create** page by `navigate`/`back`, New bill (Ctrl+N) and window close; `/dev/*` pages are not guarded. (4) Discard clears the open bill (`billId = null`), so returning to Create opens a new empty bill; the Phase 29 draft autosave must not resurrect a bill the owner discarded (delete its draft on Discard — hook point: `chooseDiscard` in `stores/guardStore.ts`). (5) Esc on Create when the page is the first entry in the router history does nothing (Dashboard is always first, so not reachable today). (6) `app:quit` was dropped from the contract; only `app:confirmQuit` exists. (7) Main never times out waiting for the renderer; a hung (not crashed) renderer would keep the window open — owner can kill the process. (8) Ctrl+N from another page opens Create with the CURRENT clean bill's replacement (always a fresh bill), not the previous one.
- **Phase 27:** (1) **New bill after save:** Ctrl+N / "New bill" still just navigates to Create, which keeps the saved bill (module-level `createBillId`); Ctrl+S on it again is an EDIT (revision + 1). Starting a fresh bill, the unsaved guard and `bills:getForEdit` are Phase 28. (2) **Save is only wired to Ctrl+S + palette**; no Save button yet (Phase 30 builds the bottom bar and should call `runSave()` from `stores/saveActions.ts`). (3) The client sends `expectedRevision` only for a bill that already has a PO number; a caller that omits it overwrites (last write wins locally). `CONFLICT_REVISION` is therefore only reachable once two windows/branches edit the same saved bill (Phase 28 edit-existing, Phase 57 sync). (4) **Server decides the party link by normalised name** (`isNewParty` / `findPartyByName`); `bill.partyId` from the client is ignored. A known party is linked but never modified — editing the stored party from a bill is not supported (Phase 45). New parties are written with `source = 'auto'`. (5) The renderer tidies party fields (trim) through `setPartyField` BEFORE saving, so a messy name produces an undo entry `Edited Party name`; a tidy one produces none. (6) Revisions: one `bill_revisions` row per save, newest 50 kept; the revision UI is Phase 44. (7) `sync_state` is always `local_only`; the outbox hook (`createBillService` `opts.outbox`) is wired to nothing until Phase 54. (8) `app:getDbInfo` PO preview and `bills:newDefaults.nextPoPreview` are previews only; the real number is allocated inside the save transaction. (9) A bill with only a name or only a total (a `partial` row) cannot be saved — it needs qty or rate (item) or name + total (adjustment). (10) Errors from the database layer other than `AppError` become a generic message in the toast (envelope behaviour). (11) The Create page applies `bills:newDefaults` after mount only if nothing changed and a value differs; the grid padding rows are re-created in that case (cosmetic).
- **Phase 26:** (1) **Nothing saves a party yet.** `parties:upsert` exists and is tested, but only `bills:save` (Phase 27) should call it, and only when `isNewParty(name, parties)` is true (shared, tested). Phase 27 must also `usePartyIndexStore.getState().load()` after a save so the new party is suggested next time, and should set `bills.party_id` by looking up `findPartyByName` / the upsert's returned id (the store has no `partyId` on purpose — a picked party is identified by its normalised name at save time; if the owner edits the name after picking, it is simply treated as a new party). (2) **Pick replaces all four fields** with the party's values (blank stored values blank the bar) — so a phone typed before picking is overwritten. (3) **Enter with nothing highlighted** does not pick the top suggestion (it keeps the typed name and moves on) so a new party that is a prefix of an existing one can still be entered by keyboard; Down+Enter picks. After a pick focus goes to Phone, not the grid. (4) Fuse runs over the NAME only; phone numbers are matched by digit substring when the query is digits/space/+/- with ≥ 3 digits (fewer digits → no suggestions). The contract's "keys name+phone" is therefore delivered by two paths, not one Fuse key (a fuzzy digit match produced false hits for "98"). (5) The index is fetched once when the bar first mounts and not refreshed (no save/delete exists yet); Phase 27/45/47 must call `load()` after changes. (6) Soft-deleted parties are never listed; `parties:delete` is Phase 45. (7) Dev seed condition counts soft-deleted rows, so an owner who empties the list in dev is not re-seeded. (8) `PartySnapshot` has no `partyId`; `billStore.applyParty` therefore records a labelled entry `Selected party <name>` but links nothing.
- **Phase 25:** (1) **Grid → party bar by keyboard** is only via Ctrl+Shift+P (focus name) / Ctrl+Shift+D (date) / the palette; Shift+Tab or Up from the first grid cell does NOT go back into the bar (the grid reducer owns those keys). (2) **Date is outside the Tab flow** (Name → Phone → Transport → grid per KB §7); reach it with Ctrl+Shift+D or the mouse. (3) The PO number is a preview only; `poNumber` in the store is '' for a new bill until Phase 27 allocates it in the save transaction. (4) The Create page opens a fresh bill unless its own bill is still in the store (module-level `createBillId`): leaving and returning keeps the work; **Ctrl+N / "New bill" currently just navigates to Create (no reset)** — Phase 27/28 own New-bill + the unsaved guard. `branchId: 'local'` is a placeholder until Phase 27/50. (5) Party name is free text — no autocomplete, no `partyId` linking (Phase 26); `finalizePartyField` (trim) exists but nothing calls it until Save (Phase 27); until then typed trailing spaces stay in the store. (6) Typing coalesces per field with the 1000 ms rule (Phase 17), so a long pause mid-name makes two undo entries. (7) Esc with the address closed is deliberately NOT consumed by the bar (it reaches `nav.back`; the unsaved-changes guard is Phase 28). (8) `/dev/grid` is unchanged and still has no party bar. (9) The old `StubPage` import for Create was removed from `App.tsx`; `StubPage` is still used by History/Customers/Settings.

- **Phase 24:** (1) **Format switch re-renders every row/cell** (20 switches = 2080 row + 15600 cell renders in the harness) because the column set changes; logic path 85 ms p50 in jsdom, certainly over budget there and **unknown in Chromium**. Not fixed (out of the "hot keystroke" paths; a fix means keying cells per column set or a shared column-template, a design change). If the owner sees the Alt+1/Alt+2 flip lag on an 80-row bill, this is the Phase 66/68 target. (2) First `paste200` sample is cold (~158 ms jsdom); later ones ~4 ms. (3) The harness's `typeName` simulates the browser's native insert with `insertTextAtSelection` after `beforeinput`; real native typing is unmeasured. (4) `holdDown` / `tabRow` still cost 6–10 ms p50 in jsdom; the remainder is jsdom DOM/`act()` overhead plus `focus()` + `closest()` per move — no further algorithmic hot spot was found by the CPU profile.

- **Phase 20:** (1) **GST % exists only in the GST format**, so it cannot be edited (typed or pasted) while Non-GST is shown; its data is kept and returns on switching back (proven). (2) **Undo of a format switch** restores the format and re-activates the stored cell; if that cell's column does not exist in the restored format the first column is used (the nearest-column rule `remapColumnKey` is applied on a *switch*, not inside `restoreSel`). (3) The switch always lands the active cell in **Append mode, caret at end, range collapsed** (a Free-mode caret/range is not carried over). (4) Alt+1/Alt+2 are `global` scope, handled by the window hotkey layer (the grid leaves Alt+digit unhandled and unprevented); **focus is restored to the grid only if it was in the grid or in the command palette** — when the Create page has a party bar (Phase 25) a switch from there will not pull focus into the grid. (5) `ACTIONS`/`SHORTCUTS` stay static; per-format entries come from `allActions()`/`allShortcuts()` (read live). Anything that wants every key binding must use those, not the static arrays. (6) Palette hint for a format with no `shortcut` is simply absent. (7) Adjustments' GST (none, D-C2) unchanged; the GST footer is the registry's `total_gst_amount` single "Total" row (Total, GST Amt, Amount) — verified on the legacy fixture (17,226.00 + 961.20 = 18,187.20 with three rows at 18 %). (8) `FormatSwitcher` is mounted on `/dev/grid` only; Phase 25/30 place it on the Create page (the dev format `<select>` was removed from `GridDev`).
- **Phase 19:** (1) **Read-only total rows are skipped, not stopped on** by the arrows (reading "arrows pass through" that way); navigation goes last body row ⇄ adjustment rows. Owner may want Items total / Final total to be visitable. (2) **Enter / Down / Tab on the last adjustment does not add rows** (the body's trailing blanks are above it); to add more rows go up. (3) The row jumps on **commit-on-leave** (Phase 11 decision), not per keystroke: type name + total, move away, and it drops below the Items total; the cell you moved to is chosen by id so you land where you meant to. Delete (Append) commits immediately and the cell follows the row. Other in-place commits that keep the cell active (format keys, undo/jump) already re-activate by id (Phase 17). (4) Paste never starts in an adjustment row and never flows into the adjustment block (notice shown); range clear/copy across adjustment rows works through `navIds`. (5) Extra-column cells and qty/rate/GST % stay editable on an adjustment row: filling qty/rate turns it back into an item (moves to the body). (6) Adjustments' own GST (none, D-C2) and paper-row counting (D-C3) are Phase 20/36's. (7) Negative styling uses `--rt-red` (no new token); `--grid-adjustment-bg` / `--grid-total-bg` were already in `tokens.css`.
- **Phase 18:** (1) `BottomBar` is mounted only on `/dev/grid` (grid + bar); Phase 30 builds the real bottom action bar on the Create page and must render/replace this one (it already has a `children` slot) — the global shortcuts Ctrl+Shift+H / Ctrl+Alt+Z open the panel/prompt only where a `BottomBar` is mounted. (2) Ctrl+Z / Ctrl+Y still work only with grid focus (party bar: Phase 25 must call `useBillStore.getState().undo()/redo()` — or the new `runUndo/runRedo` in `stores/undoActions.ts`, which also set the status message). (3) **Revert to last saved** compares against the snapshot set by `loadBill`/`markSaved` (Phase 27 must call `markSaved()` after each save, otherwise the "saved" state stays the opened state). (4) The bar's buttons are out of the Tab order (`tabIndex=-1`, mousedown prevented, same as the format toolbar): keyboard path = shortcuts + palette; Phase 30/67 decide the final tab order. (5) Panel jump keeps keyboard focus in the panel; **clicking** a row also leaves the panel open (it is a flyout, not a modal). (6) Revert restores the previously active cell (Append, caret at end) and drops any draft — found by a test where undo-after-revert committed stale cell text; fixed by passing the selection to `restore`. (7) The panel is `position: fixed` bottom-right above the bar; it is not repositioned for narrow windows. (8) Revert/panel/undo coverage of party fields, extra-column ops, paper override, row insert/delete is still owed by the phases that add them (use `recordChange`).
- **Phase 17:** (1) **Undoable state today = `formatId`, `extraCols`, `rows`** (`BillDoc`). Party-bar fields, paper override, extra-column add/remove/rename and row insert/delete do not exist yet; the phases that add them must put their state into `BillDoc` (`shared/history/billDoc.ts` `diffDocs`/`docsEqualPersisted`) and route the mutation through `billHistory.record/wrap` (labels per UR §2.6). (2) Undo is **row-level patches over a diff**, not Immer recipes inside each store action: each recorded action snapshots the doc before/after and `diffDocs` emits `rows[i]` replacements (plus length). Equivalent behaviour, cheap (identity comparison), and the old action bodies stayed untouched. (3) **Coalescing in practice:** typing is committed on cell-leave (Phase 11), so one visit to a cell = one entry already; the 1000 ms coalescing merges repeated commits of the same cell (e.g. leave/return quickly, `setCellText` loops) and typing/deleting are separate keys (decided by whether the displayed text got shorter). A cell that is left and re-entered after > 1 s is a new entry. (4) `ensureBodyRows`/trailing padding are NOT history entries; undo re-pads blank rows, so row counts after undo can differ from before by blank padding only (dirty ignores blank tail rows). (5) Undo/redo keys are handled by the **grid controller** only (grid focus); "from any field on the Create page" (party bar) arrives with Phase 25 — it should call `useBillStore.getState().undo()/redo()`. A pending Esc/modal guard is Phase 28. (6) `markSaved()` exists but nothing calls it until Phase 27 (the bill is "clean" at open; there is no Save yet). (7) Formatting a *range* is still active-cell-only (Phase 15/16 note). (8) The status line shows `Undone: <label>` / `Redone: …` / `Nothing to undo` through `gridUiStore.notice` (cleared on next key); the bottom-bar Undo/Redo buttons, history panel, Revert-to-saved (Ctrl+Alt+Z) are Phase 18 — the store already exposes `undoLabel/redoLabel/canUndo/canRedo/historyList/historyCursor/jumpTo/dirty` for them. (9) Existing grid tests that replace `historyHooks.wrap` reset it to a pass-through in `afterEach`; the real wrapper is installed when `billStore` is first imported.

- **Phase 16:** (1) **Single value + no range** is left to the browser (native text paste at the caret, numeric gate in `beforeinput`), EXCEPT a cell copied inside Quot3 pasted in Append mode, which is a cell-level paste (replaces the cell, keeps formatting). Multi-cell blocks and anything onto a range always overwrite. (2) Multi-line text is split into rows only when "recognisably TSV": contains a tab, or ends with a row separator and has ≥ 2 rows (Excel's single-column copy); otherwise it stays ONE cell with newlines → spaces. (3) Copy covers the keyboard-navigable columns only (Sl.No, GST Amt, Amount are not in a range, as in Phase 11); a copied Total cell is its displayed value, so pasting it sets an override only if it differs from the target's auto total. (4) Pasting into adjustments or onto an override-only name-less row follows Phase 13's rule (the row may leave the body for the footer). (5) Shift+click range extension and drag auto-scroll are not implemented. (6) Format keys (Phase 15) still act on the active cell only, not on a range. (7) `useGridClipboard.ts` from the phase's key-files list was not created: copy/cut/paste are events on the existing single pipeline (`gridController.ts`), same reasoning as Phases 13/15. (8) **Phase 17 hook point:** `stores/historyHooks.ts` `runAsOneEntry(label, fn)` wraps range clear (`'Clear cells'`), cut (`'Cut'`) and paste (`'Paste'`); Phase 17 assigns `historyHooks.wrap` to open/close one history entry. Single-cell commits are NOT wrapped yet.

- **Phase 15:** (1) **Pending style** is not implemented: Ctrl+B etc. with a collapsed caret formats the WHOLE cell (KB §4), and on an EMPTY name cell does nothing (runs cannot hold an empty styled run; Ctrl+H on an empty cell is a no-op). (2) Formatting acts on the active cell only — a multi-cell range is not formatted (Phase 16 may extend). (3) Only red, bold, red+bold, highlight exist (the Phase 14 run model has no arbitrary colours); "colour options" = plain Red + Red+Bold in the palette. (4) The toolbar is mounted only on `/dev/grid`; the Create page (Phase 25) must place `<FormatToolbar/>`. (5) Print export of runs is Phase 33+ (the data is already `nameRuns`, persisted as `name_runs_json`). (6) Format keys are handled in the grid controller (scope `grid` entries exist for the cheat-sheet/conflict test); the window hotkey layer does not run them.

- **Phase 13:** typing is still committed on leave, so the footer/auto total update when you Tab away, not per keystroke (decision from Phase 11 kept). `computeBill` still iterates all rows each commit (cached calc, but display-string rebuild is O(rows)); Phase 24 decides if that needs more. Adjustment entry (name + Total only) works mechanically but the row jumps to the footer — Phase 19.

- **Phase 12:** real-Chromium paste/drop into numeric cells is jsdom-simulated only (`beforeinput` with `data`); `dataTransfer` path for drops untested. Total column is still read-only (Phase 13).

- `npm audit` reports 7 vulnerabilities (2 moderate, 5 high) in the pinned tree. Not addressed (phase scope forbids audit sweeps; pins are locked). Revisit in Phase 61/69.
- `@types/node` is pinned at 22 while Electron 44's main process runs a newer Node; avoid Node-24-only APIs in shared code (already noted in DEPENDENCY_VERSIONS).

- **Phase 11 → later phases:** (1) Typing is **committed on leaving the cell** (move / focus-out / Delete), not per keystroke, so Sl.No/Amount/footer update when you Tab away; Phase 12/13 decide whether live recalculation per keystroke is needed (the controller's `commit()` is the single hook). (2) **Adjustments** (name + Total only) can't be created yet because Total is read-only; when Phase 13/19 enable the override, an adjustment row leaves the body for the footer on commit — the controller already re-locates the active cell after a commit but Phase 19 must define how an adjustment entry row is shown/edited. (3) Name edits flatten styling for mid-text edits (`setPlainName`); Phases 14/15 replace it. (4) Range selection is visual-only (`data-in-range`) + Delete/Backspace clear; copy/paste/mouse-drag ranges are Phase 16. (5) Native Ctrl+Z/Y are suppressed in cells, but nothing undoes yet (Phase 17). (6) No `scroll-margin-top` for the sticky header yet (Phase 23). (7) Any key not handled by the reducer is `NATIVE` and **not** preventDefault-ed, so global shortcuts (Ctrl+K, Ctrl+0..4, F1) keep working from inside the grid; Tab/Enter/arrows are consumed by the grid.

- **Phase 17 (unseen in a real browser):** nothing ran in Chromium/Electron. jsdom only. Unconfirmed: (a) Ctrl+Z / Ctrl+Y / Ctrl+Shift+Z really reach the page's `keydown` (no app menu exists, so Electron should not intercept them) and `preventDefault` stops the native undo that `contenteditable="plaintext-only"` would otherwise run; (b) after undo, `restoreSel` → `activateCell` → focus + caret at end in the real caret model, and `scrollIntoView` of a restored off-screen cell; (c) the repaint of the cell that was in the middle of editing when undo ran (`leave(false)` then `paint` from the store); (d) real-time feel of the 1000 ms coalescing (tests use an injected clock). If (a)–(c) misbehave the single place to fix is `components/grid/gridController.ts` (`onKeyDown` undo block, `restoreSel`).

- **Phase 18 (unseen in a real browser):** nothing ran in Chromium/Electron. Unconfirmed: the look/placement of `.q3-bbar` (≤ 44 px) and `.q3-upanel` (fixed bottom-right, z-index popover, 320 px, max 60vh) in both themes; the tooltips on disabled Undo/Redo buttons (tooltip wrapper is a span, native `disabled` buttons may swallow hover); real focus hand-off panel → grid on Esc/close and panel → panel after a jump (jsdom proves it); Enter in the panel is handled by our `keydown` (preventDefault) rather than the button's native click; the `ConfirmDialog` focus trap with Revert focused first; Ctrl+Shift+H / Ctrl+Alt+Z not being grabbed by Chromium/Windows (Ctrl+Alt on some keyboard layouts is AltGr — **owner: check Ctrl+Alt+Z on your keyboard layout**; if AltGr interferes, rebind in `shortcuts.ts` only).

- **Phase 19 (unseen in a real browser):** nothing ran in Chromium/Electron. Unconfirmed: the look of the adjustment block (tint, italic names, red negatives, footer total rows) in both themes; caret/focus when a row's DOM is moved from the body to the footer under the active cell (jsdom + synchronous `flushSync` only); scroll-into-view of an adjustment row below the fold; that the grid still shows ≥ 20 rows at 1366×768 with a 4-row footer.

- **Phase 23 (unseen in a real browser):** jsdom has no layout, so NOTHING visual is proven: sticky Sl.No/Item Name (background, z-order over the header and active cell, offset after a resize), the active-cell overlay (position, max 480 px, caret/scroll inside it, clipping by the row's `contain: paint`), ellipsis, RTL/bidi rendering, the resize handle hit area and cursor, no horizontal page scroll at 1366×768, and whether overlay + `contenteditable` caret code from Phase 11 behave. Also unconfirmed: width persistence through the real settings IPC, and that Ctrl+Alt+] / [ / 0 reach the page on the owner's layout. **Owner: check long-name rows and a 40-char header at 1366×768 first; if the overlay misbehaves, remove the last two rules in `grid.css` (the app still works, the cell just scrolls in place).**
- **Phase 22 (unseen in a real browser):** nothing ran in Chromium/Electron. Unconfirmed: popup look/focus/Esc in real Chromium; that Ctrl+M / Ctrl+U / Ctrl+Shift+M reach the page from a `contenteditable` cell and are not swallowed by Electron/OS; the marked-cell underline/tint in both themes; clipboard write from the popup (`navigator.clipboard`, no bridge call yet).
- **Phase 21 (unseen in a real browser):** nothing ran in Chromium/Electron. Unconfirmed: (a) that **Ctrl+Alt+= / Ctrl+Alt+- / Ctrl+Alt+R reach the page** on the owner's keyboard layout (Ctrl+Alt may be AltGr on some layouts); (b) real focus hand-off between the grid and the header `<input>` (double-click, Enter/Esc/blur, return of focus to the grid) and that the input's ellipsis/"full text on focus" looks right in both themes; (c) the confirmation dialog's focus/Esc behaviour in real Chromium; (d) print/PDF header wrapping for long headers is Phase 33+; (e) no frame-time measurement of add/remove (the column set changes, so every row re-renders; Phase 24 measures).
- **Phase 20 (unseen in a real browser):** nothing ran in Chromium/Electron. Unconfirmed: (a) that **Alt+1 / Alt+2 reach the page's `keydown`** from inside a `contenteditable="plaintext-only"` cell on Windows (no app menu exists, so Electron should not grab them; on layouts where Alt+digit is AltGr/another combo it may differ — **owner: check Alt+1/Alt+2 on your keyboard**; rebind only in the format's `shortcut` field); (b) caret/focus after the columns re-render (`flushSync` + `restoreSel` are jsdom-proven only; the cell's DOM node persists for surviving columns); (c) the look of `.q3-fmtsw` (segmented buttons, active underline) in both themes; (d) that the flip is < 1 frame on an 80-row bill — **no measurement taken** (the switch re-renders every row's cells because the column set changes; Phase 24 measures); (e) palette → switch → focus return ordering in real Chromium (the palette closes and returns focus to the opener, then the action runs on a microtask; jsdom-proven).

- **Phase 32 (not seen):** no real Electron/Chromium run. Unconfirmed in a real window: the whole create → save → reopen → edit → Esc → discard journey by hand, focus landing on Party name after a refused save, `QUOT3_SEED_HISTORY=1` actually seeding in `npm run dev` (the seed function is proven on a temp DB; the `index.ts` hook and env var were never executed).

- **Phase 33 → 35/36:** the owner's "up to 80 rough rows on one A4" needs a denser type tier than the static 9 pt A4 style (≈3.1 mm per row). Phase 35 (multi-page A4 / wrapping) and Phase 36 (measured-overflow net) must choose: a row-count-driven density tier, or accept 2 pages. Not decided here. Also: long wrapped names make A5 rows taller than the 40-row budget — the measured-fit net (Phase 36) is what catches that.

## Phase log

### Phase 0 — Planning and documentation (2026-10-07)
Produced the entire doc set from the owner's requirements (verbatim in `docs/reference/REQUIREMENTS_ORIGINAL.md`) and the example project's session-contract pattern: `CLAUDE_MUST_READ.md`, `MASTERPLAN.md`, `PHASES.md` (69 phases, 7 milestones, 3 hard gates), `PHASE_STATUS.md`, `API_CONTRACTS.md`, `PROJECT_STRUCTURE.md`, `DEPENDENCY_VERSIONS.md`, `docs/DATA_MODEL.md`, `docs/SYNC_POSTBOX_SPEC.md`, `docs/design/DESIGN_TOKENS.md`, `docs/security/SECURITY_MODEL.md`, and the behavioural rule files `KEYBOARD_CELL_RULES.md`, `UNDO_REDO_RULES.md`, `PAPER_ENGINE_RULES.md`, `EXTRA_COLS_UNITS_RULES.md`. Legacy-app screenshots copied to `docs/reference/legacy_app/`.
**Verification run:** package versions and peer-dependency constraints were read from the npm registry (this is what fixed Vite 7 / plugin-react 5 / TypeScript 5.9 / Tailwind 3); the legacy ruby/tomato example numbers (12 / 55 / 7) were checked by hand against the screenshot. **Not run:** no install, no Electron launch, no build.

### Phase 1 — Repo scaffold, toolchain, native module proof (2026-10-08)
**Done:** `package.json` (scripts `dev`, `build`, `typecheck`, `test`; `main: ./out/main/index.js`; engines), `.npmrc` (save-exact), `.editorconfig`, `electron.vite.config.ts` (aliases `@shared`, `@renderer`), `tsconfig.json` + `tsconfig.node.json` + `tsconfig.web.json` (strict, `noUncheckedIndexedAccess`; renderer project has no Node types), `tailwind.config.js`, `postcss.config.js`, `vitest.config.ts`, `scripts/postinstall.mjs`, `src/main/index.ts` (throwaway sqlite `:memory:` proof + placeholder window; `QUOT3_SMOKE_EXIT=1` auto-quits), `src/renderer/{index.html,main.tsx,styles/index.css}` ("Quot3 — loading"), `src/shared/constants.ts`, `src/shared/__tests__/smoke.test.ts`. Installed only the Phase 1–5 packages from `DEPENDENCY_VERSIONS.md` (exact pins, unchanged): electron, electron-vite, vite, plugin-react, typescript, react/react-dom, tailwind/postcss/autoprefixer, vitest, @types/*, @electron/rebuild, better-sqlite3, zod, decimal.js, zustand, immer, phosphor. (drizzle-orm/drizzle-kit/fuse/etc. left for the phases that use them.)
