**Verification actually run:** `npm run typecheck` ✅; `npm test` ✅ (1 test); better-sqlite3 `select sqlite_version()` = 3.53.4 under Node 22 ✅ and under Electron 44.6.0 / ABI 149 via xvfb ✅ (standalone script, not the app's main bundle). **Not run:** `electron-vite build`, `npm run dev`, real window (owner: skip builds).
**Blocker check:** the native-module blocker did not occur.

### Phase 2 — Secure shell, LocalAppData, typed IPC skeleton (2026-10-08)
**Done (contract first):** `API_CONTRACTS.md` extended (Phase 2 wiring note; `app:quit`/`app:confirmQuit` moved to Phase 28 — they belong with the before-quit guard). New: `src/shared/ipc.ts` (Result/ErrorCode/AppInfo/Quot3Bridge/EVENT_CHANNELS, zod-free), `src/main/paths.ts` (`%LOCALAPPDATA%\Quot3`, fallback `~/.quot3`, folder layout + creation), `src/main/logger.ts` (size-rotating, 1 MB x 5, never throws), `src/main/security.ts` (strict prod CSP, relaxed dev CSP, `isTrustedUrl`), `src/main/window.ts` (hardened BrowserWindow, CSP headers, permission deny, `window.open` deny, `will-navigate`/webview guards, devTools only in dev), `src/main/ipc/{envelope,register}.ts` (`wrapHandler` validates input+output, all throws → `Result`; sender-URL check), `src/preload/index.ts` (`window.quot3 = { app:{getInfo,openDataFolder}, on }`, event whitelist), `src/renderer/types/bridge.d.ts`, placeholder page now shows version/platform/data dir via the bridge. Tests: envelope, paths, logger rotation, security, **import boundaries** (`src/__tests__/importBoundaries.test.ts`).
**Edits to Phase 1 files (in place, justified):** `src/main/index.ts` replaced its inline window code with `window.ts` + userData override + single-instance lock (Phase 1 flagged this as throwaway; the sqlite proof is kept until Phase 5). `src/renderer/index.html`: removed the Phase-1 meta CSP because it breaks Vite dev (inline React-refresh preamble); a build-only Vite plugin in `electron.vite.config.ts` injects the strict meta CSP into production HTML as a `file://` fallback, and main sets the header CSP in both modes. `tsconfig.node.json` now includes `src/__tests__`.
**Decisions:** import-boundary test allows `better-sqlite3` in `main/index.ts` only until Phase 5; it does not check DOM globals inside `src/shared` (regex too noisy — revisit if a violation ever appears).
**Verification actually run:** `npm run typecheck` ✅; `npm test` ✅ (24 tests, 6 files); headless Electron 44.6.0 under xvfb, esbuild-bundled main+preload: in the renderer `typeof require`, `typeof process`, `typeof ipcRenderer` are all `undefined` ✅; bridge keys are exactly `app,on` ✅; `getInfo()` returned `{ok:true,data:{version,dataDir,platform,devMode}}` ✅; data folders + `logs/quot3.log` were created under the fake home ✅; `fetch('https://example.com')` blocked by CSP ✅; `window.open` returned null ✅; sqlite proof logged 3.53.4 ✅.

### Phase 3 — Design tokens, neumorphic primitives, theme loader (2026-10-08)
**Provenance (read this):** when this session started, the working tree already contained Phase 3 files that were NOT in the Phase 2 zip I delivered and that I did not write in this session: `src/shared/theme/{tokens,validateTheme,contrast}.ts` (+ `__tests__/validateTheme.test.ts`), `src/main/services/themeService.ts` (+ test), `src/main/ipc/themes.ts`, `src/renderer/styles/{tokens,neumorph,ui,index}.css`, and edits to `src/shared/ipc.ts`, `src/preload/index.ts`, `src/main/ipc/register.ts`, `src/main/index.ts`, `src/renderer/index.html`, plus a `themes` contract + change-log row in `API_CONTRACTS.md`. I did not know their origin (likely a prior partial Phase 3 run on the same sandbox). I **reviewed them, found them consistent with `DESIGN_TOKENS.md` and the layering rules, typecheck + tests passed, and built on them rather than rewriting** (per the no-rewrite rule). Owner: if you did not expect this, diff against your own copy.
**What I added:** `src/__tests__/tokensParity.test.ts` (tokens.css ⇄ whitelist parity both directions; both themes define identical token sets; WCAG contrast: grid text ≥ 7:1, body/secondary/muted ≥ 4.5:1, on-accent and Red+Bold legible; no hex/rgb/hsl in components/pages/ui css); `src/renderer/components/ui/*` (Icon, Surface, Button, IconButton, Input+Field, Select, Badge, Kbd, Dialog+ConfirmDialog, Toast, EmptyState, Tooltip, barrel) — Phosphor only (light/bold/duotone via `Icon`), tokens only, `aria` labels, visible focus ring, shortcuts shown via `Kbd`; `src/renderer/theme/ThemeProvider.tsx` (applies built-in via `data-theme`; custom via `base` + CSSOM `setProperty` of already-sanitised whitelisted tokens; clears previous overrides); `src/renderer/pages/dev/DesignPreview.tsx` (all primitives, theme select, Reload themes, load-error list); `main.tsx` wraps ThemeProvider/ToastProvider and hash-routes `#/dev/design-preview`; dev-page layout classes appended to `ui.css`.
**Decisions:** (1) Navy Ember's `--rt-red` is <4.5:1 on white by nature (tuned for the dark grid); print uses its own `--print-*` red (Phase 33), so the white-paper contrast check applies to Paper only (documented in the test). (2) Navy Ember is also the bare-`:root` fallback in `tokens.css`. (3) Final hex values live in `tokens.css` (the single source); `DESIGN_TOKENS.md` hex list stays "indicative".
**Verification actually run:** `npm run typecheck` ✅; `npm test` ✅ (9 files, 52 passed, 1 intentional skip). Electron 44.6.0 under xvfb with esbuild-bundled main/preload/renderer: `data-theme` + computed tokens correct for Navy Ember (`--surface-base #0f1a2e`, accent `#ff7b7b`) and Paper (`#e9edf4`, `#ba2f2f`) ✅; switching via the select reskins ✅; dropping `amber.json` + `evil.json` into the themes folder and clicking "Reload themes" listed "Amber (custom)" and reported `evil.json` as rejected (`url()`) ✅; Amber applied accent `#ffb347`, `--row-height: 99px` clamped to `32px`, unknown `--bogus` ignored ✅; no horizontal page scroll ✅; both screenshots inspected by eye ✅.

### Phase 4 — App shell, dashboard-first navigation, hotkey registry, command palette shell (2026-10-08)
**Contract first:** `API_CONTRACTS.md` §1 `shortcuts.ts` gained the concrete shape (+ `commandSearch.ts`, `routes.ts`) and a change-log row.
**Done:** `src/shared/shortcuts.ts` (single registry: `ACTIONS` for every palette command, `SHORTCUTS` for key bindings, `normalizeKeys`, `eventToKeys`, `matchShortcut(e, scopes)` with most-specific-scope-wins, `findShortcutConflicts`, `primaryKeys`); `src/shared/commandSearch.ts` (subsequence fuzzy ranking); `src/shared/routes.ts` (route table + pure history-stack functions, cap 50). Renderer: `router/routerStore.ts` (zustand in-memory router, `navCount`, a `#/dev/...` hash only seeds the first page so `#/dev/design-preview` still works), `stores/uiStore.ts` (palette/sheet open, one at a time), `hooks/useHotkeys.ts` (one window keydown listener → registry → handler; ignores `defaultPrevented`/IME, so Esc inside a dialog never also goes Back), `hooks/useAppActions.ts` (single action→behaviour map shared by hotkeys and palette), `components/layout/{AppShell,SideRail,CommandPalette,ShortcutSheet,navItems}`, `pages/{Dashboard,StubPage}.tsx`, `App.tsx`; `main.tsx` now renders `<App/>` (the Phase 3 hash switch is gone). Layout/palette/sheet/dashboard CSS appended to `ui.css` (tokens only).
**Registered shortcuts:** Ctrl+0..4 (Dashboard/Create/History/Customers/Settings), Esc (Back), Ctrl+N (New bill → opens Create stub), Ctrl+K (palette), F1 and Ctrl+/ (cheat-sheet). Palette also has "Open design preview" (no key). Everything else from `KEYBOARD_CELL_RULES.md` §8 is deliberately left to Phase 30 (freeze) and the phases that own those actions.
**Decisions:** (1) Side rail is shown on **every** page including the Dashboard (MASTERPLAN wording was ambiguous; simpler and always keyboard-reachable). (2) Esc = global `nav.back`, which is a no-op on the Dashboard; later pages/overlays must `preventDefault` Esc when they consume it (Create's Esc chain, Phase 28, will). (3) Conflict rule: same keys collide if same scope, or if one is `global`. (4) After each navigation focus moves to the page region (`tabIndex=-1`) unless the page already took focus. (5) Create/History/Customers/Settings are `StubPage` empty states naming the phase that builds them. (6) The nav-guard hook for unsaved bills is NOT added now; Phase 28 must route `navigate`/`back` through the guard.
**Verification actually run:** `npm run typecheck` ✅; `npm test` ✅ (11 files, 69 passed, 1 intentional skip) — includes registry tests (normalisation, scope matching, specificity, **conflict detector fails on any collision in the real registry**, unique ids, every shortcut → registered action), router stack tests, palette search ("go hist", "gthist" → Go to History). `npm install --ignore-scripts` was run once for typecheck/tests (lockfile unchanged). **Not run:** build, dev server, Electron, any visual/keyboard check (see Standing gaps).

### Phase 5 — SQLite, Drizzle, migrations, settings storage (2026-10-08)
**Contract first:** `API_CONTRACTS.md` (new `app:getDbInfo`, concrete `settings:*` rules, `ui.theme` now persisted, DB-layer helper signatures, change-log row), `docs/DATA_MODEL.md` (Phase 5 additions note), `DEPENDENCY_VERSIONS.md` log row.
**Done:** installed `drizzle-orm@0.45.3` + `drizzle-kit@0.31.11` (pinned). `src/main/db/schema.ts` — **all** DATA_MODEL tables incl. `sync_state`, `sync_outbox`, `backups` (12 tables, FKs, cascades, indexes); `drizzle.config.ts`; generated migration `src/main/db/migrations/0000_init.sql` (+ `meta/`). `db/migrate.ts` (own small journal-driven runner: each migration in a transaction, `user_version` = migrations applied), `db/connection.ts` (`openDatabase`: WAL, `foreign_keys=ON`, `synchronous=NORMAL`; DB with newer `user_version` → `query_only`, no migrate/seed, `writable=false`; pending migrations on an existing DB → **automatic `VACUUM INTO` pre-migration backup** into `backups/` first), `db/seed.ts` (idempotent single local branch, name `Main`, prefix `Q3-`, plus `po_counters` row), `db/poCounter.ts` (`allocatePoNumber` = one `UPDATE … RETURNING`, nests in the caller's transaction so a rolled-back save leaves no gap/duplicate; `peekNextPoNumber`), `db/settingsRepo.ts`, `services/settingsService.ts` (key regex, 64 KB cap, defaults overlay, read-only refusal), `ipc/settings.ts`, `shared/{po,settings}.ts`. `themeService` now persists `ui.theme` through the settings service. `index.ts`: Phase 1 `sqliteProof` removed, real DB opened at `%LOCALAPPDATA%\Quot3\quot3.db`, closed on `will-quit`. Bridge/preload/`register.ts` extended. Dashboard shows a dev-only DB info line. Import-boundary test no longer exempts `main/index.ts` (better-sqlite3 is now strictly under `src/main/db/`).
**Deviations/decisions:** (1) Migration is `0000_init` (drizzle-kit numbers from 0), not literally "0001"; `user_version` = 1 after it. (2) Own migration runner instead of drizzle's `migrate()` so `user_version` is the single source of truth and the downgrade guard + pre-migration backup sit around it. (3) Added unique index on `branches.po_prefix` (SYNC spec: prefix unique). (4) The pre-migration backup is a file only; it is not yet recorded in the `backups` table (Phase 50 owns that table/UI and should adopt `pre-migration-*.db` files). (5) Migration SQL location: dev reads `<appPath>/src/main/db/migrations`; **Phase 64 must ship the folder in packaged resources and point `migrationsDir` there.** (6) No bill-save logic, no UI beyond the dev line.
**Verification actually run:** `npm run typecheck` ✅; `npm test` ✅ (13 files, 84 passed, 1 intentional skip). New DB tests (real temp-file SQLite): migrations apply to an empty DB (all 12 tables, WAL, FK on, user_version = schemaVersion); reopen is a no-op (no re-seed, no backup); newer `user_version` → writes refused (SQL-level and service-level); pending migration takes a consistent backup first and the backup opens as the old v1 state with data intact; a failing migration rolls back fully; journal/SQL files agree; branch seed; settings round-trip + persistence across reopen + key/size validation + corrupt JSON = unset; PO counter increments, unique over 50 calls and across reopen, rolls back with the caller's transaction, passes 9999 → `Q3-10000`, unknown branch throws; FK cascade (`bill_rows`, `bill_revisions`), FK/unique constraints; `ui.theme` persists across restart. **Not run:** build, dev server, Electron, IPC over the real bridge (see Standing gaps). The "two simultaneous saves" PO case is only meaningful once Phase 27 exists; here atomicity is proven structurally (single-statement allocation inside a transaction; better-sqlite3 is synchronous in one process).

### Phase 6 — Money and calculation engine, pure (2026-10-08)
**Contract first:** `API_CONTRACTS.md` §1 (`money.ts`, `classifyRow`, `calcRow`, `computeBill` concrete shapes + decisions) and a change-log row.
**Done:** `src/shared/types.ts` (all §0 types, `ColumnDef`/`FormatDef` stub), `src/shared/money.ts` (decimal.js clone, precision 28, ROUND_HALF_UP; strict `D`, `tryD`, `isNumericStr`, `round2`, `roundInt`, `toDec`, `toMoney`, `fmtMoney` with Indian grouping + ₹, `fmtQty`, `add/mul/sum`), `src/shared/calc/{classifyRow,rowCalc,billTotals}.ts`. Fixtures: `__fixtures__/bills.ts` (terse row builder + the 18 visible rows of the legacy screenshot; items total 17,226.00 — rows 1–6 amounts are hidden behind the popup in the image so are qty × rate), `__fixtures__/formats.ts` (STUB GST / non-GST `FormatDef`s per MASTERPLAN §10; Phase 8's registry replaces them).
**Decisions (owner may flip):** (1) Blank GST % is treated as 0 % (GST format still shows `0.00` GST, Amount = Total). (2) Round-off line appears only when non-zero; with no adjustments it renders `Items total → Round off → Final total`. (3) A row with qty and an override but no rate is an item whose total is the override. (4) Warning codes are stable strings (`MISSING_NAME`, `MISSING_QTY_OR_RATE`, `INVALID_NUMBER`, `NEGATIVE_QTY_RATE`), `computeBill` prefixes them `CODE:rowId`. (5) Invalid cell text never throws in calc — treated as blank and flagged. (6) `D()` rejects exponents/hex/NaN/Infinity so a pasted `1e3` can't become 1000 silently. (7) GST-vs-plain behaviour comes from `format.columns` (has `gstAmt`) and `format.footer`; no format-name branching anywhere (grep for `=== 'gst'`/`'non_gst'` in `src/` is empty). (8) Row `total` money strings are always 2-dp (`"90.00"`).
**Verification actually run:** `npm run typecheck` ✅; `npm test` ✅ (16 files, 144 passed, 1 intentional skip). Covers: item with qty+rate; only qty; only rate; override wins (and GST from the overridden total); adjustment (name + total only) moves to the adjustment block; negative adjustment (incl. larger than items); GST format — adjustments in Amount column only, no GST on them; Sl.No skips adjustments/partials/blanks/nameless items and stays gapless; `0.1 + 0.2`, `1.1 × 1.1`, `19.99 × 3`, HALF_UP at `.005`/`.5` incl. negatives; sums beyond 2^53; **1,000,000-value sum exact** and a 100,000-row `computeBill`; the legacy screenshot bill reproduces every visible amount and the 17,226.00 total; round-off on/off; garbage text never throws. Property tests (seeded PRNG, no new dependency): footer = sum of rows and grand = items + adjustments over 200 random bills per format; totals order-independent over 100 shuffles per format; format switch leaves rows untouched and non-GST total == GST items total; round-off grand total always whole with |round-off| ≤ 0.5. Performance of calc (the 240 Hz budget) is **not** measured here — Phase 13/24 own that.
**Not covered / carried forward:** `paperRows` is exposed but the 40/30/80/70 thresholds are untouched (Phase 36). Phase 13 must keep incremental recompute (this phase's `computeBill` is whole-bill, ~tens of ms per 100k rows; fine per bill of ≤ ~100 rows but the grid must not call it per keystroke across the whole table without memoisation).

### Phase 7 — Units parser and extra-column summary engine, pure (2026-10-08)
**Contract:** `API_CONTRACTS.md` §1 `units/*` already matched; added `unitsSummaryToText` + `UNLABELLED_MARK` and a change-log row.
**Done:** `src/shared/units/{parseUnits,summarizeUnits,index}.ts`; fixture `__tests__/fixtures/legacy_ruby_tomato.ts`; `__tests__/units.test.ts`. All arithmetic via `money.ts` (decimal.js); units are canonical strings (`"5"`, `"0.5"`).
**Decisions (owner may flip):** (1) A bare `-5`/`=3` (empty code) is **not parsed** — there is no code, and it is more likely a negative number than a pair. (2) Only *trailing* stray brackets are stripped (per spec); a stray leading `(` stays in the code (`(125-5` → code `(125`). (3) A marked cell with blank text is labelled `(unlabelled)`. (4) D-X1 implemented as default: marked cell's own number is not counted. (5) Rows are processed in array order (not by `idx`). (6) `unitsSummaryToText(summary, 'units'|'marked')` added so Phase 22's popups can share the copy-as-text format; the 'marked' view omits columns with no marks.
**Verification actually run:** `npm run typecheck` ✅; `npm test` ✅ (179 passed, 1 intentional skip; 35 new). Proves: all 11 rows of the §2 table; en/em dash, minus sign, brackets, stray closers, fractions, exact decimals, last-separator-wins; legacy fixture → `ruby = 12`, marked `idk bruhh = 55`, `tomato = 7`; unmark merges back (67); first-row mark; multiple/adjacent marks; independent columns; no input mutation. `npm install --ignore-scripts` run once; lockfile unchanged. **Not run:** build/dev (owner: skip). Nothing UI-facing exists yet.

### Phase 8 — Format registry and bill data model, pure (2026-10-08)
**Contract first:** `API_CONTRACTS.md` §1 `format/registry.ts` extended (extra exports) and new `billModel.ts` section + change-log row.
**Done:** `src/shared/format/{registry,nonGst,gst,index}.ts` — registry with `registerFormat` (validates: snake_case id, unique id, one default, unique shortcut, unique column keys, `name` column present, print widths ≤ 100 %), `unregisterFormat` (non-built-ins only; for tests/future user formats), `hasFormat`, `getFormat` (throws on unknown), `resolveFormat` (stored ids: unknown → default), `listFormats`, `defaultFormatId`, `validateFormat`; entries are frozen. `src/shared/billModel.ts` — `createEmptyBill`, `createEmptyRow`, `normalizeBill`, `normalizeExtraCols`, Zod schemas (`billRecordSchema`, `billRowRecordSchema`, …), `serializeBill` (Bill → `{bill: BillRecord, rows: BillRowRecord[]}` using the Drizzle property names, recomputes `totalsJson` + `itemRowCount` through `computeBill`), `deserializeBill` (non-throwing `ParseResult`), `withFormat`. Tests: `format/__tests__/registry.test.ts`, `__tests__/billModel.test.ts`.
**Decisions (owner may flip):** (1) `createEmptyBill` yields `poSeq 0` / `poNumber ''` = "not allocated"; the PO number is allocated in the save transaction (Phase 27). Default 1 blank row (the grid pads for display, Phase 9). (2) `normalizeBill` nulls blank **and invalid** decimals (the model holds only valid `Dec`; cell-level invalid-text handling is Phase 12's job), keeps valid values as typed (only `12.`/`.5`/`+3` → plain notation), reindexes `idx`, re-ids duplicate row ids, drops empty runs, de-dupes extra columns (blank header → `Col N`), and **keeps extras for keys not in `extraCols`** so a removed column's data can return via undo. (3) `deserializeBill` keeps an unknown stored `format_id` as-is (never rewrites data on read); callers use `resolveFormat`. (4) `total` column is `editable: true` + `overridable` (the Phase 6 stub fixture says `editable:false`); Phase 13 decides how the override edit works. (5) `__fixtures__/formats.ts` stubs left untouched; a parity test asserts they match the registry on keys/labels/kinds/align/footer/A5 limit. Widths (`widthPx`, `printWidthPct`) are first guesses — Phases 9/33 may tune them. (6) `Bill.paperOverride` outside A4/A5 → null.
**Verification actually run:** `npm run typecheck` ✅; `npm test` ✅ (19 files, 208 passed, 1 intentional skip; 29 new). Proves: GST columns `slNo,name,qty,rate,total,gstRate,gstAmt,amount`; non-GST `slNo,name,rate,qty,total`; defaults/shortcuts/A5 limits/footers; a **dummy third format registers and drives `computeBill` with no other change**; validation rejections; serialise→JSON→deserialise equality (rich, empty, GST bills); totals cache = calc engine; shuffled rows re-ordered; malformed JSON/records rejected without throwing; `withFormat` keeps `rows` reference-identical and byte-identical across GST⇄non-GST; grep-style test fails on any `=== 'gst'` / `'non_gst'` comparison or `case` in `src/` (non-test files). **Not run:** build/dev (owner: skip). No DB write/read of bills exists yet (Phase 27), so the record shapes are checked against the Zod schema, not against a live SQLite insert.

### Phase 9 — Grid core render (2026-10-08)
**Contract first:** `API_CONTRACTS.md` — new `gridModel.ts` section + `billStore` skeleton note + change-log row; `ROUTES.devGrid` and action `dev.grid` documented.
**Done:** `src/shared/gridModel.ts` (pure): `MIN_TRAILING_BLANK_ROWS = 3`, `isEntryBlank`, `countTrailingBlankRows`, `ensureTrailingBlankRows`, `buildGridColumns` (registry columns + extras), `gridTemplateColumns`, `GridView`/`buildGridView` (body = item/blank/partial display rows, footer = everything else incl. adjustments; **structural sharing**: unchanged rows/footer keep object identity). `src/renderer/stores/billStore.ts` (zustand skeleton: `loadBill`, `setFormatId`, `patchRow`; `view` rebuilt via whole-bill `computeBill` on each change). `components/grid/{BillGrid,GridRow,GridCell,ColumnHeader,FooterRows,renderProbe,index}`; `styles/grid.css` (tokens only; imported from `index.css`; added to the no-hardcoded-colour scan); `pages/dev/GridDev.tsx` (legacy fixture + 80-row fixture; a **dev-only** format select — the real switching UI/shortcuts are Phase 20); fixtures `shared/__fixtures__/gridBills.ts`; route `/dev/grid`, palette action `dev.grid`.
**Architecture as built:** `GridCell` subscribes to one string (`view.body[rowId].cells[colKey]`), `GridRow` to its row kind only, `FooterRows` to `view.footer`, `BillGrid` to format/extra cols/body ids/footer count. CSS grid per row with a shared `--q3-grid-cols` custom property; sticky header; `contain: layout style paint` per row; no virtualisation; ARIA `grid/row/gridcell/columnheader` + `aria-rowindex/rowcount/colcount`.
**Decisions (owner may flip):** (1) **Adjustment rows render in the footer block**, not in the body — as Phase 6's `computeBill.display` and MASTERPLAN §9 specify. Consequence for Phase 11/19: an adjustment typed in the middle of the body will visually jump to the footer; Phase 19 must decide how the entry row is shown/edited. (2) Trailing blank rows are **real padded `BillRow`s in the store** (idempotent; ≥ 3 after the last used row; a row with only extras text/mark/style counts as used). **Phase 27 must trim trailing blanks on save.** (3) Cells show the formatted display strings (`fmtQty`, 2-dp money), not raw text; Phase 11–13 editing reads raw row fields. (4) Item names render as plain text; red/bold/highlight runs are drawn in Phase 15. (5) `billStore.view` recompute is whole-bill; Phase 13 makes it incremental — the render-count guarantee already holds. (6) Extra columns are 110 px, left-aligned; their header is `extraCols[].header`. (7) Added dev dependency `jsdom@30.1.2` (pinned) — no React Testing Library; tests use `react-dom/client` + `act`.
**Verification actually run:** `npm run typecheck` ✅; `npm test` ✅ (21 files, 230 passed, 1 intentional skip; +21 new). Pure: trailing-blank rule, columns for GST/non-GST/extras, template string, body/footer split, legacy bill total 17,226.00 and gapless Sl.No, structural sharing, view ≡ `computeBill` grand total, format switch reuses rows. **jsdom render tests:** header labels (non-GST/GST + extras), 18 items + 3 blanks + grand-total footer, first-row cell texts, format swap keeps the same row elements, 80-row bill renders all rows; **render counts** — renaming an item re-renders exactly 1 cell (0 rows/headers/footers/grid shell); changing qty re-renders exactly that row's qty + amount cells + the footer; on an 80-row GST bill a rate edit touches ≤ 4 cells of that row only; using the last blank row re-pads (that row's shell + 3 new rows) with no other existing row re-rendering. **Not run:** build, dev server, Electron, screenshots (see Known issues / standing gaps).

### Phase 10 — Cell navigation state machine, pure (2026-10-08)
**Contract first:** `API_CONTRACTS.md` `nav/cellNav.ts` section extended in place + change-log row; `PROJECT_STRUCTURE.md` marked.
**Done:** `src/shared/nav/cellNav.ts` (`navReduce`, `NavState`, `GridShape`, `KeyInput`, `NavEffect`, `LEFT_UNLOCK_PRESSES`, `LEFT_EDGE_LEAVES_CELL`, helpers incl. `isNumericCharAllowed`, `arriveAt`, `clickInText`, `syncCaret`, `dataEdgeRow`, `lastUsedCell`) and `src/shared/nav/__tests__/cellNav.test.ts` (84 tests; all 12 KB §9 cases + extras). Nothing outside `src/shared/nav` was touched except docs.
**Decisions (owner may flip; KB gave no ruling):** (1) `MOVE` always = arrive Append, caret at end. (2) Right/Left that leave a cell **wrap rows** like Tab (Left on an empty cell → previous cell; Left on a non-empty cell at the edge only leaves if D-K3 is flipped; an empty cell always lets Left leave in either mode). (3) **Enter on the last row** and Right/Tab on the very last cell emit `ENSURE_ROW`; Down/PageDown on the last row stop. (4) **Ctrl+A**: Append (or empty / range active / second press in Free) selects all cells via `EXTEND_RANGE{from,to}`; first press in Free is native text select-all. (5) **Range**: Shift+Up/Down always begin/extend; Shift+Left/Right begin a range only at the matching edge (Append: Shift+Right begins, Shift+Left unlocks to Free text-selection; empty cell: both begin). Shrinking back to the anchor ends the range. Any non-shift key collapses the range first (`COLLAPSE_RANGE`), except Delete/Backspace (→ `CLEAR_RANGE`, arrival Append), Esc (collapses, not PAGE_ESCAPE), Ctrl+A/C/X/V/Z/Y (NATIVE, range kept). Typing with a range collapses it and appends to the active cell (never destroys content). (6) Ctrl+Right: Free → next word end, at end edge / Append → like Right (next cell). (7) Alt+arrows, Ctrl+Tab, F-keys, Ctrl+B/Z/C/V etc. → `NATIVE` (owned by later phases/DOM). (8) Numeric predicate: digits, one `.`, `-` only at index 0 (and only if `allowNegative`); a digit/dot can't be inserted before a leading `-`. (9) `navReduce` takes an optional 4th `opts` arg so tests can flip D-K1/D-K3.
**Contract for Phase 11:** the DOM layer must call `syncCaret` after native edits / mouse / selectionchange (Free-mode edge detection depends on it), create `ENSURE_ROW` rows before applying the next `MOVE`, and treat `NATIVE` as "don't preventDefault". Free-mode printable characters in numeric cells are filtered using the tracked caret only (a selection that replaces a `.` is not modelled) — Phase 11/12's `beforeinput` sanitiser must be the final gate.
**Verification actually run:** `npm install --ignore-scripts` once (lockfile unchanged); `npm run typecheck` ✅; `npm test` ✅ (22 files, 314 passed, 1 intentional skip). **Not run:** build / dev server (owner: skip). No DOM exists for this phase, so real caret behaviour is a Phase 11 standing gap, not a Phase 10 one.

### Phase 11 — Cell editing modes in the DOM (2026-10-08)
**Contract first:** `API_CONTRACTS.md` — new `gridEdit.ts` section + change-log row.
**Done:** `src/shared/gridEdit.ts` (pure, see contract); `GridColumn` in `gridModel.ts` gained `editable`/`kind` (edited in place; existing tests untouched and green); `billStore` gained `setCellText`/`setCellTexts`/`ensureBodyRows`; `stores/gridUiStore.ts` (mode indicator state); `components/grid/gridController.ts` (**the single event pipeline**: one native listener each for `keydown`, `beforeinput`, `mousedown`, `mouseup`, `dblclick`, `focusin`, `focusout` on the grid root → `navReduce` → DOM effects), `gridDom.ts` (caret/selection helpers), `GridStatus.tsx` (APPEND / EDIT indicator, shows the armed-Left hint); `GridCell.tsx` edited in place (span now has **no React children** — its text is written by a layout effect, and left alone while `data-editing`, so React and the browser never fight over the same nodes); `BillGrid.tsx` attaches the controller (`tabIndex=0` root; focusing it activates the remembered/first cell); `GridDev.tsx` shows the status; `grid.css` appended (active outline, free/armed styles, range tint — tokens only).
**Behaviour implemented:** arrival always Append with caret at end (and Append re-parks the caret at the end before every key, so typing can never replace content); printable keys in Append are inserted by us at the end (`INSERT_TEXT`), in Free they are native; Left×2 / F2 / Home / Ctrl+Left / Shift+Left unlock; Esc in Free → Append (content kept), Esc in Append is NOT prevented so it bubbles to the global Back (Phase 28 adds the guard); Delete in Append clears + commits, native in Free; Tab/Shift+Tab/Enter/arrows/Ctrl+arrows/PageUp/Down/Ctrl+Home/End per the reducer, creating a trailing row via `flushSync(ensureBodyRows)` before moving into it; mouse: click on text → Free at the click point, click on cell padding → Append at end; native `historyUndo/Redo`, `insertParagraph/LineBreak` prevented; numeric cells: reducer filters keystrokes and `beforeinput` is the final gate (paste/drop validated against the resulting text; newlines → spaces in text cells); visible mode indicator.
**Decisions (owner may flip):** (1) **Sl.No, GST Amt and Amount are not focusable** — Tab skips them (so Tab from the last editable cell goes to the next row's Item Name). Total is focusable but read-only until Phase 13. (2) Commit on leave (see carried-forward). (3) An invalid numeric draft (`-`, `.`) reverts to the previous value on commit; empty clears. (4) Focus leaving the grid only commits — the cell stays active so coming back resumes. (5) `Enter` on the last row opens a new row (reducer decision from Phase 10).
**Verification actually run:** `npm run typecheck` ✅; `npm test` ✅ (24 files, 343 passed, 1 intentional skip; +29 new: 6 pure `gridEdit` tests, 23 jsdom pipeline tests — arrival/caret-at-end, append-typing, caret re-parking, Left arm/unlock/disarm, F2/Esc/Home/End/Right-edge exit, native keys unprevented in Free, commit on Tab, Delete semantics, numeric filtering + `beforeinput` gate + undo suppression + paste newline sanitising, Total read-only, Sl.No skipped, trailing-row creation, range mark + clear, mouse padding/text paths, focus-out commit, no clobbering by store updates elsewhere, **render budget: typing in Append = 0 React renders; leaving the cell re-renders only the committed cell**). **Not run:** build, dev server, Electron, any real-browser check (see Standing gaps — read the Phase 11 bullet).

### Phase 12 — Numeric cells and Sl.No auto rule (2026-10-08)
**Scope finding:** Phases 10/11 had already delivered keystroke filtering (`isNumericCharAllowed`, leading `-` only in Total via `allowsNegative`), the `beforeinput` final gate, D-M7 rejection of negative qty/rate/GST % in `applyCellText`, Sl.No rendering from `computeBill` (`cells.slNo`), and auto trailing rows. Phase 12 therefore added only the missing pieces and their proof; no `NumCell.tsx` was created (numeric behaviour lives in the single controller pipeline, a separate component would duplicate it) and `money.ts` was not changed (nothing needed).
**Contract first:** `API_CONTRACTS.md` `gridEdit.ts` line gained `sanitizeNumericPaste` + change-log row.
**Done:** `src/shared/gridEdit.ts` — `sanitizeNumericPaste(raw, current, start, end, allowNegative) => string | null`: strips whitespace/NBSP, thousands commas, a leading `₹`/`Rs`/`INR`, a trailing `/-`, normalises the unicode minus; then accepts only if the RESULTING cell text is a valid draft, else rejects the whole paste (null). `gridController.onBeforeInput`: paste/drop into a numeric cell now goes through it (previously `1,250` or `₹ 90` was rejected outright); typed characters unchanged.
**Decisions (owner may flip):** (1) A paste that is not a clean number inserts nothing (no partial insert). (2) Comma stripping is unconditional (`1,25,000.50` → `125000.50`); a European `1,5` would become `15` — acceptable for an Indian-rupee app, flagged. (3) Sl.No is derived (never typed) and updates on commit of the row's cells (Phase 11's commit-on-leave); live per-keystroke renumbering is not done — Phase 13 may revisit via `commit()`.
**Verification actually run:** `npm install --ignore-scripts` once (lockfile unchanged); `npm run typecheck` ✅; `npm test` ✅ (24 files, 351 passed, 1 intentional skip; +8 new: 4 pure sanitiser tests, 4 jsdom: paste `1,250` → `1250` and garbage inserts nothing, negative paste/typing rejected in qty/rate, Sl.No numbers a named row once qty or rate exists and un-numbers when both cleared, keyboard-only flow name → Tab → rate → Tab → qty → Tab gives rate/qty stored, Amount `100.00` and a Sl.No). **Not run:** build, dev server, Electron — real browser paste (clipboard `insertFromPaste` payload shape) is unconfirmed.

### Phase 13 — Auto-calculation wiring and total override (2026-10-08)
**Contract first:** `API_CONTRACTS.md` — `calcRowCached`/`calcStats`/`RowCalc.auto`, `DisplayRow.override`/`ovrAuto`, `billStore.resetTotal`, `applyCellText` 4th arg, action `grid.resetTotal` + change-log row.
**Done:** (1) **Incremental recalculation:** `calcRowCached(format, row)` (WeakMap per format → per immutable row object) used by `computeBill`; an edit replaces one row object so exactly one `calcRow` runs, then the footer is re-summed from cached row results. (2) **Override edit:** `TOTAL_OVERRIDE_EDITABLE = true`; `applyCellText` Total branch: blank → clear override (so **Delete** in Append resets to auto), unfinished drafts (`-`, `.`) revert, text numerically equal to what the cell showed is ignored (visiting never creates an override), anything else sets `totalOverride` (negatives allowed, D-M7). (3) **Marker:** body `DisplayRow` carries `override`/`ovrAuto`; `GridCell` sets `data-override` + `title` ("Manual total (auto: X)…") on the Total cell only (separate primitive selector, no extra renders); CSS corner flag + italic (not colour-only). (4) **Reset:** `billStore.resetTotal(rowId)`; **Ctrl+Backspace** on a Total cell (intercepted in the controller before the native word-delete), registered as `grid.resetTotal` (scope `grid`, palette "Reset total to auto", handler via `gridUiStore.resetTotal` set by the mounted controller); **right-click** an overridden Total → small menu "Reset to auto total" (Esc closes, focus returns to grid). (5) GST columns follow the effective (overridden) total — proven through the store with a format toggle.
**Decisions (owner may flip):** (1) No separate `TotalCell.tsx`: the marker lives in `GridCell` behind `col.key === 'total'` to avoid a second cell component with its own subscription/DOM-write logic. (2) You cannot pin an override equal to the current auto value (it is read as "unchanged"); type a different value, then it sticks. (3) An override typed on a row with a name but no qty/rate makes it an **adjustment**, which leaves the body for the footer on commit (Phase 19 owns how adjustments are entered/shown — carried-forward note from Phase 11 now live). (4) The context menu is inline in `BillGrid` (renderer-only, tokens only), not a global component.
**Verification actually run:** `npm run typecheck` ✅; `npm test` ✅ (25 files, 363 passed, 1 intentional skip; +12 new/updated): pure Total text rules; calc spy — first compute 80 `calcRow`s, identical recompute 0, one edited row exactly 1, cache is per format; jsdom — visiting Total changes nothing, typing `750` sets override and flags the cell, Delete resets, Ctrl+Backspace resets and is consumed, leading `-` accepted in Total, override survives a qty change and the footer stays, reset restores auto and footer moves, GST amount/amount from the overridden total and format switch keeps the override, a Total edit re-renders ≤ 2 cells, context menu appears only on overridden totals and resets. Shortcut conflict test (real registry) still green with the new binding. **Not run:** build, dev server, Electron (see Standing gaps).

### Phase 14 — Rich-text runs engine, pure (2026-10-08)
**Contract first:** `API_CONTRACTS.md` `richtext/runs.ts` section extended in place (concrete API) + change-log row; `PROJECT_STRUCTURE.md` marked.
**Done:** `src/shared/richtext/runs.ts` (+ `__tests__/runs.test.ts`): `plain`, `fromPlain`, `length`, `normalize`, `isCanonical`, `runsEqual`, `resolveRange`, `sliceRuns`, `concatRuns`, `styleAt`, `rangeState`, `toggleBold`, `toggleRed`, `toggleHighlight`, `setRedBold`, `clearFormat`, `applyEdit`, `applyTextChange`. Nothing outside `src/shared/richtext` and docs was touched — **`setPlainName` is NOT yet replaced** (Phase 15 wires `applyTextChange` into `applyCellText`/the controller).
**Decisions (owner may flip):** (1) Collapsed range (caret, no selection) formats the **whole cell** (KB §4). (2) Toggle rule = Excel: all flagged → remove; none or mixed → add. (3) `setRedBold` is a toggle: all red+bold → clears both; otherwise sets both (a bold-only selection gains red and stays bold). (4) Inserted text inherits the style of the first replaced char, else the char to the LEFT of the caret, else (caret at 0) the first char; an empty cell inserts plain; optional `style` override exists for Phase 15's pending-style typing (e.g. Ctrl+B with no selection in an empty cell cannot be stored in runs, so Phase 15 must hold it as UI state). (5) Surrogate pairs are never split: a caret inside a pair moves to its start; a selection edge inside a pair widens outward. Grapheme clusters (combining marks, Indic conjuncts) are **not** treated as units — code units are preserved exactly, but a selection may separate a base char from its combining mark. (6) Results are always normalised; inputs never mutated.
**Verification actually run:** `npm run typecheck` ✅; `npm test` ✅ (26 files, 456 passed, 1 intentional skip; +93 new): unit tests for every function and all selection cases (partial / whole / mixed / collapsed / reversed / empty cell), style inheritance, surrogate and Unicode safety, slice/concat; **property tests — 60 seeded random 80-step sessions** of mixed inserts/replaces/deletes/toggles/Red+Bold/clear, each step checked against an independent per-code-point reference model (text AND per-character styling equal), result canonical + `normalize` idempotent, and no lone surrogate; plus 300 random `applyTextChange` rewrites (plain text equals target, canonical). **Not run:** build/dev (owner: skip). No UI exists for this phase.

### Phase 21 — Extra columns: add / remove / rename (2026-10-08)
**Contract first:** `API_CONTRACTS.md` §1 (Phase 21 as built paragraph) + change-log row; `PROJECT_STRUCTURE.md` marked.
**Scope finding:** `ExtraColDef`, per-row `extras`, `buildGridColumns` (extras appended after the format's own, navigable), persistence and normalisation already existed (Phases 8/9/11), as did undo of whole-doc changes (`BillDoc.extraCols`). Phase 21 added the management surface only.
**Done:** `shared/extraCols.ts` (pure: `MAX_EXTRA_COLS`=12, `MAX_EXTRA_HEADER_LEN`=40, `extraColCount`, `sanitizeExtraHeader`, `nextExtraColKey`, `nextExtraColHeader`, `extraColHasData`, `addExtraCol`, `removeExtraCol`, `renameExtraCol`); `billStore` `addExtraCol` / `removeExtraCol` / `renameExtraCol` (each ONE labelled entry: `Added column 'Col 1'`, `Removed column 'ruby'`, `Renamed column 'a' to 'b'`); `shortcuts.ts` actions+keys `cols.add` Ctrl+Alt+=, `cols.remove` Ctrl+Alt+-, `cols.rename` Ctrl+Alt+R (all `global`, palette rows automatic); `stores/extraColUiStore.ts` + `stores/extraColActions.ts` (draft flush, target column, confirm, active-cell re-seat, status notices); `components/create/ExtraColsToolbar.tsx` (Plus / Minus / PencilSimple buttons + remove confirmation, mounted on `/dev/grid`); `components/grid/ColumnHeader.tsx` (double-click → in-place single-line `<input>`, Enter/blur commit, Esc cancel); `gridController.ts` ignores keydown/beforeinput/mousedown/copy/cut/paste from `.q3-gh__input`; `useAppActions` handlers; `grid.css` (tokens only).
**Decisions (owner may flip):** (1) **Remove deletes the column's cells from every row** (no orphan data); the undo entry's patches hold header, position and every cell/mark, so undo restores all (proven). (2) Ctrl+Alt+- / Ctrl+Alt+R / toolbar target **the active cell's column if it is an extra one, else the LAST extra column**; no extras → a status notice. (3) Confirmation only when the column has text or a mark; an empty column is removed immediately (still undoable). (4) New keys are the smallest free `cN` (also avoiding keys left in any row's `extras`), default header the smallest free `Col N` (case-insensitive). (5) Cap of 12 extra columns (status notice at the cap). (6) Header rename: whitespace collapsed to one line, capped at 40 chars; an empty or unchanged result keeps the old header. (7) After Add the active cell moves to the new column on the same row (only when a cell was active); after Remove it moves to the nearest surviving column. (8) Ctrl+Alt+= / Ctrl+Alt+- are `global` per the phase text; on layouts where Ctrl+Alt is AltGr they may not arrive (owner to check; rebind in `shortcuts.ts` only).
**Verification actually run:** `npm install --ignore-scripts` once; `npm run typecheck` ✅; `npm test` ✅ (41 files, 643 passed, 1 intentional skip; +16 new). Pure: add/default header/key-skip incl. orphan data/cap, remove (cells only of that key, other rows keep identity, unknown key), hasData (text, mark, blanks), rename sanitising/no-op cases, shortcuts match + no conflicts. jsdom: Ctrl+Alt+= adds `Col 1` as a labelled undoable entry; toolbar +; `extraColCount` exposed; empty column removed without confirm, column with data confirms then deletes header+cells; remove → undo restores headers/cells/dirty=false, redo removes again; Alt+1/Alt+2-style format switch keeps extras and rows byte-identical; double-click rename editor (focus, Enter commit + undo, Esc cancel, blank keeps old, 40-char cap); keys typed in the header input are not consumed by the grid; Ctrl+Alt+R opens the editor on the last extra column; typed data in an added column survives rename. **Not run:** build, dev server, Electron, any real-browser check (see Standing gaps).

**Contract first:** `API_CONTRACTS.md` §1 (Phase 23 paragraph) + change-log row; `PROJECT_STRUCTURE.md` marked.
**Done:** `shared/columnWidths.ts` (pure: `MIN/MAX_COL_WIDTH_PX` 40/640, `COL_WIDTH_STEP_PX` 16, `clampColWidth`, `sanitizeColWidths`, `applyColWidths`, `setColWidth`/`resetColWidth`/`resetFormatColWidths`, `stickyOffsets`, `STICKY_COL_KEYS` = slNo+name, `sliceGraphemes`); `settings.ts` default `grid.colWidths`; `stores/columnWidthStore.ts` (per-format overrides, loaded from / debounced-saved to the `grid.colWidths` setting through `window.quot3.settings`, in-memory when no bridge); `stores/columnWidthActions.ts`; `BillGrid` applies widths and sets `--q3-sticky-name-left`; `ColumnHeader` resize handle (drag = live resize, double-click = reset); `GridCell` sets the full text as `title`; actions+keys `grid.colWiden` Ctrl+Alt+], `grid.colNarrow` Ctrl+Alt+[, `grid.colWidthsReset` Ctrl+Alt+0 (global); `sanitizeExtraHeader` now cuts by grapheme (emoji-safe); `grid.css` (sticky Sl.No/Item Name via low-specificity `:where` rules, `unicode-bidi: plaintext`, active-cell overlay); fixture `longTextGridBill()` (300-char name, RTL, emoji/ZWJ/flag, 40-char header).

(4) Sticky columns are Sl.No and Item Name only. (5) Keyboard resize acts on the active cell's column in 16 px steps. (6) Truncation is CSS ellipsis (so RTL/emoji are never cut mid-cluster); the only JS cut is the header cap, now by grapheme. (7) Print wrapping of long names/headers is Phase 33+ (not touched).

### Phase 24 — Grid performance harness and GRID GATE (2026-10-08)
**Contract first:** `API_CONTRACTS.md` §1 (Phase 24 paragraph) + change-log row; `PROJECT_STRUCTURE.md` marked.
**Done:** pure `shared/perf/stats.ts` (`percentile` nearest-rank, `summarize`, `meetsBudget`, `perfRowsToMarkdown`) + constants `PERF_P95_BUDGET_MS = 4.2`, `PERF_KEY_HZ = 240` in `shared/constants.ts`; `renderer/perf/perfHarness.ts` (injected `PerfEnv`: clock, `flush`, optional `nextFrame`/`sleepUntil`; six scenarios `typeName | holdDown | tabRow | paste200 | formatSwitch | undoStorm` driving the REAL `GridController` with synthetic events; per-scenario `logic` and (browser) `paint` summaries, `renderProbe` counts, `long-animation-frame` count); dev page `pages/dev/PerfDev.tsx` at `/dev/perf` (`ROUTES.devPerf`, palette action `dev.perf` "Open perf harness (dev)", no key), results table + "Copy as PHASE_STATUS rows"; `grid.css` `.q3-perf`.
**Regression fix (hot path):** CPU-profiling the nav scenario showed every key doing several attribute-selector scans (`[data-row-id] [data-col] .q3-gc__text`), rebuilding the editable-column list, and rebuilding a row Map. `gridController.ts` now caches the span per (row, col) (a hit is trusted only if still connected and still under the same `data-row-id` / `data-col`), memoises `navCols()` on (formatId, extraCols identity) and the row Map on `rows` identity. Hold-Down logic path 25.25 → 9.40 ms p50 (40.9 → 18.7 p95) in jsdom; Tab 9.4 → 6.6 p50; no behaviour change (whole suite green).
**Decisions (owner may flip):** (1) Two metrics per scenario — `logic` (dispatch→DOM, synchronous because the controller uses `flushSync`) and `paint` (to next frame). (2) Paint waits are not awaited inline, so the 240 Hz pacing is not distorted by display refresh. (3) The "no whole-grid re-render" proof is a test assertion on `renderProbe`: nav/typing/undo never re-render the grid shell, and hold-Down/Tab re-render fewer rows than keys pressed. (4) Format switch re-rendering all rows is accepted and logged, not fixed (Known issues).
**Verification actually run:** `npm run typecheck` ✅; `npm test` ✅ (46 files, 675 passed, 1 intentional skip; +13 new: stats ×5, harness ×8 incl. page mount). **Not run:** `electron-vite build` / dev server / Electron / any real-browser check (owner: skip builds).

|---|---|

No production behaviour change except the type import.

**Done:** pure `shared/draft.ts`; constants `DRAFT_AUTOSAVE_MS = 2000`, `DRAFT_STALE_DAYS = 14`; `main/db/draftRepo.ts`, `main/services/draftService.ts`, `main/ipc/drafts.ts` (`billPayloadSchema` now exported from `ipc/bills.ts`), registered in `register.ts`; `index.ts` builds the service, sweeps stale drafts at launch and sends `drafts:recoverable` on `did-finish-load`; bridge `window.quot3.drafts` (`shared/ipc.ts`, preload). **Behaviour:** while the open bill is dirty, ~2 s after the last store change one timer fires and writes ONE draft row (id = bill-session id; `billId` set when it is an edit of a saved bill; payload = whole bill minus grid padding). Undo-to-clean, Save and Discard delete it. On launch drafts older than 14 days are removed in one transaction with ONE `audit_log` row (`draft`/`cleanup`, ids + count); the rest are offered on the Dashboard as "Recover unsaved bill — party, time" (button per draft + Discard with a confirmation). Recover opens it on Create with an EMPTY undo history, dirty, same session id (so autosave keeps updating the same row), through the unsaved-changes guard.

### Phase 33 — Print template engine and the Rough template (2026-10-09)
**Contract first:** `API_CONTRACTS.md` `print/printModel.ts` entry rewritten "as built" + change-log row; `PROJECT_STRUCTURE.md` updated.
**Done:** `shared/paper/paperTypes.ts` (`PaperDecision`, `simplePaperDecision` — Phase 36 imports the type, must not redeclare), `shared/print/printConstants.ts` (page mm, margins, font pt, width budget, font file list, `RUPEE`), `shared/print/printModel.ts` (`buildPrintModel`, `printColumnShares`, `buildPrintColumns`, `DEFAULT_PRINT_SETTINGS`, types), `shared/print/template/printCss.ts` + `renderHtml.ts` (`renderPrintHtml(model, {fontBaseUrl | fontUrls})`, `runsHtml`, `escapeHtml`), `main/print/template/index.ts` (`printFontsDir`, `printFontBaseUrl`, `renderBillHtml`), `resources/fonts/*` (Noto Sans 400/700, latin + latin-ext woff2 + OFL licence), `shared/__fixtures__/printBills.ts` (7 named print fixtures), dev page `/dev/print-preview` (route `devPrintPreview`, action `dev.printPreview`, palette only, `pages/dev/PrintPreviewDev.tsx`, `print/fontUrls.ts`, `types/vite-assets.d.ts`).
**What it does:** Rough model = party name/phone/transport only (no company, address, PO, date); body = item + partial rows (blank rows dropped; a row with only extra-column text kept); footer = Items total → adjustment rows → Adjustments total → (Round off) → Final total, or one "Total" — straight from `computeBill`, no money maths in print code; extras follow the format columns; name runs → `r`/`b`/`hl` spans; cell highlight kept; ₹ on the final amount only (rightmost value cell; `-₹400.00` for negatives; `rupeeOnTotal` setting). Column widths in hundredths of a percent, exactly 100%: the name column pays for extras (11% each) down to 20%, then everything scales. `@page` A5/A4 with 6/10 mm margins; header row repeats (`thead` table-header-group), rows never split; text cells wrap (`overflow-wrap:anywhere`), numerics never need to. `--print-*` tokens only, independent of the UI theme.
**Decisions (owner may flip):** (1) The renderer is PURE and lives in `shared/print/template/` (not `src/main/print/template/` as PHASES.md said) so the renderer-process dev preview can call it without importing `src/main`; the main folder holds the Node bits (font dir/URL, one-call `renderBillHtml`). (2) Fonts are **woff2 from `@fontsource/noto-sans` 5.3.0 (latin + latin-ext, regular + bold)**, not `.ttf`: GitHub raw downloads were blocked in the sandbox; the latin-ext file holds U+20B9 (checked with fontTools). Chromium embeds them as Noto Sans in PDF (verified). (3) **A5 text is 7.5 pt / A4 9 pt** — the smallest that puts 40 rough rows + the footer on ONE A5 page (owner's 40-row rule); the Professional template may need to differ. (4) Marks (Ctrl+M) are NOT printed (a mark is an on-screen segmenting aid). (5) Partial rows (name only / total only) ARE printed, never totalled. (6) Professional model DATA is built (company/address/PO/date) but `renderPrintHtml` refuses it with `PRO_TEMPLATE_NOT_BUILT` until Phase 34. (7) Dev preview uses the sample fixtures, not the open bill; it pins `simplePaperDecision` (real `decidePaper` is Phase 36). (8) `package.json` has no electron-builder config yet: Phase 66 must ship `resources/fonts` as extra resources (`printFontsDir(resourcesRoot)` expects `<root>/fonts`).
**Verification actually run:** `npm install --ignore-scripts` once; `npm run typecheck` ✅ (3 projects); `npm test` ✅ (66 files, 922 passed, 1 intentional skip; +29 new: `shared/print/__tests__/printModel.test.ts` (widths for 0–12 extras in both formats, legacy bill total 17226.00, adjustment order and values, GST Amount-only footers, runs/hl, blank/extra-only rows, Rough has no PO/address/company, no mutation, determinism, snapshots), `renderHtml.test.ts` (escaping, spans, @page A5/A4, font-face + ₹ range, `--print-*` only, header, colgroup = 100%, total-label span, wrapping, Pro refused, snapshot), `main/print/__tests__/template.test.ts` (font files exist and are real woff2, licence ships, file:// font URLs)). Extra one-off check (not a test): the generated HTML for legacy/adjustment/longText/40-row fixtures printed to PDF with the sandbox's headless Chromium — all 1 page (A5 40 rows, A4 40 rows), ₹ rendered, fonts embedded. **Not run:** `electron-vite build`, dev server, Electron, `webContents.print`/`printToPDF`, any real printer.

### Phase 35 — Long-text wrapping and multi-page A4 (2026-10-09)
**Contract first:** `API_CONTRACTS.md` — "Phase 35 additions" paragraph under the print entry + change-log row; `PROJECT_STRUCTURE.md` updated.
**Done:** (1) **Totals are their own row group** — `renderHtml.ts` emits `<tbody class="q3p-main">` (items) + `<tbody class="q3p-foot">` (totals); deliberately not a `<tfoot>` (that repeats on every page). CSS (`printCss.ts`): header row repeats (`table-header-group`), rows never split, the foot group never splits, last item row `break-after: avoid` so totals are never alone on a page, `hyphens: none`, `overflow-wrap: anywhere` on text cells/headers/body, numeric cells `nowrap` (a number is never broken mid-digit). (2) **Column floors** — `PRINT_MIN_SHARE` (Sl.No 8 %, numeric 11 %, extra 8 %, name 20 %) applied in `printColumnShares` only when extras squeeze the table (registry shares are untouched otherwise, so Phase 33 width tests still hold). (3) **GST registry print widths** changed (Sl.No 5→7, name 31→27, Quantity 9→11): at 5 % / 9 % the `Sl.No` and `Quantity` headers broke mid-word on A5 (seen in a screenshot). (4) **Fixtures** `name60`, `name120`, `header40`, `gstExtra3` (GST + 3 extras, long names/headers/cells), `rows81`, `rows150`. (5) **Real layout tests** — `main/print/__tests__/layoutHarness.ts` drives the sandbox's headless Chromium (no Electron, no new dependency): nothing exceeds the sheet / no horizontal overflow for 10 fixtures × A5/A4 × Rough/Professional; numeric cells and short headers are never broken (5 fixture/paper combos); a 120-char name wraps and the row grows; `rows81`/`rows150` on A4 → > 1 page, the header row on EVERY page, "Total" only on the LAST page together with item rows (via `pdftotext`, skipped if absent); 40 rough rows + footer = 1 A5 page. The suite skips itself when no Chromium exists. (6) Screenshots saved: `docs/reference/qa/{name120,header40,gstExtra3}-A5.png`, `gstExtra3-A4.png`.
**Decisions (owner may flip):** (1) **Multi-page A4 is accepted over a denser type tier**: 81+ rough rows simply flow onto a second A4 page at 9 pt (the Phase 33 carry-forward question); no row-count-driven font shrink. (2) GST + 3 extra columns (10 columns) is **not** word-clean on A5 — the paper engine (Phase 36) must send it to A4; it is tested on A4 only. (3) Numeric cells never wrap; if a number ever exceeded its column it would be clipped by the column, not wrapped — the floors are what prevent it, and the layout test proves the fixtures. (4) Professional header repetition on later pages is NOT done (only the table header repeats); the company/party block prints on page 1 only. (5) Header cells of one long unbroken word still break mid-word as a last resort (`overflow-wrap:anywhere`), by design.
**Verification actually run:** `npm run typecheck` ✅ (3 projects); `npm test` ✅ (67 files, 936 passed, 1 intentional skip; +10 new incl. the Chromium layout suite); layout checks ran in the sandbox's real headless Chromium (`/opt/pw-browsers/chromium-1194`). Updated snapshots (intended): rough adjustment HTML (new tbody split) and the extraCols/adjustment model widths in the squeezed case. **Not run:** `electron-vite build`, dev server, Electron `webContents.print`/`printToPDF`, Playwright, any real printer, owner look-review of the long-text output.

### Phase 36 — Paper engine (pure) and thresholds (2026-10-09)
**Contract first:** `API_CONTRACTS.md` — "Phase 36 as built" under `paper/paperRules.ts` + change-log row; `PROJECT_STRUCTURE.md` updated.
**Done:** `shared/paper/paperConstants.ts` (`A5_MAX_ROWS`, `A4_MAX_ROWS`) and `shared/paper/paperRules.ts` (`decidePaper`, `pagesFor`) implementing PAPER_ENGINE_RULES §3 exactly; the extra-column limit is read from `FormatDef.a5ExtraColLimit` (no format-id branching; the rule-2 wording is derived from the limit and the format label). Tests: all 17 §6 matrix rows (size, pages, warning present, requiresAck) plus wording, rule-2-before-rule-3, forced/overflow semantics, bad-input tolerance, unknown format; **property sweeps** (exhaustive, no new dependency): more rows never gives a smaller page or fewer A4 pages, more extra columns never gives a smaller page and beyond the format limit is never A5, pages ≥ 1, unforced A5 is always 1 page, forced is always honoured; **literal guard** `paperConstants.test.ts` fails if 40/30/80/70 appear as literals in any paper/print-related source file other than `paperConstants.ts`.
**Decisions (owner may flip):** (1) **Guard scope:** the spec says "no other source file", but an unscoped scan hits unrelated numbers (pixel sizes, 40/120-char field caps, calendar days, perf-harness counts, theme token ranges), so the guard scans files whose path contains `paper`/`print` or that use `decidePaper`/`PaperDecision`/the threshold names/`paperRules`/`paperConstants`. (2) **Forced A5 with many rows** gets pages for A5 (e.g. 60 rough rows → 2 pages) rather than 1, per "computed for that size". (3) **Measured overflow** adds a warning but not `requiresAck` (only rule 2 requires an ack, per §5). (4) Counts are floored and clamped at 0 rather than throwing; an unknown format id throws. (5) A bill that is both over the A4 row limit and has too many extra columns keeps `requiresAck: true` and paginates on A4.
**Verification actually run:** `npm run typecheck` ✅ (3 projects); `src/shared/paper` 34 tests ✅; full `npx vitest run` ✅ (69 files, 970 passed, 1 intentional skip). **Not run:** build, dev server, Electron, any caller (none exists until Phase 37).

### Phase 37 — Print flow: dialog, preview, force size, warnings, printing (2026-10-09)
**Contract first:** `API_CONTRACTS.md` — IPC table rows for `print:decide` / `print:print`, "Phase 37 as built" paragraph, change-log row; `PROJECT_STRUCTURE.md` updated.
**Done:** (1) Pure `shared/print/printFlow.ts` (decision wiring `decideForBill`, tolerant `resolvePrintSettings`, overflow geometry + `MEASURE_SCRIPT`, `printDialogKey`). (2) `main/services/printService.ts`: rules → render → measure (unforced A5 only) → re-decide with `measuredOverflow` → print the FINAL decision's HTML; ack enforced in main; cancel vs failure distinguished (`PRINT_FAILED`). (3) `main/print/electronPrintPort.ts`: hidden sandboxed `BrowserWindow` + `webContents.print` (silent only with a configured printer; template `@page` margins authoritative). (4) `ipc/print.ts` (strict Zod, reuses the bill payload schema), bridge + preload + `IpcContext.print`. (5) Renderer: `printUiStore`, `printActions`, `PrintDialog` (decision, reasons, warnings, live preview of the page that prints); Ctrl+P = Rough, Ctrl+Shift+P = Professional, bottom-bar buttons real. (6) Settings defaults `print.rupeeOnTotal` / `print.silent` / `print.printer`; `company.profile` read when present (screens are Phase 48).
**Decisions (owner may flip):** (1) **Acknowledgement key:** with `requiresAck`, Enter alone does NOT print — it highlights the warning; **Y** acknowledges, then Enter prints (PE says "one explicit confirmation key press"; PE §7 lists "A / 4 / 5" — here **A = automatic** (back to the rules' choice), 4 / 5 force). Forcing a size (4/5) clears the ack requirement because forced is always honoured. (2) **The bill's saved `paperOverride` counts as forced**; the A key also drops it for this print. A forced size is never measured. (3) **Measured-overflow switch is announced, not confirmed** (no ack) — the dialog already measured before Enter, so the owner normally sees the A4 decision first. (4) **Forced size is per print, not saved** onto the bill. (5) **Preview** is drawn in the renderer from the same pure model with bundler font URLs (main's file:// fonts are blocked by the renderer CSP `font-src 'self'`); main renders its own HTML for printing/measuring. (6) The dialog takes keys at the window (capture) while open because focus starts on the footer button. (7) Silent print is only honoured with a configured printer; otherwise the system dialog always shows. (8) The owner closing the system print dialog is quiet (no toast, no error). (9) Only the first `print:decide` per open measures at A5; 4/5/A re-decide (forced A5/A4 skip measuring).
**Verification actually run:** `npm run typecheck` ✅ (3 projects). New/updated tests ✅: `printFlow` (15), `printService` (13), `ipc/print` (4), `printDialog` renderer flow (9), updated `bottomBar` tests, and a real headless-Chromium check that `MEASURE_SCRIPT` + `isOverflow` agree with layout (legacy / 40 rough rows / name120 fit A5; 81 rows overflow). Full `npx vitest run`: 73 files — 1013 passed, 1 failed (a `#fff` literal I added to `grid.css` tripped the no-hardcoded-colours guard); fixed (`white`) and `tokensParity.test.ts` re-run ✅ (11 passed, 1 skipped); **a second complete full-suite run was NOT done**, since only that CSS line changed after the full run. **Not run:** `electron-vite build`, dev server, Electron `webContents.print`/the hidden window, any real printer (standing gap — owner confirms physical output; the PDF path arrives in Phase 38), key handling in the real app window.

Renderer: `stores/uiPrefsStore.ts`, `ThemeProvider` re-applies preferences after every theme change and loads them at start, three new Settings sections (**Themes**: list with Built-in / Custom badges, live apply, skipped-file errors with reasons, Open themes folder, Reload themes, Export current theme as a starter file; **Density and font size**; **Keyboard shortcuts**: read-only searchable table), three palette actions, `.q3-theme*` / `.q3-sc*` CSS (tokens only). Tests written: `shared/theme/__tests__/starter.test.ts`, `shared/settings/__tests__/uiPrefs.test.ts` (also the shortcut rows), three new cases appended to `themeService.test.ts`.
**Verification actually run:** (1) `npm install --ignore-scripts` -> 403 Forbidden from the registry; no `node_modules`; so **`npm run typecheck` NOT run and `npx vitest run` NOT run - nothing was proven by the project's own toolchain.** (2) With the global `tsc` (transpile only, `--ignoreConfig`, no dependency types) I compiled `starter.ts`, `uiPrefs.ts`, `shortcutList.ts` and `themeService.ts` with no errors reported other than the missing `node` / `zod` types, then ran a Node script: starter keeps only whitelisted safe tokens and passes `validateTheme`; unique ids (`-copy`, `-copy-2`, 40-char cap); pref validation / tolerant read / tokens; shortcut rows (81 actions) and search; service: `openFolder` creates the folder and calls the port, a failing port is refused, `exportStarter` writes a loadable file, never overwrites (second export gets `-copy-2`), appears in the next `list()`, unknown id = not found, unusable values refused. **The React components, the store, `ThemeProvider` change, IPC handler, preload, CSS and the new Vitest files were NOT compiled or run.**
**Decisions (owner may flip):** (1) Shortcut viewer is read-only; **rebinding is an open question** (per the phase text). (2) Density / font size = two optional whole numbers (row height 22-32, grid font 11-16, the same ranges themes are clamped to); empty = the theme's own value; applied on top of the theme and kept across theme switches. (3) The starter file is built from the LIVE computed tokens (built-in colours exist only in `tokens.css`) and is written straight into the themes folder as `<id>-copy.json`, never overwriting; the user then edits it and presses Reload themes. (4) "Preview" = applying live (one click); there is no separate try-then-revert mode. (5) Preferences use the generic `settings:get/set` (not `appSettings`), two sequential writes. (6) Theme errors come from the existing scan and are shown with file name and reason; they are not persisted.
**Not done / left for later:** rebinding shortcuts; a revert-preview mode; deleting a theme file from the app; font-family preference; the whole-UI scale (only the grid values).

### Phase 50 - Backup and restore (2026-10-09)

No schema change, no migration.
**Done:** pure `shared/backup/backupRules.ts`. Main: `db/backupRepo.ts`, `db/pendingRestore.ts`, `services/backupService.ts` (`create / list / status / autoIfDue / restore / delete / importFile / resetLocalData`), `ipc/backup.ts` (strict Zod), `import/electronBackupPorts.ts`; `main/index.ts` applies a staged restore BEFORE `openDatabase`, builds the service and fires the daily automatic backup at launch; bridge + preload `window.quot3.backup`. Renderer: `stores/backupStore.ts`, Settings **Backup and restore** section (Create backup, Import a backup file, Reset local data, list with kind / bills / size, Restore and Delete per row, typed RESTORE / RESET dialogs), `BackupRecoveryPrompt` in `AppShell` (start-up integrity failure), actions `backup.create` (Ctrl+Alt+B) and `settings.backup`, `.q3-bk*` CSS (tokens only). Tests written: `shared/backup/__tests__/backupRules.test.ts`, `main/services/__tests__/backupService.test.ts` (real temp SQLite + real files); the Phase 49 settings page test was updated in place for eight sections.
**Verification actually run:** `npm install` impossible (403) so **`npm run typecheck` and `npx vitest run` NOT run; no vitest file of this phase has ever executed.** What was run: the global `tsc` (transpile, no dependency types) over `backupService.ts`, `backupRepo.ts`, `pendingRestore.ts`, `backupRules.ts` (only missing-`node`/`zod`-types noise), then a Node script against a **SHIM of better-sqlite3 built on Node's built-in `node:sqlite`** (not the real module): manual backup opens and matches the bill count; daily rule (second call the same day does nothing) and pruning to 2 autos while the manual one stays; delete refuses `../x.db` and a missing file; a Phase 5 style `pre-migration-...db` is adopted; a garbage file is listed unreadable and Restore refuses it; Restore refuses without the word and does not restart; Restore (lower-case `restore` accepted) makes a pre-restore backup holding the 4-bill state, then `applyPendingRestore` swaps the 3-bill backup in, removes the marker, and a second apply does nothing; Reset needs `RESET`, backs up, removes the DB at the next launch; Import: cancel, good file as `imported`, garbage refused; read-only refuses create. **NOT run:** anything on the real better-sqlite3 (its `backup()` promise, `readonly` open of a WAL-mode copy, `pragma` result shape are assumed from its documented API), the React section / dialogs / store, the recovery prompt, the IPC handler, preload, Electron relaunch, the native Open dialog, CSS. No jsdom test of the Backup section was written.
**Decisions (owner may flip):** (1) Restore and Reset STAGE and relaunch (`app.relaunch(); app.exit(0)` after 400 ms) instead of swapping live; unsaved Create bills rely on draft autosave. (2) Only `auto` backups are pruned (default 14; setting `backup.keepAuto` is read by nothing yet and has NO Settings field); manual / imported / pre-restore / pre-migration are kept until deleted by hand. (3) **Import does not restore**: it adds the file to the list and the owner presses Restore (typed). Phase 58's branch bootstrap adds the prefix assignment on top. (4) A backup newer than this build (`user_version` higher) is refused. (5) The Dashboard's "last backup" still reads the newest `*.db` mtime, so a pre-restore copy also counts. (6) `Ctrl+Alt+B` navigates to the Backup section (so the result is visible) rather than showing a toast. (7) Reset keeps the `assets/` folder (logo file) on disk but the company profile in the database is gone. (8) Startup integrity runs synchronously when the service is created. (9) No auto-recovery: a failed check only prompts; it never swaps files by itself.
**Not done / left for later:** Settings field for the automatic-backup count; backup encryption / password; scheduled backups while the app stays open past midnight (only first launch of a day); a progress indicator for big files; the `backup:done` event is still unused.

### Phase 51 - Sync design lock and Supabase SQL (2026-10-09)
**Contract first:** `API_CONTRACTS.md` - change-log row + "Phase 51 as built" paragraph (server RPC contract, deliberate differences from the spec text); `docs/SUPABASE_MIGRATIONS.md` (new, the RPC reference + run order); `docs/security/SECURITY_MODEL.md` (sync section extended); `PROJECT_STRUCTURE.md`, `DEPENDENCY_VERSIONS.md` (log row, no package) updated. No app code, no schema change, no migration.
**Done:** `supabase/sql/01_licenses.sql`, `02_branches.sql`, `03_postbox.sql`, `04_rpcs.sql` (`qt_license_hash`, `qt_auth`, `qt_register_branch`, `qt_list_branches`, `qt_push`, `qt_pull`, `qt_ack`, `qt_touch` + grants), `05_sweep.sql` (`qt_sweep` + guarded pg_cron schedule), `90_selftest.sql` (about 45 PASS / FAIL checks in one transaction ending in ROLLBACK), `scripts/generate-license.mjs` (key + insert line, hash only), `src/__tests__/supabaseSql.test.ts`.
**Verification actually run:** No Postgres tool exists here (`psql`, `pg-mem`, `pglast` all absent; npm 403), so **no SQL statement was executed or even parsed by Postgres**. Run: (1) a Node script with the same checks as `supabaseSql.test.ts` over the SQL text: RLS enabled + revoke on all three tables, zero `create policy`, all 8 SECURITY DEFINER functions pin `set search_path = ''`, the six RPCs each call `qt_auth` and are the only granted ones, `qt_auth` / `qt_license_hash` / `qt_sweep` revoked from `anon` / `authenticated`, the license failures raise one identical message + code, no `service_role`, no readable bill column in `postbox`, balanced `$$` and parentheses, each file ends a statement, limits (200 / 1 MB / 12-byte nonce) present; (2) `scripts/generate-license.mjs` run for real: well-formed key, hash independently reproduced with Python `hashlib` for `Q3-AAAA-BBBB-CCCC` (`5153313e...0ed9`), label quoting (`O''Brien`), seat range refused, 2000 keys distinct, key trimmed / case-insensitive. **NOT run:** the vitest file itself, any SQL, the self-test, the pg_cron block, Supabase.
**Decisions (owner may flip):** (1) **Fixed-prefix hash instead of a per-row salt** (a salted hash cannot be looked up by equality); key stays 60 bits as the spec's format dictates - the weakness is recorded in `docs/SUPABASE_MIGRATIONS.md` / SECURITY_MODEL. (2) base64 text for bytea in JSON; `translate(encode(..,'base64'), E'\n', '')` because Postgres inserts line breaks. (3) Added limits: 8 MB JSON per push, about 4 MB per pull, 1000 ids per ack. (4) `qt_sweep` is not callable by the app and takes no license. (5) No wrong-key lockout (a failed call rolls back any counter); relies on Supabase rate limits. (6) Re-registering a branch updates it in place and takes no seat; no "remove branch" RPC yet. (7) Custom SQLSTATEs `Q3L01 ... Q3I03` so the client can map errors without parsing text. (8) Errors for a recipient outside the organisation and for an unknown sender branch are both `unknown_branch`. (9) `90_selftest.sql` uses `set local role anon` through a `pg_temp` helper to prove direct table access is closed; it needs a role allowed to `set role`.
**Not done / left for later:** executing any of it; a remove-branch / rotate-license RPC; wrong-key lockout; Edge Function for the sweep; the app side (Phases 52-60); `docs/SYNC_POSTBOX_SPEC.md` was NOT edited (differences are listed in API_CONTRACTS).

**Contract first:** `API_CONTRACTS.md` - `license` row concretised, "Phase 52 as built" paragraph, change-log row; `docs/DATA_MODEL.md` (`license.*` keys), `docs/security/SECURITY_MODEL.md` (client-side paragraph), `PROJECT_STRUCTURE.md`, `DEPENDENCY_VERSIONS.md` (log row, no package) updated. No schema change, no migration.
