Main: `sync/transport.ts` (`SupabaseTransport`, `InMemoryTransport`, `TransportError`), `sync/secretStore.ts` + `sync/electronSecretStore.ts`, `sync/licenseService.ts`, `db/syncStateRepo.ts`, `ipc/license.ts` (strict Zod), `main/index.ts` + `register.ts` + `preload` + `Quot3Bridge.license`. Renderer: `stores/licenseStore.ts`, `components/settings/SyncSection.tsx`, Settings section `sync`, palette action `settings.sync`, CSS in `grid.css`. Tests: `shared/sync/__tests__/license.test.ts` (7), `main/sync/__tests__/transport.test.ts` (14: retries, timeouts, error mapping, in-memory server rules), `main/sync/__tests__/licenseService.test.ts` (14 + 1 IPC, real temp SQLite: secrets never on disk in plain text, never in logs / audit / IPC results, all-or-nothing, offline vs license problem, deactivate round trip).
**Verification actually run:** registry reachable this session, so on a SCRATCH COPY: `npm ci --ignore-scripts` (lockfile unchanged), `better-sqlite3` compiled with `node-gyp rebuild --nodedir=/opt/node22`, `tsc --noEmit` on `tsconfig.node/web/integration` = clean, full `npx vitest run` = all green (1453 tests; the one failure it found, the Phase 50 import-boundary slip, was fixed and its test re-run). `npm run build` / dev server NOT run (owner instruction). Real Supabase, Electron `safeStorage` and the UI NOT run (see Standing gaps).
**Decisions (owner may flip):** (1) **`fetch` + PostgREST instead of `@supabase/supabase-js`** - three RPC calls need nothing more, no new dependency or lockfile change, and the injected-fetch seam is easy to test; swap later if the owner prefers the SDK. (2) The Supabase URL and anon key are typed by the owner in the activation form and stored in `sync_state` (the anon key is public); the passphrase is typed twice and stored encrypted for Phase 53. (3) The registered branch is the local branch with its stored name and (upper-cased) prefix; a prefix that does not fit the server rule is explained before anything is sent. (4) A local prefix change after registering is only DETECTED (`prefixInSync`, warning in the UI); fixing it = deactivate + activate again (same branch id, no new seat). (5) Offline / license problems never un-activate; `status({ check: true })` only records the link state. (6) Deactivate keeps bills and does not tell the server (there is no remove-branch RPC). (7) Activation is refused outright when `safeStorage` is unavailable - no plaintext fallback.
**Fixed from earlier phases (hard blockers for a clean typecheck / test run):** Phase 49 `ShortcutsSection.tsx` passed `Kbd` children instead of its `keys` prop (TS2322); Phase 50 `backupService.ts` imported `better-sqlite3` outside `src/main/db/` (importBoundaries test) - `probeDatabase` moved to `db/probe.ts` and re-exported from the service, behaviour unchanged. The settings-page test was updated for the ninth section.
**Not done / left for later:** the sync on/off switch and Sync status page (Phase 59); using the passphrase (Phase 53); remove-branch / rotate-license; re-register without deactivating; the `sync:status` event; a jsdom test of `SyncSection`.

### Phase 53 - Encryption layer (2026-10-10)
**Contract first:** `API_CONTRACTS.md` - change-log row + "Phase 53 as built"; `docs/DATA_MODEL.md`, `docs/security/SECURITY_MODEL.md`, `PROJECT_STRUCTURE.md`, `DEPENDENCY_VERSIONS.md` (no package) updated. No schema change, no migration, no IPC change.
**Found on arrival:** `crypto.ts`, `canonicalJson.ts` and their tests were already in the zip (not recorded in any doc, so presumably an earlier unfinished attempt). I reviewed them against SYNC spec section 4, ran their tests (27 green) and kept them unchanged; the phase's remaining work was the key caching / service wiring and the docs.
**Done:** `crypto.ts` (deriveKey, keyCheck / verifyKeyCheck, aadFor, gcmSeal / gcmOpen, seal / open with `Q3E1.` envelope, wrap / unwrap key through `SecretStore`, `SyncCryptoError` with WRONG_KEY / TAMPERED / MALFORMED / UNSUPPORTED, 32 MB inflate cap); `canonicalJson.ts`; `licenseService`: key derived at activation (injectable `kdfIterations` for tests), stored as `license.encKeyBlob` + `license.encKeyCheck`, passphrase no longer stored, `encryptionKey()` added, `deactivate` wipes it; `SyncSection` label now "Encryption key".
**Verification actually run:** Vitest on `src/main/sync` + `src/renderer/components/settings` = 62 tests green (crypto 20: RFC 7914 PBKDF2 vector, GCM spec test case 14, pinned deriveKey / key-check / sealed-bytes vectors, round trip, compression, fresh nonce, wrong passphrase, other salt, swapped bill id / revision, tampered ciphertext / tag / nonce / version, malformed, decompression bomb; canonicalJson 7; licenseService 18 incl. 3 new for the key). `tsc --noEmit` on node / web / integration = clean. **Not run:** the full `npx vitest run` (only the touched folders), `npm run build`, dev server, real Electron `safeStorage`, real Supabase, the Settings page in a window.
**Decisions (owner may flip):** (1) `LicenseStatus.passphraseSaved` keeps its name but now means "encryption key ready" (avoids an IPC / bridge change). (2) Activations made under Phase 52 have no key: Settings shows it as missing and the owner deactivates + activates again (no migration, since the passphrase is not recoverable without being typed). (3) `open` checks the key check BEFORE the GCM tag so a wrong passphrase is reported as such. (4) No separate "verify passphrase" action yet; `verifyKeyCheck` is available for a later one.
**Not done / left for later:** using seal / open on real bills (Phases 55-56); changing the org passphrase; a Settings control to re-enter the passphrase without deactivating.

### Phase 54 - Outbox (local change capture) (2026-10-10)
**Contract first:** `API_CONTRACTS.md` - change-log row + "Phase 54 as built"; `docs/DATA_MODEL.md`, `PROJECT_STRUCTURE.md`, `DEPENDENCY_VERSIONS.md` (no package) updated. No schema change, no migration (`sync_outbox` and `bills.sync_state` existed from `0000_init`).
**Done:** pure `shared/sync/outbox.ts`; `db/outboxRepo.ts`; `sync/outbox.ts` (`createOutbox`); the Phase 27 / 43 outbox hooks now carry an `op` and are wired in `main/index.ts` (capture only while a license is activated); `ipc/sync.ts` + `IpcContext.outbox` + preload / `Quot3Bridge.sync.outbox()`; `SyncTransport.push / pull / ack` with `SupabaseTransport` and a server-faithful `InMemoryTransport` (two virtual branches in one process); `crypto.sealedToParts / partsToSealed` so a sealed bill maps onto the postbox `nonce` + `ciphertext` columns.
**Verification actually run:** new tests: `sync/__tests__/outbox.test.ts` x12 (real temp SQLite: row written with the save and bill marked pending; nothing captured while not activated; failed validation leaves no row; a throwing hook and a rolled-back transaction leave no row; newer change replaces the unsent one; delete / restore ops and revisions; sent rows kept then pruned after 7 days; markSynced rules incl. conflict; attempts -> failed at 10, error cut to 300, retry; ordering; status; strict IPC), `sync/__tests__/postbox.test.ts` x11 (push / pull / ack between two virtual branches, fan-out, paging, all-or-nothing and every server limit, other organisation, a real sealed bill travelling A -> B and opening, parts round trip, `SupabaseTransport` request / answer mapping with mocked HTTP). `tsc --noEmit` on node / web / integration = clean. **One full `npx vitest run` = 118 files passed / 2 skipped, 1478 passed / 29 skipped, 0 failed.** **Not run:** `npm run build`, dev server, Electron, real Supabase (the three RPCs were still never executed on Postgres), any UI.
**Decisions (owner may flip):** (1) **Capture only while activated**; bills made before activation are never queued (Phase 58's backup import is the way to give a new branch history). (2) **One unsent row per bill**: a newer change deletes older pending / failed rows of that bill (the snapshot is built at push time, so older rows add nothing); a failed row therefore disappears if the bill is edited again before the owner retries. (3) No snapshot or hash is stored in the row (`payload_hash` stays NULL). (4) The number of rows is not capped: a long offline period just keeps one row per changed bill. (5) Inspection IPC is read-only (`sync:outbox`); retry / control channels belong to Phase 59. (6) Purging a bill from the trash for good does not queue anything - the tombstone was already queued when the bill was trashed. (7) Revision numbers in rows follow the existing hook: `head.revision + 1` for delete / restore (what the bill row really gets).
**Not done / left for later:** the push worker and anything that calls `push` (55); pull / apply / ack (56); no Sync status UI yet (59); no jsdom test for the new bridge method (no renderer code uses it yet).

### Phase 55 - Push worker (2026-10-10)
**Contract first:** `API_CONTRACTS.md` - change-log row + "Phase 55 as built"; `PROJECT_STRUCTURE.md`, `DEPENDENCY_VERSIONS.md` (no package) updated. No schema change, no migration, no IPC channel.
**Done:** pure `shared/sync/push.ts` (timing constants, `pushDelayMs`, status types); `main/sync/pushWorker.ts` (`createPushWorker`: `start / stop / kick / notifyOnline / runOnce / status`); `LicenseService.recipients()`; `main/index.ts` creates the worker after the bill service, the capture hook kicks it, `start()` only when the DB is writable, `stop()` on quit.
**Verification actually run:** in a SCRATCH COPY (`npm ci --ignore-scripts`; the `better-sqlite3` linux prebuild that ships in the package loaded as is): `src/main/sync/__tests__/pushWorker.test.ts` x13 (fan-out to every other branch with a real sealed bill opened by the recipient; one batched call and outbox order; delete / restore ops with the deleted flag and revisions 1-2-3; offline = no row blamed, doubling wait, kick ignored while backing off, `notifyOnline`, then everything flows; flaky push that fails twice; poison row isolated from a good one, 10 attempts -> `failed`, left alone, retry works; license pause and missing key / credentials block without blaming rows; restart resumes; no other branch settles rows; a branch that joined later is included; seal failure blames only its row; start / kick / stop timers; overlapping `runOnce` shared) and `shared/sync/__tests__/push.test.ts` x1. `tsc --noEmit` on node / web / integration = clean. **One full `npx vitest run` = 120 files passed / 2 skipped, 1492 passed / 29 skipped, 0 failed.** `npm run build` and the dev server NOT run (owner instruction). **Not run:** real Supabase (the RPCs were still never executed on Postgres), Electron, any UI; the real timers (`setTimeout`) path of the worker was only exercised through the injected timer seam.
**Decisions (owner may flip):** (1) **Connectivity problems never count against a row** (offline / timeout / server trouble), and neither does a refused license or a missing key: the worker pauses and backs off; only a request the server refuses for content, or a row that cannot be sealed, is blamed. (2) Such a refusal on a multi-row batch is retried one row at a time to isolate the culprit; a row is blamed at most once per run. (3) The snapshot is the stored bill record (minus local `syncState`) + stored rows + `deleted`, built at push time, sealed with the bill's CURRENT revision; the `op` follows `deleted_at`, not the outbox row. (4) The other-branch list is refreshed from the server every 10 minutes in memory (not persisted; the stored `license.branches` is the fallback), so a branch that joined after this one activated is included without re-activating. (5) A bill that no longer exists, or a license with no other branch yet, settles the row as synced with nothing sent (rows do not pile up; a late branch gets history from a backup import, Phase 58). (6) `kick()` is ignored while backing off; `notifyOnline()` is the way to retry sooner (Phase 59 retry button / Phase 56's regain hook). (7) A crash between the server accepting a push and `markSynced` re-sends that row: the receiver must apply idempotently (Phase 56). (8) A sealed bill over 1 MB is refused locally and blamed like any bad row (never silently dropped).
**Not done / left for later:** any IPC / bridge for push status or retry (Phase 59); a Sync status UI; automatic "network regain" detection (Electron `net` / `online` events are not wired: the 15 s loop and `notifyOnline()` cover it for now); pull / apply / ack (56); conflict handling (57).

### Phase 56 - Pull, apply, acknowledge (2026-10-10)
**Contract first:** `API_CONTRACTS.md` - change-log row + "Phase 56 as built"; `PROJECT_STRUCTURE.md`, `DEPENDENCY_VERSIONS.md` (no package) updated. No schema change, no migration.
**Done:** pure `shared/sync/pull.ts`; `db/remoteApplyRepo.ts`; `sync/remoteApplier.ts` (idempotent apply, held changes for the open bill); `sync/pullWorker.ts` (30 s loop, cursor, pending acknowledgements, skip after 10 runs); IPC `sync:editor / held / resolveHeld` + bridge + preload; event `bills:remoteChanged { id, held }`; `main/index.ts` wiring; `licenseService.deactivate` also clears `sync.*`. Renderer: `remoteChangeStore`, `RemoteChangeBanner` on Create, `startRemoteSync` in `AppShell`, palette actions `sync.keepMine` / `sync.loadTheirs`, `.q3-remote*` CSS (tokens only).
**Verification actually run:** in a SCRATCH COPY (`npm ci --ignore-scripts`, shipped better-sqlite3 prebuild): `main/sync/__tests__/pullWorker.test.ts` x15 - bill A -> B intact with rows / revision / branch row, acknowledged, no outbox echo; replay (same row twice = once); update / older-after-newer / delete tombstone / restore; crash between apply and ack (restart acknowledges, not applied twice); decrypt failure stays unacknowledged, skipped after 10 runs while later bills still arrive; wrong key; offline / license pauses; editor-open hold, newer replaces held, Load theirs, Keep mine, release on leaving, held loses to a later local edit; strict IPC; unknown sender branch; ordering rule + `pullDelayMs`. `remoteChangeBanner.test.ts` x2 (jsdom, fake bridge). `tsc --noEmit` on node / web / integration = clean. **Full `npx vitest run` = 121 files + the new banner file, all green (1508 passed, 29 skipped).** `npm run build` / dev server NOT run (owner instruction). **Not run:** real Supabase (`qt_pull` / `qt_ack` SQL never executed), Electron, the real window (banner look, focus), real timers.
**Decisions (owner may flip):** (1) The bill counts as "open in the editor" for as long as it is the bill in the Create session (also while the owner is on another page), reported by the renderer through `sync:editor`. (2) A held change is stored in `sync_state` (`sync.held.<billId>`), so it survives a restart; it is acknowledged on the server as soon as it is safely held. (3) Leaving the bill applies the held change by the normal ordering (a later local save wins and the held one is dropped). (4) A row that cannot be decrypted / applied blocks the rows behind it for up to 10 runs (about 5 minutes plus backoff), then is skipped for good and stays unacknowledged on the server (listed in `PullStatus.skipped`). (5) Origin / sender branches unknown locally are created from the server's branch list; a bill's `party_id` that does not exist locally is stored as NULL (the snapshot keeps the name). (6) An applied bill whose local copy had unsent edits keeps `sync_state = 'pending'`; its outbox row then re-sends the (now remote) content, which the other side ignores. (7) Load theirs replaces whatever is on screen (unsaved edits included). (8) The tie-break author of a local version is approximated (see next-phase note).
**Not done / left for later:** conflict recording (`conflict` state, audit row, loser stored as a revision) and the convergence property tests (57); the Sync status UI and any IPC for pull status / retry (59); automatic network-regain detection (`notifyOnline()` exists); History / Dashboard lists do not yet refresh live on `bills:remoteChanged` (they reload when opened); `fromBranchName` in the banner is always empty (the banner says "another branch").

### Phase 57 - Conflict resolution and tombstones (2026-10-10)
**Contract first:** `API_CONTRACTS.md` - change-log row + "Phase 57 as built"; `PROJECT_STRUCTURE.md`, `DEPENDENCY_VERSIONS.md` (no package) updated. No schema change, no migration.
**Done:** pure `shared/sync/conflict.ts` (`sameContent`, `ConflictDetail`, `ConflictInfo`); `RemoteApplyRepo` extended (record / rows / authorOf / putRevision / deleteRevisionsAbove / auditConflict / conflicts / clearConflict); `remoteApplier` records a conflict when a local version loses a race (full local snapshot into `audit_log`, bill marked `conflict`), ignores an identical version re-sent by another sender, replaces the revision row of a reused number and drops revision rows above the applied one; the local version's author is now the revision row's `saved_by_branch`; IPC `sync:conflicts` / `sync:dismissConflict` + bridge; `pullWorker` counts conflicts and flags the event; `billService` stores the local branch as `saved_by_branch`.
**Verification actually run:** in a SCRATCH COPY: `conflicts.test.ts` x8 and `conflict.test.ts` x2. Scenarios: concurrent edits (later wins on both branches, loser kept intact in the audit snapshot, `conflict` survives a send and is cleared by dismiss), an older edit arriving later is ignored with no conflict, delete vs edit in both directions (kinds `delete` / `edit`), delete then restore resurrects on three branches and a late tombstone does not kill a restored bill, identical re-send changes nothing, strict IPC. **Property 1:** 40 seeds x 4 receivers, 7 random versions (time ties, revisions 1-4, authors, deletes, plus duplicates) applied in a different random order on each receiver: every receiver ends on the same state as the maximum by the ordering rule (revision, time, deleted flag, content AND author). **Property 2:** 25 seeds x 3 branches doing 14 random saves / deletes / restores with random partial pushes and pulls, then settling: all three branches hold identical bills (revision, time, deleted flag, rows) and no outbox row failed. `tsc --noEmit` on node / web / integration clean. **Full `npx vitest run` = 124 files passed / 2 skipped, 1518 passed / 29 skipped, 0 failed.** `npm run build` / dev server NOT run (owner instruction). **Not run:** real Supabase, Electron, any window; no renderer code was added in this phase.
**Decisions (owner may flip):** (1) **What counts as a conflict:** the local version differs in content AND (it was never sent, or it was made on this computer and the winner's revision number is not higher). A winner with a HIGHER revision over a local version that was already sent is treated as a normal continuation (not recorded); the older local version then stays in the revision list. (2) **Where the loser lives:** the audit snapshot only. It is NOT put back into the revision list, because the winner's revision number can be the same number (unique per bill) and a lower winning number would collide with the next save. (3) `conflict` persists until the owner dismisses it or saves the bill again. (4) The conflict list returns only the latest conflict per bill still marked `conflict`. (5) Revision rows above an applied revision are deleted (clock-skew case). (6) A delete / restore made here has no revision row; its author is taken to be this branch, so a lost delete is recorded as a conflict of kind `delete`.
**Known limits / carried forward:** (a) a concurrent edit chain where the remote side made MORE edits (higher revision) than the local already-sent version is not recorded as a conflict (the local version stays in the revision list as an older revision). (b) Purging a bill from the trash is local only: if another branch later edits that bill it reappears. (c) Two versions with exactly the same time, revision and author id are impossible by construction and not handled. (d) Clock skew between computers decides "newer": a branch with a clock far ahead wins every race (spec accepts last-write-wins on `updated_at`; Phase 60 adds a note). 
**Not done / left for later:** any UI for conflicts (a list, badge, "restore my version" from the audit snapshot) - Phase 59; History / Dashboard live refresh; the bootstrap import (58).

### Phase 58 - New-branch bootstrap import (2026-10-10)
**Contract first:** `API_CONTRACTS.md` - change-log row + "Phase 58 as built"; `PROJECT_STRUCTURE.md`, `DEPENDENCY_VERSIONS.md` (no package) updated. No schema change, no migration.
**Done:** pure `shared/sync/bootstrap.ts`; `db/bootstrapRepo.ts` (opens the backup file READ-ONLY, one transaction on the live DB, `dryRun` rolls back); `sync/bootstrapService.ts` (file checks, prefix rules, safety backup first, audit row `bootstrap_import`); `ipc/bootstrap.ts` + `IpcContext.bootstrap` + preload / `Quot3Bridge.bootstrap`; `main/index.ts` wiring (safety backup = `backup.create('manual')`). Renderer: `stores/bootstrapStore.ts`, `components/settings/BootstrapPanel.tsx` (shown under `SyncSection`), palette action `settings.joinBranch`, `.q3-bs*` CSS (tokens only). Flow: Phase 50 Import (or an earlier `imported-*` file) -> review counts / branches / PO prefix -> Import -> next-step hint.
**Verification actually run:** in a SCRATCH COPY (`npm ci --ignore-scripts`, shipped better-sqlite3 prebuild): `main/sync/__tests__/bootstrap.test.ts` x9 on real temp SQLite pairs - preview is a dry run with the real counts and writes nothing; apply keeps ids / PO numbers / branch ids / revisions / rows, marks bills `synced`, queues nothing, leaves `sync_state` (license / cursor) untouched; clashing prefix refused until a new one is given, then the next local bill is `NB-0001` while imported ones keep `Q3-`; second import adds nothing; trashed bills skipped; an existing bill is kept as edited here and a same-branch-same-sequence bill under another id is skipped; a mid-way failure rolls everything back (including the prefix); unsafe / missing / newer files, read-only DB and a failing safety backup refused; strict IPC. `tsc --noEmit` on node / web / integration = clean. **Full `npx vitest run` = 127 files passed, 1555 passed / 1 skipped, 0 failed.** `npm run build` / dev server NOT run (owner instruction). **Not run:** the panel in a window, Electron's file dialog, any real second computer, real Supabase.
**Decisions (owner may flip):** (1) **Merge, not restore** (a restore would replace this computer's license, keys, branch and settings). (2) **Dedupe by bill id only**; an existing bill is never overwritten by the file. (3) **Trashed bills in the file are not imported.** (4) **Imported bills are `synced`, never queued**; other branches already have them, or get them from their own backup. (5) **This computer keeps its local branch id**; only its prefix can change (same transaction). The file's own local branch arrives as a remote branch, so its PO numbers stay valid and unique. (6) **Customers are added by id only** (no name matching), live ones only. (7) A safety backup is always made first (kind `manual`; a failure stops the import). (8) Do this BEFORE activating sync, so the prefix is registered once; after activation a changed prefix needs Deactivate + Activate (existing Phase 52 warning).
**Not done / left for later:** the Sync on/off switch and status (59); a jsdom test for `BootstrapPanel`; History / Dashboard live refresh after the import (they reload when opened); name-based customer dedupe; importing the file's trashed bills.

### Phase 60 - Sync hardening and two-branch simulation (2026-10-10)
**Contract first:** `API_CONTRACTS.md` - change-log row + "Phase 60 as built"; `PROJECT_STRUCTURE.md`, `docs/SUPABASE_MIGRATIONS.md` updated. No schema change, no migration, no IPC, no package, no UI.

**Verification actually run (scratch copy, `npm ci --ignore-scripts`):** `harness.test.ts` x5: FlakyTransport seeded / both loss kinds; a push whose answer was lost is re-sent and the other branch ends with one copy; a bill over 1 MiB is blamed and never sent while the next bill flows; clock skew (+10 min) converges with the fast clock winning; **soak = 3 seeds x 1000 random operations** (create / edit / delete / restore / go offline / push / pull, 15% lost requests, 10% lost answers, up to 2 s virtual delay, 90 s skew on one branch) -> after healing every branch has the same bills (revision, time, deleted flag, rows), the postbox is empty, nothing pending or failed. Metrics line printed for seed 11 (calls by function, losses, items and bytes moved). `tsc --noEmit` node and integration clean. **Not run:** full `npx vitest run`, `tsc` for the web project (no renderer file touched), build / dev server (owner instruction), real Supabase.
**Decisions (owner may flip):** (1) Payload limits already existed from Phases 52 / 55 (client pre-check at 1 MiB per sealed bill, batch caps); Phase 60 proves them rather than changing them. (2) "Metrics logged" = the harness logs a content-free counts-and-bytes line; the real workers keep their existing `log.info / warn` lines, no new always-on telemetry was added. (3) Delays are virtual (summed, not slept) so 1000 operations run in about a second. (4) Clock skew is accepted, not corrected: documented in `SUPABASE_MIGRATIONS.md`; a server-time comparison warning is left for later.
**Not done / left for later:** real-Supabase run (owner checklist); automatic clock-skew warning; a dev-menu button to run the harness from the app (it is a test-time harness only); the Phase 57 limit (a) (remote chain with more edits than a sent local version is not recorded as a conflict) is unchanged.

### Phase 61 - Audit log and crash logging (2026-10-10)
**Contract first:** `API_CONTRACTS.md` - rows for `audit:*` / `diagnostics:*`, change-log row + "Phase 61 as built"; `PROJECT_STRUCTURE.md` updated. No schema change, no migration, no package. New setting `audit.retentionDays` (default 365).
**Done:** pure `shared/audit.ts` and `shared/diagnostics.ts`; `db/auditRepo.ts`, `db/diagnosticsRepo.ts`; `services/auditService.ts` (`record / list / facets / retentionDays / purgeOld`), `services/diagnosticsService.ts`, `crash.ts` (session marker, crash records, `installCrashHandlers`), `ipc/audit.ts`; `billService` (create / edit / delete), `trashService` (restore) and `backupService` (import) take an optional `audit` sink and write inside their own transactions; `index.ts` installs the handlers before ready, starts / ends the session marker, records staged restore / reset and crash recovery at launch, runs the retention sweep, wires everything. Renderer: `errorReporting.ts`, `ErrorBoundary` (around `App`), `diagnosticsStore`, `auditStore`, `CrashNotice` (Dashboard), `AuditSection` (Settings > Activity log), palette actions `settings.audit`, `diagnostics.copy`; CSS in `grid.css` (tokens only). `TrashHead` gained `poNumber`.
**Verification actually run (scratch copy, `npm ci --ignore-scripts`):** `audit.test.ts` x7 and `diagnostics.test.ts` x4 (pure); `auditService.test.ts` x7 on real temp SQLite (one row each for create / edit / delete / restore with the local branch, a failed save writes none, filters / literal `%` search / day range / paging, conflict snapshot not returned, retention keeps conflict rows of bills still in conflict and writes ONE purge row, read-only and a throwing repo, strict IPC); `crash.test.ts` x12 (FORCED exceptions through fake `process` / `app` emitters: scrubbed log line with no user folder or license key, crash recorded, non-Error rejection, clean exits ignored, duplicates logged once, a throwing logger never escapes, dispose; clean quit = no notice, unclean exit = notice that survives until dismissed, crash blamed only on its own run; diagnostics service: scrubbed renderer report logged once, Copy diagnostics text has counts and log lines only, failing probes survive); `phase61Ui.test.ts` x4 (jsdom: window error / rejection reach the bridge, crash notice Copy / Dismiss, Activity log list / details / retention, error boundary fallback). `tsc --noEmit` node / web / integration clean. **One full `npx vitest run` = 133 files passed / 2 skipped, 1585 passed / 29 skipped, 0 failed** (after one expected exact-match fix in `settingsPage.test.ts`: ten sections now). `npm run build` and the dev server NOT run (owner instruction). **Not run:** Electron, a real crash, the real clipboard, any window.
**Decisions (owner may flip):** (1) **An uncaught exception in main is logged and recorded but does not stop the app** (Electron's default dialog is replaced); the log and Copy diagnostics hold the details. (2) The "did not close properly" notice is driven by an unclean exit (`session.json`), not by every logged error; it stays on the Dashboard until dismissed. In dev, stopping the dev server counts as an unclean exit and shows it. (3) Audit rows for bill create / edit hold PO number, revision, party name and row count (local database only); logs and diagnostics never hold bill content. (4) Backup restore / reset audit rows are written at the NEXT launch into the new database (the old one is replaced), detail `{ staged: true }`. (5) Retention is a Settings value (90 days to 5 years offered), applied at launch only; conflict rows of bills still in conflict are never swept. (6) Renderer error reports are throttled (same message once, 25 per run). (7) Audit stays local; it is not synced. (8) The Activity log is read-only (no manual delete).
**Not done / left for later:** audit rows for settings changes and customer create / update (only customer delete is audited, as before); an "Open logs folder" button; "restore my version" from a conflict row; a dev-menu way to force a crash. The `npm audit` note under carried-forward is unchanged (not in scope).

### Phase 62 - Auto-updater (2026-10-10)
**Contract first:** `API_CONTRACTS.md` - change-log row + "Phase 62 as built"; `PROJECT_STRUCTURE.md`, `DEPENDENCY_VERSIONS.md` updated. No schema change, no migration. New package `electron-updater` 6.8.9 (exact pin, `package.json` + `package-lock.json`). New settings `updates.auto`, `updates.channel`, `updates.feedUrl`.
**Done:** pure `shared/updater.ts`; `services/updateService.ts` (state machine, schedule 45 s then 6 h, install gate, audit rows); `services/updaterPort.ts` (real electron-updater, lazy, `autoDownload` / `autoInstallOnAppQuit` off); `ipc/updates.ts`; event `updates:state`; bridge + preload; `main/index.ts` wiring (`prepareInstall` sets `quitConfirmed`, `will-quit` stops the timer). Renderer: `updateStore` (+ `startUpdates` in `AppShell`, reports unsaved state to main), `UpdateBanner`, Settings > Updates (Check for updates, progress, Restart to update, background switch, channel, optional address), palette `settings.updates` / `updates.check`, audit labels, CSS (tokens only).
**Verification actually run (scratch copy, `npm ci --ignore-scripts`):** `shared/__tests__/updater.test.ts` x5; `main/services/__tests__/updateService.test.ts` x12 against a MOCK FEED port (newer -> download -> ready with progress, same / older / garbage version = up to date, unreachable feed -> short sentence with no host in it, channel / GitHub / generic feed reach the updater with no downgrade, invalid saved address makes no request, auto switch vs manual check, schedule 45 s / 6 h, unpackaged = unavailable and never touches the updater, second check does not re-download, install refused while a bill is unsaved or nothing downloaded and restarts nothing, install after save audits and calls quitAndInstall once, setConfig validation, strict IPC); `phase62Ui.test.ts` x4 (jsdom: banner only when ready and says it waits while a bill is unsaved, Restart on a clean bill installs, on an unsaved bill the Save / Discard / Cancel guard opens and nothing installs, Settings section Check / Save address / refused address reason). `tsc --noEmit` node / web / integration clean. **Full `npx vitest run` = 136 files passed / 2 skipped, 1606 passed / 29 skipped, 0 failed** (after one expected fix in `settingsPage.test.ts`: eleven sections now). `npm run build` and the dev server NOT run (owner instruction).
**Decisions (owner may flip):** (1) An update downloads by itself after a check (background), but INSTALLS only from the owner's click. (2) Install is gated twice: renderer guard (`requestLeave`) and main (`updates:setBusy` from the store; `install()` refuses while busy). (3) `autoInstallOnAppQuit` is off, so quitting never installs silently. (4) Address rules: empty = the address electron-builder bakes into the installer (Phase 64), `github:owner/repo`, or an https:// folder; http and user:password are refused. (5) Beta channel allows pre-releases, never downgrades. (6) Not running from an installed copy (dev) = "Updates are not available here". (7) The banner is a fixed bar at the top right, shown only when an update is ready. (8) Updater errors are shown as one short sentence; raw text only goes to the log (message only).
**Not run / standing gaps:** a REAL update flow (no release exists yet; needs Phase 64's `app-update.yml`, a published release with `latest.yml`, and an installed older copy); electron-updater itself (only its port was faked); Windows signature verification (Phase 65); the banner / section layout in a window; real restart-and-relaunch behaviour with the window `close` handler (`quitConfirmed` is set before `quitAndInstall`; unconfirmed in Electron).
**Not done / left for later:** release notes display; download pause / cancel; update check when the network returns; "skip this version".

### Phase 63 - App icon and installer branding (2026-10-10)
**Contract first:** `API_CONTRACTS.md` - change-log row + "Phase 63 as built"; `PROJECT_STRUCTURE.md` updated. No schema change, no migration, no IPC channel, no package. `package.json` gained `productName` and `author` (needed by electron-builder in Phase 64).
**Owner input:** first a 500 px PNG (artwork only ~314 px, so too small), then the real 1024 px image: icon on a solid black background with a Gemini sparkle watermark at the bottom right.
**Done:** `resources/icons/` = `icon.ico` (16, 24, 32, 48, 64, 128, 256), `icon-256.png`, `icon-512.png` (transparent corners), and `source-original-1024.jpg` (the untouched upload, kept so the icons can be regenerated). Background and watermark were removed by cutting the rounded square itself (edges found from the image, corner radius 140 px fitted and checked by eye against three radii), so both the black and the watermark fall outside it; the dark glow around the artwork is gone. Pure `shared/appInfo.ts` (`APP_NAME`, `APP_ID = app.quot3.desktop`, `PUBLISHER`, `ICON_FILES`, version scheme `MAJOR.MINOR.PATCH[-beta.N]`, `formatVersion`, `copyrightLine`, `pickIconPath`); `main/appIcon.ts` (`resolveAppIcon`: packaged `<resources>/icons` first, then `<app>/resources/icons`; `.ico` on Windows, PNG elsewhere); `window.ts` takes `iconPath`; `index.ts` sets `app.setAppUserModelId(APP_ID)` on Windows and passes the icon. Renderer: `settings/AboutSection.tsx` (icon, name, version from `app:getInfo`, tagline, publisher, copyright), Settings section `about` ("About Quot3"), palette `settings.about`, `.q3-about*` CSS (tokens only).
**Verification actually run (scratch copy, `npm ci --ignore-scripts`):** `shared/__tests__/appInfo.test.ts` x5 (identity, version scheme, `pickIconPath`, REAL file checks: `icon.ico` header parses, every entry inside the file and exactly the 7 sizes; both PNGs square RGBA 8-bit at 256 / 512), `main/__tests__/appIcon.test.ts` x2, `settings/__tests__/aboutSection.test.ts` x1 (jsdom). Final icon looked at as an image on white (clean edges, no black, no watermark). `tsc --noEmit` node / web / integration clean. **Full `npx vitest run` = 141 files passed, 1642 passed / 1 skipped, 0 failed** (after one expected fix in `settingsPage.test.ts`: twelve sections now). `npm run build` / dev server NOT run (owner instruction).
**Decisions (owner may flip):** (1) The art is used as the owner's rounded-square tile with transparent corners (not a flat square). (2) One `.ico` serves window, taskbar, installer, uninstaller and shortcuts. (3) `APP_ID` = `app.quot3.desktop` and publisher `Quot3`: placeholders the owner may rename BEFORE the first release (changing `APP_ID` later breaks pins and updates). (4) Version scheme is plain semver, `-beta.N` for the beta channel; the shown version is `package.json` (0.1.0) until the owner bumps it. (5) About shows no license text or web address (none exists yet).
**Not run / standing gaps:** the taskbar, title-bar and Alt+Tab icon in a real Windows window; how the icon looks at 16 / 24 px (small sizes are plain downscales of the 1024 art, not hand-tuned); the About layout in a window; the installer / uninstaller icon (Phase 64 wires it). The 512 px PNG is a downscale of the 1024 art, so nothing is upscaled.
**Not done / left for later:** hand-tuned 16 / 32 px icon variants; installer header / sidebar bitmaps (Phase 64 may add them); a web address and licence text in About.

### Phase 64 - Windows installer (2026-10-10)
**Contract first:** `API_CONTRACTS.md` - change-log row + "Phase 64 as built"; `PROJECT_STRUCTURE.md`, `DEPENDENCY_VERSIONS.md` updated. No schema change, no migration, no IPC. New devDependency `electron-builder` 26.15.3 (exact pin; `package-lock.json` only gained packages, nothing existing changed). `package.json` scripts `pack`, `dist`.
**Done:** `electron-builder.yml` (appId `app.quot3.desktop`, per-user NSIS: `oneClick: false`, `perMachine: false`, `allowElevation: false`, choose-folder allowed, desktop + Start-menu shortcuts, run after finish, `Quot3-Setup-${version}.exe`, icons from Phase 63, no file associations, `npmRebuild: false`, only `out/**` + `package.json` in the asar, better-sqlite3 trimmed to the win32-x64 binary and unpacked from the asar, `extraResources` = migrations, fonts, icons); `build/installer.nsh` (uninstall asks "Also delete your Quot3 data?", default No, silent = keep, never asked during an update); `main/runtimePaths.ts` (`resolveRuntimePaths`: installed = `<resources>\{migrations,fonts,icons}`, dev = source tree) used by `index.ts` for the migrations folder and the print fonts root (this closes the Phase 5 / Phase 33 "Phase 64 must ship the folder" notes). No auto-launch is registered anywhere (test-checked).
**Verification actually run (scratch copy):** `main/__tests__/runtimePaths.test.ts` x7 (paths installed / dev; the yml's identity, per-user flags, shipped folders, referenced files all exist, no `setLoginItemSettings`, uninstall prompt defaults No and skips updates). **`electron-builder --win --dir` really ran on Linux** with a stub `out/main/index.js` (the real build was skipped per owner): config accepted by electron-builder's schema, the Windows Electron 44.6.0 app (`Quot3.exe`) was assembled, `resources/` held `migrations` (with `meta/_journal.json`), `fonts`, `icons`, and `app.asar.unpacked` held only `better-sqlite3/prebuilds/win32-x64.node` plus its JS. The first try correctly failed without `out/` ("entry file not found"). `tsc --noEmit` node and integration clean (web untouched). `npx vitest run src/main/__tests__ src/shared/__tests__/appInfo.test.ts` = 8 files, 40 passed. Full suite NOT re-run (Phase 63 full run was 1642 passed; this phase touched only `index.ts`, one comment and new files).
**Decisions (owner may flip):** (1) **Publish target NOT set** - the owner has not said GitHub or a web address, and a wrong placeholder would fail silently. So the installer has no `app-update.yml`; auto-update works only after the owner either adds a `publish:` block (GitHub: `provider: github`, `owner`, `repo`; or `provider: generic`, `url`) and publishes `latest.yml`, or types the address into Settings > Updates. (2) `npm run dist` uses `--publish never`. (3) x64 only. (4) The uninstaller keeps data unless the owner clicks Yes (data is in `%LOCALAPPDATA%\Quot3`, outside the install folder). (5) Install folder is the default per-user `%LOCALAPPDATA%\Programs\Quot3`; the owner may change it in the installer.
**Not run / standing gaps:** the real `electron-vite build` into `out/` and the NSIS installer .exe (needs Windows or a CI with wine; **not built, no `Quot3-Setup-x.y.z.exe` exists yet**); installing, launching, shortcuts, uninstall prompt, per-user (no admin) behaviour on Windows; better-sqlite3 loading from `app.asar.unpacked` on a real install; migrations / fonts found under `resources\` at run time; the Phase 62 real update flow. SmartScreen will warn on an unsigned installer (Phase 65).
**Not done / left for later:** publish block, code signing (65), installer sidebar bitmaps, arm64, a CI workflow file.

### Phase 65 - Code-signing and SmartScreen notes (2026-10-10)
**Contract first:** `API_CONTRACTS.md` - change-log row + "Phase 65 as built"; `PROJECT_STRUCTURE.md` updated. No schema change, no migration, no IPC, no app code, no package.
**Done:** `docs/RELEASE_SIGNING.md` (what signing does, certificate options with Microsoft's Oct 2026 prices / eligibility, SmartScreen in plain words, antivirus false-positive guidance, how to switch signing on for a certificate file / token or Azure Artifact Signing, 12-step release checklist, owner decisions still open). `electron-builder.yml` `win.signtoolOptions` (SHA-256, DigiCert RFC 3161 timestamp; `publisherName` only as a commented example). `scripts/signing-env.mjs` (reports which method is configured, never prints a value; exit 1 when nothing or only part is set). `package.json` scripts `check:signing` and `dist:signed` (env check, then build with `-c.forceCodeSigning=true`).
**Verification actually run (scratch copy):** `main/__tests__/signing.test.ts` x8 (script exit codes for nothing / half-set / complete certificate / Azure, both variable spellings, no secret ever printed, script parses; yml has the hooks and no secrets or early `publisherName`; `dist:signed` order and force flag; doc sections present). `electron-builder --win --dir` re-run with the new `signtoolOptions`: config accepted, Windows app assembled, no error without a certificate. `tsc --noEmit` node clean. Prices and rules were read from Microsoft Learn "Code signing options" and electron-builder's docs on 2026-10-10 (web), not tested.
**Decisions (owner may flip):** (1) **Recommendation only, nothing bought:** Azure Artifact Signing if the owner's country is eligible, otherwise OV with the CA's cloud signing. (2) `publisherName` stays unset until a real certificate exists (setting it earlier would make every update check fail). (3) `npm run dist` stays unsigned for testing; `dist:signed` is the release command. (4) Signing secrets only via environment variables / CI secret store. (5) The doc tells users to expect SmartScreen warnings on early releases; no claim that signing removes them.
**Not run / standing gaps:** a real signature (needs a certificate and, for hardware-token OV, the token machine); `Get-AuthenticodeSignature` output; SmartScreen / antivirus behaviour of a real download; Azure setup; `publisherName` update verification with a real signed pair of releases; the 12-step checklist itself (nothing to release yet). electron-builder logs "signing with signtool.exe" even with no certificate: it produced no error and no signature here, but a real unsigned-vs-signed check on Windows is still owed.
**Not done / left for later:** CI workflow file that runs the checklist; a PowerShell verify script (the command is in the doc, not shipped untested); Store (MSIX) package.

### Phase 66 - Playwright-Electron e2e suite (2026-10-10)
**Contract first:** `API_CONTRACTS.md` - change-log row + "Phase 66 as built"; `PROJECT_STRUCTURE.md`, `DEPENDENCY_VERSIONS.md` updated. No schema change, no migration, no IPC channel. New devDependency `@playwright/test` 1.63.0 (exact pin; lockfile only gained packages). One one-line app change: `electronBackupPorts.restart()` skips `app.relaunch()` when `QUOT3_E2E=1` (so the restore test starts the second run itself instead of leaving a stray copy; normal runs unchanged, test-checked).
**Done:** `playwright.config.ts` (one worker, no retries, `tests-e2e/**/*.e2e.ts`); `tsconfig.e2e.json` (now part of `npm run typecheck`); `tests-e2e/helpers/app.ts` (launch the BUILT app `out/main/index.js` with a private empty data folder via `HOME` / `LOCALAPPDATA` / `USERPROFILE`, `--no-sandbox` on Linux, `QUOT3_E2E=1`, `QUOT3_IMAGE_DEBUG=1`; keyboard helpers `go`, `palette`, `startBill`, `typeItem`, `typeAdjustment`, `saveBill`; `bridge(page).call('ns.method', ...)`) and `helpers/bills.ts` (bill payload builders, `pdfPageSize` from the PDF MediaBox, `pngSize`). Specs: `01-create-history` (Dashboard -> Create -> keyboard-only bill with an adjustment -> Ctrl+S -> History -> reopen -> edit -> Esc prompt -> Discard leaves revision 1, Save makes revision 2, same PO), `02-format-switch` (Alt+1 / Alt+2 lossless, hidden GST % kept after save), `03-extra-col-mark` (Ctrl+Alt+=, units text, Ctrl+M, Ctrl+U and Ctrl+Shift+M dialogs, mark kept after save), `04-print-pdf` (real `print.pdf`: small = A5 1 page, 41 rough rows = A4 1 page, 31 professional rows = A4, 3 extra columns = A4, 81 rows = 2 A4 pages; page size read from the file), `05-image-copy` (real `image.copy`: PNG pixels equal the reported size, A5 ratio, A4 larger), `06-trash-restore` (Delete -> Recently deleted -> restore, same PO, revision + 1), `07-backup-restore` (Ctrl+Alt+B, change data, restore refused without the typed word, restore, app exits, relaunch shows the backed-up data and a pre-restore copy exists).
**Verification actually run (scratch copy, `npm ci --ignore-scripts`):** `tsc --noEmit` for `tsconfig.e2e.json` and `tsconfig.node.json` clean; `src/main/__tests__/e2eSuite.test.ts` x7 (seven spec files present, exact pin and scripts, config, restore-relaunch guard, `pdfPageSize` / `pngSize` / bill builders on synthetic data); `npx playwright test --list` parses all 12 tests. Full `npx vitest run` = 141 files passed / 2 skipped, 1635 passed / 29 skipped, 1 failed: `pdfExport.test.ts` (real-Chromium PDF test) failed once under the full parallel load and **passed 6/6 when re-run alone** (load flake, not touched by this phase). `npm run build`, the dev server, Electron and `xvfb-run` were NOT run (owner instruction to skip builds).
**Decisions (owner may flip):** (1) PDF / image / trash / backup specs call the real bridge (`window.quot3.*`) from the page instead of driving print dialogs, because a print dialog, the clipboard and native file dialogs cannot be scripted reliably; `print.pdf` is given `savePath` so no Save dialog opens. (2) Create / History flows are keyboard-only (shortcuts + palette); three spots use a mouse click on purpose (landing on the GST % cell and on row 1 of the grid) and are marked in the specs. (3) Tests start from an empty database each file; no seeded data. (4) Typecheck now includes the suite.
**Not run / standing gaps:** EVERY spec. Selectors and key paths were derived from the source (aria labels, `data-col`, `data-party-field`, `role=dialog`, shortcut registry) and from the Phase 32 jsdom flow, not from a live window, so the first real run may need small fixes (most likely: where focus lands after opening a bill from History, whether Delete in History asks for confirmation (the spec accepts it if it does), what the Tab order does around the derived Amount column, the exact text of the Units summary). Image copy goes to the real clipboard in the app; the spec only checks the reported size and the debug PNG. The "headless via xvfb" part is wired (`npm run test:e2e:xvfb`) but unproven. A real-window pass of items already listed under Standing gaps is still owed.
**How the owner runs it:** `npm install` once, then `npm run test:e2e` on a machine with a display (or `npm run test:e2e:xvfb` on Linux). It builds first (`electron-vite build`), then runs the 12 tests; each starts its own Quot3 with a temp data folder, so your real bills are never touched.
**Not done / left for later:** a CI workflow; screenshots / trace on failure beyond Playwright's defaults; GST print and Professional-mode image specs; History search and revisions specs; the party autocomplete flow.

### Phase 67 - Full keyboard-only acceptance (2026-10-10)
**Contract first:** `API_CONTRACTS.md` - change-log row + "Phase 67 as built"; `PROJECT_STRUCTURE.md` updated. No schema change, no migration, no IPC, no package. New pure data `CONTEXT_KEY_HINTS` in `shared/shortcutList.ts`.
**Done:** (1) `ShortcutSheet` (F1 / Ctrl+/) was missing every palette-only action and every in-control key; it now lists ALL 94 actions (keys, or "Palette (Ctrl+K)") plus a section "Inside the grid, lists and dialogs" built from `CONTEXT_KEY_HINTS` (taken from `KEYBOARD_CELL_RULES.md` and the list / dialog code). The sheet's scroll area is now keyboard focusable (`tabIndex 0`, `role=region`). A key shown twice (F1 + Ctrl+/) is now one row with both keys. CSS tokens only. (2) New tests: `src/__tests__/keyboardAcceptance.test.ts` x12 (ActionId type == `ACTIONS` ids, unique ids and labels, no key conflicts, every action has a handler in `useAppActions` and every handler is a registered action, the keyless actions equal a REVIEWED palette-only list of 35, the five pages / palette / help / save / new have keys, core bill / history / customers / output actions have keys, every IconButton taken out of the Tab order has a key twin, no click handler on a plain element without a role, double-click and right-click uses are a reviewed list each with a keyboard twin); `layout/__tests__/keyboardAcceptance.test.ts` x6 (jsdom: sheet lists every action once and the context keys, Dialog Tab / Shift+Tab wrap and Esc closes and focus returns to the opener, palette opens focused, Tab stays in it, Enter runs the top hit, **every one of the 94 actions is found by its own label and runs from the palette with the keyboard**); +1 in `shortcuts.test.ts` (context hints well-formed). No focus trap or missing handler was found by these checks; the only real gap fixed was the incomplete F1 sheet.
**Verification actually run (scratch copy):** `npm run typecheck` (4 projects) clean. **Full `npx vitest run` = 144 files passed / 2 skipped, 1655 passed / 29 skipped, 0 failed.** `npm run build`, dev server, Electron and the Phase 66 e2e suite NOT run (owner instruction / no display).
**Keyboard checklist (evidence = how it was checked; "A" automated test, "S" read from source, "W" needs a real window and is NOT confirmed):**
- Dashboard: reach with Ctrl+0 (A); cards and lists are tabbable, lists take arrows / Home / End / Enter (S); recover-drafts via palette `draft.recover` (A); crash notice buttons are buttons (S). Real tab order and focus ring: W.
- Create: Ctrl+1 / Ctrl+N; party fields Ctrl+Shift+F, date Ctrl+Shift+E, address Alt+Down / `create.addressClose`; party autocomplete Up / Down / Enter, Esc closes the list only (S, jsdom test from Phase 32); grid cell keys per `KEYBOARD_CELL_RULES.md` (Phases 10 / 11 tests); formats Alt+1 / Alt+2 (A + Phase 32); extra columns Ctrl+Alt+= / - / R, Mark Ctrl+M, Units Ctrl+U / Ctrl+Shift+M (A); undo / redo / history panel / revert (Phases 17 / 18 tests); save Ctrl+S, Esc guard S / D / Esc (Phase 28 / 32 tests); outputs Ctrl+P, Ctrl+Shift+P, Ctrl+I, Ctrl+Shift+I, Ctrl+Shift+T, PDF via palette (A: keys exist + palette runs them). Real caret / focus behaviour: W (see Standing gaps; the Phase 66 spec `01-create-history` is the first live check).
- History: Ctrl+2; dates / bills lists arrows / Home / End / PageUp / PageDown, Enter opens, Delete deletes, Ctrl+A, Ctrl+F search, Ctrl+Shift+D trash, Ctrl+Z undo delete, revisions Ctrl+Shift+V; filters / refresh / restore / purge are palette-only (A). Trash view uses Enter (restore), Delete (purge), Esc (back) shown on its buttons (S). Branch filter is a native select (S).
- Customers: Ctrl+3; search, add (Insert), edit (Enter / F2), delete, bills registered with keys (A); inline edit fields tab in order (S).
- Settings: Ctrl+4; every section is a palette action (A); the sections' own controls are native inputs / buttons (S). Backup now Ctrl+Alt+B (A).
- Dialogs and overlays: all use `Dialog` (Tab wrap, Esc, focus return: A) or handle Esc themselves (UndoPanel, FormatPalette, CommandPalette, grid menu: S scan found Escape handling in each); palette Tab is swallowed on purpose (A).
- Help: F1 / Ctrl+/ (A), Ctrl+K (A); the sheet scrolls by keyboard (S: tabbable region; real scrolling W).
- Not keyboard-reachable on purpose: none found. Mouse extras (double-click on a list row, the grid's right-click "Reset total to auto") each have a key twin (A).
**Decisions (owner may flip):** (1) A key-less action is allowed when it is reachable from the palette; the 35 such actions are pinned in a reviewed list, so a new key-less action fails the test until it is added there on purpose. (2) Not every palette-only action got a new key (that would use up the Ctrl / Alt combinations for rarely used commands); the likely candidates if the owner wants more keys: `history.refresh`, `history.clearFilters`, `history.restore` / `history.purge` (already Enter / Delete inside the Trash view), `output.pdfRough`, `sync.now`. (3) The F1 sheet is generated, never hand-written, except `CONTEXT_KEY_HINTS` (a short hand-kept list that mirrors `KEYBOARD_CELL_RULES.md`; update both together).
**Not run / standing gaps:** a real keyboard-only walk through every page in the Electron window (tab order, visible focus ring, focus after navigation, caret behaviour, scrolling of the sheet); screen-reader checks (not in scope); the Phase 66 e2e suite (never executed).
**Not done / left for later:** rebinding keys (still read-only); keys for the 35 palette-only actions (see decision 2); a visible "skip to content" link.

### Phase 68 - Performance acceptance (2026-10-10)
**Contract first:** `API_CONTRACTS.md` - change-log row + "Phase 68 as built"; `PROJECT_STRUCTURE.md` updated; new `docs/reference/qa/PERF_ACCEPTANCE.md` (budgets, how to repeat on the owner's PC and on an installed build). No schema change, no migration, no IPC channel, no package. `package.json` gained script `perf:acceptance`; `tsconfig.node.json` includes the new `vitest.perf.config.ts`.
**Done:** (1) pure `shared/perf/acceptance.ts` (the 15 proposed budgets, verdict, PHASE_STATUS rows, `heapGrowthMbPerHour`) and `shared/perf/startupMarks.ts`. (2) `main/index.ts`: four start-up marks (`app_ready`, `db_open`, `window_shown`, `page_loaded`) and ONE log line `Start-up timing (ms since the process started)`; this is how the owner reads real start-up time of an installed build. (3) `src/main/perf/acceptance.perf.ts` + `vitest.perf.config.ts` = `npm run perf:acceptance`, kept out of `npm test`: seeds 5 000 bills x 12 rows and 2 000 parties into a temp SQLite file and times start-up (new / large database), History (dates, first page, deep page via cursors, search), Customers (list + stats, page filter), party autocomplete (index build, per keystroke), bill save into the large database, print HTML for 80 rows, one real headless-Chromium PDF and PNG, and a soak with forced GC (list / search / customers / autocomplete / save / print build in a loop; heap slope after warm-up). (4) The Phase 24 harness was re-run in jsdom.
**Verification actually run (scratch copy, `npm ci --ignore-scripts`):** `shared/perf/__tests__/acceptance.test.ts` x6 (budgets unique and positive, verdict edge cases, row format, heap slope incl. warm-up and too-few-samples, start-up marks). `npm run perf:acceptance` several times on Linux / Node 22 / sandbox Chromium (numbers in the Performance log above; last run with a 90 s soak = 883 cycles, JS heap 18.9 -> 18.8 MB, slope about 4.5 MB/h which is noise at that length). `npm run typecheck` (4 projects) clean. **Full `npx vitest run` = 145 files passed / 2 skipped, 1661 passed / 29 skipped, 0 failed.** `npm run build`, dev server, Electron and the e2e suite NOT run (owner instruction / no display).
**Result:** every acceptance scenario is within its proposed budget on the build sandbox. Slowest: History search over 5 000 bills, p95 about 115 ms (budget 250; it loads every bill's item text and ranks in JS, so it grows with the number of bills: roughly 4x at 20 000, and the box waits `SEARCH_DEBOUNCE_MS` before running); History first page about 10 ms; Customers list + stats about 16 ms (339 KB over IPC for 2 000 customers); Customers filter and party autocomplete about 11 ms p95 per keystroke in Node; saving into a 5 000-bill database about 8 ms; opening a 9 MB database about 10 ms; PDF and PNG about 0.85 s each including a cold browser start. Both lists were already virtualised (Phases 41 / 45), so only the visible rows exist in the DOM.
**Phase 24 harness re-run vs the Phase 24 baseline:** no regression after 43 more phases. Render counts are identical (hold Down / Tab: grid 0 / rows 0 / cells 0; paste 100 rows: grid 2 / rows 44 / cells 305; undo storm: cells 240), and the jsdom times are equal or lower. **The one exception carried from Phase 24 is unchanged: switching Non-GST / GST re-renders every row's cells (20 grids / 2 080 rows / 15 600 cells per 20 switches; jsdom p95 about 247 ms).** It is by design (the column set changes), it is a once-in-a-while action rather than a keystroke, jsdom is far slower than Chromium, and no fix was attempted without a real browser to measure against. **Exception explained to the owner, not fixed.** If a real window shows a visible pause on Alt+1 / Alt+2, the single place to look is the per-row memoisation keyed on the column set.
**Decisions (owner may flip):** (1) The budgets are MINE (the owner never gave numbers for these scenarios); change them in `shared/perf/acceptance.ts`. (2) The acceptance run is not part of `npm test` (seeding 5 000 bills and soaking are too slow and machine-dependent for every run); it never fails on speed, only on a wrong result. (3) The harness page `/dev/perf` is reachable in release builds too (palette "Open perf harness (dev)"); it was left in on purpose so the real-paint numbers can be taken on the installed app. (4) Start-up timing is a log line, not a screen. (5) History search was not rewritten (no SQL / index change): within budget at 5 000 bills.
**Not run / standing gaps (all owed to the owner's PC):** (a) **ANY measurement on a release build** (`npm run dist` / the installer was not built) and on Windows hardware; (b) real start-up time (the log line has never been written by a real run; `window_shown` vs the 3 000 ms budget is unconfirmed); (c) the real keystroke-to-paint numbers of the Phase 24 harness (still never run in Chromium); (d) PDF and image time inside Electron (`printToPDF`, the capture window and the clipboard write were not run; the sandbox numbers are the Chromium command line); (e) **the one-hour soak was NOT run** (longest here: 90 s) and renderer / Chromium memory was not measured at all; the soak covers Node-side work only; (f) History and Customers page rendering with the big lists in a real window (jsdom has no layout, so the virtual window was not exercised at 5 000 / 2 000 rows; only the data side was); (g) a hand-made database from years of use (the seed is synthetic: 12 rows per bill, 400 days).
**Not done / left for later:** a Settings or Dashboard "Start-up time" display; an in-app memory readout; an automatic one-hour soak driver for the renderer (the manual steps are in `PERF_ACCEPTANCE.md` section 4); any change to History search (it would only be needed far beyond 5 000 bills).

### Phase 70 - Create fresh-bill fix + header border alignment (2026-10-10)
**Contract first:** no schema, IPC or shared-function change. `API_CONTRACTS.md` gained a short "Phase 70 as built" note; `PROJECT_STRUCTURE.md` line for `create/` updated.
**Done:** (1) `stores/createSession.ts`: new `endSessionAfterSave()` (clears the module-level session id only - no reload, no navigation) and a module-level `useBillStore.subscribe` that re-points the session at the open bill as soon as it is dirty again. (2) `stores/saveActions.ts`: calls `endSessionAfterSave()` after a successful save (after `markSaved`). Effect: after any successful Save, the next time Create mounts (Dashboard, rail, Esc then back) it opens a BLANK bill; the bill on screen at the moment of saving is untouched, so Ctrl+S then keep typing still works and the next save still sends `expectedRevision`. An unsaved (dirty, never or since-saved) bill keeps resuming as before. (3) `components/create/BottomBar.tsx`: a visible "New bill" button (FilePlus, Ctrl+N tooltip, `requestLeave(startFreshBill)` so a dirty bill asks first) next to Save. No new shortcut: `bill.new` already exists in the registry and palette. (4) `styles/grid.css`: header cells and body cells already shared one `grid-template-columns` variable and one cell rule; the found defect is that a later bare `.q3-gh__cell { position: relative }` (equal specificity) overrode the sticky rule, so the HEADER's Sl.No / Item Name cells were not sticky while the body's were - dividers drifted apart as soon as the grid scrolled sideways. It now excludes `slNo`/`name`. Also added `box-sizing: border-box` to the shared cell rule so both use one box model.
**Existing test changed (justified):** `unsavedGuard.test.ts` "a saved bill with new edits says Save changes?" assumed Create resumes the saved bill after leaving; Phase 70 deliberately reverses that. It now asserts a blank bill on return, and a new test covers "edit again right after saving says Save changes?".
**Tests written (NOT RUN):** `components/create/__tests__/unsavedGuard.test.ts` (new describe "Phase 70", 5 tests); `components/grid/__tests__/headerGeometry.test.ts` (4 source-level checks: one shared template, one shared cell rule, no sticky override, template derivation); `tests-e2e/08-create-fresh-header-align.e2e.ts` (header right edge vs body right edge per column, |dx| <= 0.5 px, also after scrolling right; blank-after-save; resume; New button). The CSS assertions in the geometry test were checked with a plain Node regex run against the real file; nothing else was executed.
**Assumptions [A] (from MILESTONE8_DESIGN):** an unsaved bill is resumable and New starts fresh; "After a successful Save the Create session clears" is applied to every successful save, including saving an edited existing bill.
**Not run / standing gaps:** no `npm install`, typecheck, vitest, build, dev server or Playwright in this session. The pixel-alignment e2e needs a real window. The header-misalignment diagnosis is from reading CSS only: if the owner still sees a 1px offset on a STATIC (unscrolled) grid, the next suspect is sub-pixel snapping of the `1fr` Item Name column between the sticky header layer and the body rows (fix would be integer column widths computed in JS in `gridTemplateColumns`).
**Next:** Phase 71 (warm render window + instant image copy).

### Phase 71 - Warm render window + instant image copy (2026-10-10)
**Contract first:** `API_CONTRACTS.md` "Phase 71 as built" (new channel `image:prerender`, `ImageCopyResponse.cached?`). No schema, migration or package change.
**Done:** (1) `main/services/imageService.ts`: the work is split into `render()` (resolve -> cache lookup -> open/measure/capture) and the clipboard write. New content-hash cache keyed by `imageCacheKey(html, paper)` (sha256 of the exact HTML + paper, so bill content, mode, settings and PO number are all in the key), LRU, max `IMAGE_CACHE_MAX_ENTRIES` = 4 and `IMAGE_CACHE_MAX_BYTES` = 48 MB. `copy()` on a hit opens no window and captures nothing; it only writes the clipboard. New `prerender()` (silent: render failure resolves, busy skips), `warm()`, `dispose()`. A copy that arrives while a pre-render runs waits for it (then hits the cache) instead of being refused; two copies at once are still refused. Failed renders are never cached. The body of `createImageService` was restructured in one edit (a rewrite of that function, not the file); behaviour of the old tests is kept except `copy` now also returns `cached`. (2) `main/print/electronImagePort.ts`: ONE pooled hidden window (`backgroundThrottling:false`): `open` takes it (or builds one), `close` wipes the page with `about:blank` and returns it to the pool; a window that failed to load is destroyed, not pooled; `warm()` builds it ahead (called 1.5 s after start-up from `main/index.ts`), `dispose()` on `will-quit`. `writePng` now uses `clipboard.writeImage(nativeImage.createFromBuffer(...))` straight from main instead of the web `ClipboardItem`. (3) IPC/bridge: `image:prerender` (same strict input as `image:copy`), preload `image.prerender`, `Quot3Bridge` type. (4) Renderer: `stores/imagePrerender.ts` - after the bill has not changed for 1.5 s the open bill is sent via an idle callback; only the LAST-USED mode (default rough) is pre-rendered, an unchanged payload is not re-sent; mounted by `pages/Create.tsx`; `copyImageOfBill` records the mode.
**Tests written (NOT RUN):** `main/services/__tests__/imageService.test.ts` (+9 Phase 71 tests: prerender fills cache and leaves the clipboard alone, hit path opens/captures nothing, changed HTML re-renders, key stability, LRU eviction, failures not cached, prerender silent/skips when busy, copy waits for pre-render, warm/dispose, a hit does zero port work and is under 50 ms with the fake port); `main/ipc/__tests__/image.test.ts` (+1, existing two updated for the new service shape); `renderer/stores/__tests__/imagePrerender.test.ts` (4 tests: payload/mode/dedupe, no bridge/no content, debounce gives one request, rejection allows retry).
**Assumptions / decisions (owner may flip):** (a) only one mode is pre-rendered (the last used) to keep an edit pause to one render; (b) pre-render also runs for unsaved bills with content; (c) cache is in main memory only, cleared on quit; (d) the 1.5 s idle delay and cache limits are constants in the two files above. Phase 72 will change scale/quality; the cache key already follows the HTML, so Phase 72 must add the scale setting to the key (noted for it).
**Not run / standing gaps:** the Electron half (pooled window reuse, `about:blank` wipe, a second copy after the pool is warm, `clipboard.writeImage` paste into WhatsApp/Paint) was never executed; the owner must measure real click-to-clipboard time cold vs warm vs cache hit (e.g. with `QUOT3_IMAGE_DEBUG=1` and a stopwatch) and confirm a paste still works. The "timing test" in the unit suite measures only our own overhead with a fake port. Risk to watch: a window reused after `enableDeviceEmulation` - `disableDeviceEmulation` is called both after capture and when pooling.
**Next:** Phase 72 (HD image quality: `image.scale` 4-5x, verify the scale really applies, respect `IMAGE_MAX_PX`).

### Phase 72 - HD image quality (2026-10-10)
**Contract first:** `API_CONTRACTS.md` "Phase 72 as built" (new setting key `image.scale`, new channel `appSettings:saveImage`, `AppSettingsState.image`, `ImageCopyResponse.scale/scaleApplied`). No schema or migration (settings table), no package.
**Done:** (1) `shared/print/imageFlow.ts`: `IMAGE_SCALE_RANGE` {2..5, default 4}, `readImageScale` (tolerant), `validateImageScale` (strict, refuses with a message), `imageScalesFor(want)` (wanted scale then each whole step down to 2), `IMAGE_SCALES` is now `[4,3,2]` (was `[3,2]`), `verifyScale(png, plan)` (real PNG width vs rect width x scale, 1 px tolerance, also returns the effective scale). `IMAGE_MAX_PX` (16,000) and `planCapture` are unchanged: the largest scale in the list that fits wins, a bill too long even at 2x is still refused with the existing message (never a silent downscale). (2) `main/services/imageService.ts`: reads `getScale()` on every render (Settings change applies to the next copy), plans with `imageScalesFor(want)`, then MEASURES the output: if the PNG width is not rect x scale it captures once more, and if it is still wrong it keeps the image, logs `Image scale did not apply` (wanted / expected / actual / effective) and reports `scaleApplied:false` - a copy never fails because of this. Result carries `scale` and `scaleApplied`. Cache key now includes the wanted scale (Phase 71's note honoured). (3) Settings: `image: { scale }` in `AppSettingsState`, `appSettings:saveImage` (zod `{scale:number}`, re-validated in the service), preload + bridge type, `settingsStore.saveImage`, and an "Image quality" form (select 2x small / 3x good / 4x HD default / 5x maximum) under the Print tab (`ImageQualitySection`, no new tab or shortcut). `main/index.ts` wires `getScale: () => readImageScale(settings.getAll())`. (4) Harness fix: `outputHarness.ts` hard-coded `deviceScaleFactor: 3`; it now plans from a 1x probe page and captures in a context at exactly `plan.scale`, and `imageCapture.test.ts` asserts `verifyScale(...).ok` on real Chromium PNGs.
**Existing tests changed (justified):** `imageFlow.test.ts` default scale 3 -> 4; `imageService.test.ts` fake capture now returns a PNG whose width follows the plan (so the new verification passes) and expected sizes became 2096 x 3296 (524 x 824 css px at 4x); `appSettingsService.test.ts` first-read test also expects `image:{scale:4}`; `settingsPage.test.ts` fixture gained `image` and `saveImage`.
**Tests written (NOT RUN):** `imageFlow.test.ts` (+5: range/default/fallback list, tolerant read, strict validate, 5x fall-back to 4x, verifyScale), `imageService.test.ts` (+7: default 4x width, 2/3/5x, long bill steps down and says which, too long even at 2x refused, wrong width retried once then flagged + warned, wrong-then-right accepted silently, cache key follows the scale), `appSettingsService.test.ts` (+1 image save/refuse), `settingsPage.test.ts` (+1 select shows 4, options 2-5, saves 5), `imageCapture.test.ts` (+1 real-Chromium assertion per scenario, skipped when Chromium is absent).
**Assumptions / decisions (owner may flip):** (a) whole numbers 2-5 only, default 4 (the plan said "4-5x"; 2 and 3 are offered as smaller-file choices); (b) the control lives on the Print tab rather than a new tab; (c) a long bill silently uses a lower step (the toast shows the real pixel size, and `scale` is in the response) - it does not warn; (d) the cache is keyed by the WANTED scale, not the fallback one.
**Not run / standing gaps:** no code ran this session. Not confirmed: that Electron's `enableDeviceEmulation` really yields rect x scale pixels at 4x/5x on the owner's Windows PC (this is exactly what `verifyScale` + the log line will reveal: look for `Image scale did not apply` in the logs folder), file size / memory at 5x on a long bill, copy time at 4-5x vs the old 3x (a bigger PNG is slower to capture and to put on the clipboard; Phase 71's cache hides it only for a repeated copy), and how the picture looks pasted into WhatsApp (owner eyeball, standing gap per the phase entry).
**Next:** Phase 73 (print dialog opens instantly from the pure paper engine; top-aligned print).

### Phase 73 - Print dialog instant + top-aligned print (2026-10-10)
**Contract first:** `API_CONTRACTS.md` "Phase 73 as built" (new channel `print:decideQuick`, `provisional?` on the decide response). No schema, no package.
**Done (instant dialog):** The slow part of opening the dialog was `print:decide`: it renders the bill, loads it in a hidden window and measures (once for A5, up to 5 times for the A4 density fit) before the dialog could show anything. Now `printService.decideQuick` returns the pure engine's `decideForBill` answer + settings without touching the port (`resolve(req, quick)` returns right after the first render), exposed as `print:decideQuick` (zod = same strict input as decide) + preload + optional bridge method. `printActions.decide()` calls quick first (dialog shows size, reasons, warnings, preview and enables Print/PDF; header text "Checking that the text fits..." while `measuring`), then the existing `print:decide`, which replaces the answer (A5 -> A4 on measured overflow, density tier). If the measured call fails, the instant answer stays with `measureFailed: true` (no error banner). Print / Save PDF still re-resolve with measurement in main, so a click during the measuring window cannot print an unchecked A5. `autoPdf` (Save PDF action) waits for the measured answer. Superseded requests are ignored by the existing `seq` guard.
**Done (top-aligned):** Diagnosis (source level, NOT seen on paper): the template cannot centre vertically - `.q3p-sheet` only has `margin:0 auto` (horizontal), body has no flex/grid/height, and the only top gap is the `@page` margin (A5/A4 constants). `electronPrintPort.print` passes `margins: none` + `pageSize`, so Chromium adds nothing. A vertical shift on the owner's printer is therefore most likely the printer driver / Windows page setup (e.g. "centre on page", paper-size mapping, scaling "fit to page", or the printer's unprintable-area offset), not the template. I made the CSS explicit anyway (`body{display:block;height:auto;min-height:0}`, `.q3p-sheet{position:static;padding:0}`) so no default can shift it, and added a test that fails if vertical centring / top offsets are ever introduced. No driver workaround was invented.
**Tests written (NOT RUN):** `printService.test.ts` (+3: quick never measures and is provisional, no density fit, same settings as the measured answer), `ipc/print.test.ts` (+1: decideQuick validates and calls only decideQuick), `renderHtml.test.ts` (+1: top-aligned CSS guard for A5/A4), `printDialog.test.ts` (+2: instant answer shown with Print enabled while measured decide pending, then refined; failing measured decide keeps the instant answer).
**Assumptions / decisions (owner may flip):** (a) two IPC calls rather than one streaming call (simple, keeps `print:decide` untouched and existing tests valid); (b) the instant answer never shows the density fit, so an A4 bill's preview may change type size slightly once measured; (c) Print stays enabled while measuring because main re-measures on print.
**Not run / standing gaps:** dialog open time not measured (expected: one IPC round trip + one render, no hidden window); real printer top alignment not confirmed. To settle the driver question on the owner's PC: print the same bill via "Save PDF" (template only, no driver) and compare with paper; if the PDF is top-aligned and paper is not, it is the printer's page setup (turn off centring / set paper to A4/A5 with "actual size").

### Phase 80 - Extra free cells, grid UI (2026-10-10)
**Contract first:** `API_CONTRACTS.md` "Phase 80 as built". No schema, setting, IPC or package.
**Done:** (1) New pure `shared/freeCells.ts`: virtual row ids (`fx:<footerKey>` for the Items total / Adjustments total / Final total (or single Total) row, `tr:<n>` for a tail row), `freeNavIds` (display-order navigation ids), `getFreeCell`, `applyFreeCellTexts` / `putFreeCell(s)` (minimal state: empty cells dropped, empty footer keys dropped, trailing empty tail rows trimmed), `toggleFreeMark`, `stripFreeColumn`, `freeColumnHasData`, `displayedTailCount`. (2) Store: `setFreeCells(cells)` (ONE undo entry, coalesces per cell while typing), `toggleMark` accepts free ids, `removeExtraCol` also strips the column's free cells (undo restores them), `requestRemoveExtraCol` asks for confirmation when free cells hold data. (3) UI: new `grid/FreeCells.tsx` (`FreeCell`, read-only cells, `FreeTotalRow`, `TailRows`); `FooterRows` renders the total rows via `FreeTotalRow` when extra columns exist; `BillGrid` mounts `TailRows` and counts them in `aria-rowcount`; `gridUiStore.tailShown` (UI-only minimum of shown tail rows). (4) `gridController`: one `navIds()` (store `view.navIds` unchanged; with extra columns it adds the `fx:` / `tr:` rows in display order: body, Items total, adjustments, Adjustments total, Final total, tail rows); commit / clear / cut / copy / paste / Mark / undo-restore route free rows to `setFreeCells`; a paste that STARTS on a free row (`pasteFree`) flows down the free rows and grows the tail, only extra columns take text, skipped cells are counted in the status line; Enter / Tab past the last tail row opens another tail row (display only until it holds text); `freezeTail()` keeps shown tail rows on screen while a write trims stored ones. (5) Units: `summarizeUnits(rows, extraCols, free?)` where `free = { footerExtras, tailRows, footerKinds? }`; the cells are taken in DISPLAY order (item rows, Items total, adjustment rows, Adjustments total, Final total, tail rows) so a segment continues through them; a marked free cell starts its own segment (`rowId` = the free row id); without free cells the old array order is kept exactly. `UnitsPopup` passes the free cells and only the footer rows actually shown.
**Tests written (NOT RUN):** `shared/__tests__/freeCells.test.ts` (ids, nav order, edit / trim / mark / strip, units continuation incl. hidden footer rows and adjustment display order), `grid/__tests__/freeCells.test.ts` (jsdom: structure, typing + Enter + undo, read-only cells, tail growth, trimming, paste down the tail, units popup, column removal). **Existing tests adjusted** because bills with extra columns now have more navigable rows: `billGrid.render.test.ts` (body-row filter ignores `fx:` / `tr:` ids), `adjustments.test.ts` (mounts the bill without extra columns), `gridEditing.test.ts` (the "Tab past the last cell creates a trailing row" case uses a bill without extra columns). Other tests that run keyboard flows to the very end of a bill WITH extra columns (gridUndo, undoUi, extraCols, gridClipboard, richtextUi) were not re-read; if one fails it is probably this behaviour change, not a bug.
**Behaviour change (deliberate, owner may flip):** with extra columns, Down / Enter / Tab past the last body row now continue into the total rows and the tail rows instead of stopping / opening a body row (the grid always keeps >= 3 blank body rows, so entry never stalls). Without extra columns nothing changes.
**Assumptions / decisions:** (a) the non-extra cells of a total row or tail row are navigable but read-only (keeps the keyboard grid rectangular; no reducer change); (b) free cells exist only while the bill has extra columns; (c) tail rows show one extra blank entry row; Enter on it opens one more (`tailShown`, reset when the bill id changes); (d) free cells of a footer row that is not currently shown (e.g. Items total after the last adjustment is removed) stay stored but are not counted in units; Phase 81 must use the same "shown rows only" rule in print / TSV / image; (e) Mark (Ctrl+M) works on free cells.
**Not run / standing gaps:** no install, typecheck, vitest or build (owner asked to skip the build; a TypeScript syntax-only transpile of all touched files passed, `shared/freeCells.ts` also passed `tsc --strict --noUncheckedIndexedAccess --exactOptionalPropertyTypes` alone). Unverified: type-checking of the controller / store / components against the real types, every new and adjusted test, real caret / focus behaviour in Chromium for the new cells, the visual look of tail rows (CSS in `grid.css`, tokens only), the grid perf harness (the render-count tests in `billGrid.render.test.ts` should be unaffected: `FreeCell` subscribes only to its own stored cell).

(2) New pure `shared/calc/percentPresets.ts` (setting key, default, validation, lenient read, `percentResults` = % of / total + % / total - %).
