**Assumptions / decisions (owner may flip):** (a) `a + b%` takes the percent of the running left value only when `b%` is the whole right-hand term, so `100 + 2*10%` = 100.2; (b) `a / b%` = a / (b/100); (c) `x` is always multiplication (so `0x10` = 0); (d) result text shows at most 10 decimals while the value keeps 28 digits, so `1/3*3` has value 0.9999999999999999999999999999 and text 1; (e) numbers must be < 1e24, results >= 1e24 are errors; (f) percent presets are stored as canonical comma text, duplicates collapsed, blank restores the default; (g) the number display uses plain digits (no Indian grouping) in `text`; the panel (Phase 83) may format it with `fmtMoney`-style grouping.

### Phase 84 - Legacy importer, pure (2026-10-10)
**Contract first:** `API_CONTRACTS.md` "Phase 84 as built". No schema, IPC, UI or package; pure code only (`shared/legacyImport/*`).
**Done:** (1) `sha256.ts`: pure SHA-256 (`sha256Hex`) and `uuidFromText` (name-based UUID, v5 layout) because `shared` may not import Node. (2) `parse.ts`: tolerant `parseLegacyFile(text, bytes)` (never throws; error rows for bad JSON / missing keys / no digits in `bill_no` / bad date / blank party / over 2 MB / over 500 items), `legacyBillNumber`, `parseLegacyDate`, `legacyDecimal`, ignored-field counters. (3) `convert.ts`: `convertLegacyBill` -> `Bill` (format lookup table, red-bold ranges -> runs clamped, `amt` kept as override only when it differs from qty x rate by > 0.01 or qty / rate are blank, adjustments as override rows, extra columns via the Phase 21 key generator, `manual_N` preferred over `extras[N]`, marked, name highlight, GSTIN line in the address, trailing blank rows dropped, deterministic row ids, `roundOff` 0). (4) `plan.ts`: `planLegacyImport(input)` -> `{ bills, newParties, report }`: totals through `serializeBill`'s totals cache (the one engine), fingerprint = SHA-256 of `normParty|ISO date|bill_no text|grand total|item count`, bill id derived from it, already-here skip (fingerprint, id, or earlier-imported party + number), same-number rules (same party = same bill saved twice, keep later date / more items / larger total / file name; different party = earliest keeps the number, others get the next free number after the highest), numbers already used here renumbered, owner `choices` per file, PO counter = max(current, highest assigned + 1), party grouping by exact normalised name (existing linked, new ones take each field from the newest bill that has it, deterministic party ids), report (errors, skipped, renumbered, clash groups, raw duplicate groups, PO range, next PO, new / existing parties, totals differences with the "left out the X adjustment" note, format counts, convert counts, ignored counts, warnings).
**Tests:** new `shared/legacyImport/__tests__/legacyImport.test.ts` (31) + helper `corpus.ts` (reads the private zip with `node:zlib`; the corpus block is skipped when the zip is absent; only counts are asserted, nothing is logged). **RAN** in an isolated vitest (temp install outside the project: vitest, decimal.js, zod): all 31 pass. Corpus numbers: 768 read / 0 errors; 172 GST + 596 non-GST (4 of them `default`); 481 adjustments (27 negative); 275 red-bold ranges; 12 marked cells; exactly 19 raw duplicate-number groups (28 clash groups after prefixes are stripped, as the spec predicted for 106 vs BR1_106); 760 bills created + 8 skipped as "saved twice"; 25 renumbered; no shared PO number; 400 totals differences; 416 new parties; second run creates 0.
**Assumptions / decisions (owner may flip):** (a) the spec text says the next PO after the owner's import is Q3-0744, but its own renumber rule puts renumbered bills after the highest number, so with this corpus the highest assigned is 768 and the next PO is **Q3-0769**; I followed the rules (spec section 11 says "highest assigned + 1"). (b) `importedRefs` (party name + `legacy_ref` of earlier imports) was added to `ExistingState`; without it a re-run would import the skipped "saved twice" copies (they have no fingerprint row). Phase 85 must pass it. (c) createdAt / updatedAt use the file's date and time written as UTC (the old app stored local time with no zone) and 12:00:00 when there is no time. (d) every red-bold range read is counted (275), even the 2 empty ones that format nothing. (e) parties and bills get deterministic ids (`uuidFromText`), so two branches converge. (f) an unknown `format_mode` imports as non-GST with a warning. (g) the legacy `total` is only used by the differences report (tolerance 0.5).
**Existing code touched:** none (new folder only). To satisfy two existing guard tests the importer asks `serializeBill` for the grand total (no direct `computeBill` call) and maps old format names with a lookup table (no `=== 'gst'`).
**Known issue carried forward (not mine):** `shared/calc/__tests__/roundOffWiring.test.ts` (Phase 77, never run before) fails 2 tests in the isolated run: `totalsJson.grandTotal` is "100.00" where the test expects "100", and `diffBills(bill(), bill(0)).identical` is false where true is expected. Probably wrong expectations (the totals cache stores 2-dp money) but the second one could be a real behaviour question; not investigated. Phase 85 must not rely on those two behaviours without checking.
**Not run / standing gaps:** no install, project typecheck, project vitest or build (owner asked to skip). **Not done (by scope):** DB / migration / transaction / backup / undo import (Phase 85), UI (Phase 86), sync (87+).

**Contract first:** `API_CONTRACTS.md` "Phase 86 as built". No schema, no migration, no package. One new event channel (`legacyImport:progress`).

(3) Pure `shared/legacyImport/uiModel.ts`: pick / progress / tag types, `previewLines`, `reportToText` / `reportToCsv`, `legacyRefScore`, `importedNote`, `clashChoiceFor`. (4) Renderer: `stores/legacyImportStore.ts` and `components/legacyImport/LegacyImportDialog.tsx` (modal in AppShell): Pick (folder / .json files, past imports with "Undo this import") -> Reading (`Reading N of M`, Cancel) -> Preview (counts, PO range, next PO, parties, totals differences, warnings, clash table with a per-file dropdown preset to the planner default; a change re-previews) -> Importing -> Report (counts, problem / skipped / renumbered / totals-differ lists, "Save report as text / CSV", "Undo this import"). Opened from the palette / `Ctrl+Shift+L` (`legacyImport.open`), the Settings side list, the Customers header and the History source row. (5) History: `BillListItem` gains optional `legacyRef` / `importBatchId`; the list shows a small "Imported" tag; `history:listDates / listBills / search` accept `source` (`all | created | imported`) and `batch`; search matches the legacy reference (`BR1_106`, `106`); `SourceFilter` row (All bills / Created here / Imported, batch picker inside Imported) and `clearFilters` resets it. (6) Create: `ImportedNote` in the party bar shows an "Imported - BR1_106" mark whose tooltip / aria-label is the full note ("Imported from the old app - old number BR1_106 - 08-10-26"); the tags live on the bill row so they survive edits.

**A real defect found and fixed during this phase:** the folder label used `path.basename`, which is platform specific, so a Windows path seen by a non-Windows process leaked whole into the label the renderer gets. It now uses a separator-agnostic last-segment helper (`lastSegment`), covered by a test that asserts no path text appears in any IPC answer.

(c) `batch` in a History query implies the Imported filter. (d) Changing a clash choice re-reads the files (a few hundred ms for the owner's folder) instead of re-planning in the renderer. (e) Closing the dialog is ignored while the write transaction runs. (f) Past imports are listed on the Pick step of the dialog (no separate Settings section). (g) Report files from "Save report" contain party and file names and go where the owner chooses; the automatic `logs/import-*.json` is unchanged.

### Phase 87 - Sync: parties on receive + relink (2026-10-11)
**Contract first:** `API_CONTRACTS.md` "Phase 87 as built". No schema, no migration, no package, no IPC.
**Done:** (1) `remoteApplyRepo`: `findPartyByNorm`, `insertParty` (`source = 'sync'`), `unlinkedBills`, `linkParty` (sets ONLY `party_id`). (2) `remoteApplier.write`: when the received bill's `partyId` is unknown here (or null) and the bill is not a delete, the party is found by exact normalised name (`normalizePartyName`, live parties only, oldest first) or created from the snapshot (name / phone / address / transport); a blank name creates nothing. (3) New `RemoteApplier.relinkParties()` (one transaction): links every live bill with `party_id IS NULL` the same way; idempotent. (4) `main/index.ts` runs it once at start-up, guarded by the sync-state flag `sync.partiesRelinked` and `dbh.writable`; a failure is logged and the flag stays unset so it retries next start.
**Tests (RAN, pass):** new `main/sync/__tests__/partiesOnReceive.test.ts` (4: two virtual branches - one party per normalised name with `source = sync`, all received bills linked, nothing enqueued back; existing local party reused; relink repairs unlinked bills, second run changes nothing, revision / updated_at untouched; blank name stays unlinked). Whole `src/main/sync` + `src/main/db` re-run: 154/154 pass. `tsc --noEmit` (node) shows no errors in the touched files.
**Assumptions / decisions:** (a) the received party id is NOT reused (parties are not synced; the link is by name only, as the spec says); (b) a bill received as a delete never creates a party; (c) relink also covers bills made on this branch with no party link (same rule); (d) the link does not bump revision or `updated_at` and is not sent out; (e) an existing party's phone / address are not updated from the incoming bill.
**Not run / standing gaps:** no `npm run build`, no Electron, no real Supabase; the start-up relink call in `main/index.ts` is compiled-checked only (not executed in a real app); Customers page count after a real pull not seen.
**Not done (by scope):** import enqueue / dedupe (88), progress UI + count RPC (89).
**Next:** Phase 88 (Sync: import to outbox + dedupe).

### Phase 88 - Sync: import to outbox + dedupe (2026-10-11)
**Contract first:** `API_CONTRACTS.md` "Phase 88 as built". No schema, no migration, no package.
**Done:** (1) `OutboxRepo.enqueueMany` / `Outbox.captureMany` (one prune, prepared statements reused, no-op while sync is off). (2) `legacyImportService.commit(source, choices, run, send = true)`: inside the import transaction, after all bills are written, `outboxMany` queues every imported bill (revision 1, upsert); the whole batch is all-or-nothing with the import. `main/index.ts` wires `outboxMany` (+ push kick). (3) The three import tags (`legacyRef`, `importBatchId`, `importFingerprint`) are now optional fields of `billRecordSchema`, selected by `billRepo.getRecord` (so the push worker sends them) and written by `remoteApplyRepo.upsertBill` (`COALESCE` on update, so a snapshot from an older branch never erases a local tag). (4) Dedupe on receive: `RemoteApplier.apply` returns `ignored` when another bill id already holds the incoming `import_fingerprint` (trashed bills included; the unique index could otherwise throw); a repeated delivery of the same version was already ignored by the equal-version rule. (5) The "Send imported bills to the other branches" switch (spec section 7, deferred from Phase 86) is on the Preview step (default on): `send` flows UI store -> bridge -> IPC (`legacyImport:commit`, optional boolean) -> service.
**Tests (RAN, pass):** new `main/sync/__tests__/importOutbox.test.ts` (3): a 1 000-file import queues 1 000 pending rows and marks the bills `pending`, pushes / pulls to a second branch (1 000 bills, all three tags present, 40 parties made by Phase 87, nothing queued back), a duplicate delivery and the same fingerprint under another bill id are both `ignored` (still 1 000 bills); `send = false` queues nothing and leaves bills `local_only`; sync off imports fine with an empty outbox. Whole `src/main`, `renderer/components/legacyImport`, `shared/sync`: 598 pass, 3 fail (the known `e2eSuite` seven-flows test and `billServiceRoundOff` x2 from Phase 77, untouched). `tsc --noEmit` node + web: no errors in touched files.
**Assumptions / decisions:** (a) the batch id travels as-is; a receiving branch has no `import_batches` row for it, so it shows the Imported tag but cannot undo or filter-pick that batch (undo is for the importing branch only); (b) the dedupe is by fingerprint at apply time only (the deterministic bill id already makes two branches' imports the same bill); (c) the existing-bills-before-activation rule is unchanged: only new imports are queued, there is no "send an earlier import" action; (d) the switch label is plain text and its checkbox is unstyled apart from one flex class.
**Not run / standing gaps:** no `npm run build`, no Electron, no real Supabase; the new switch's look is unseen; an old branch (without Phase 88) strips the three tags, so update all branches before importing; the new switch has no jsdom test (store path only).
**Not done (by scope):** progress events / bar and `qt_pending_count` RPC (89).
**Next:** Phase 89 (Sync progress UI + count RPC).

### Phase 89 - Sync progress UI + count RPC (2026-10-11)
**Contract first:** `API_CONTRACTS.md` "Phase 89 as built". No schema, no package.
**Done:** (1) `supabase/sql/06_pending_count.sql`: `qt_pending_count(p_license, p_branch_id) -> { pending }` (same `qt_auth` check as `qt_pull`, grants to anon / authenticated); listed in `docs/SUPABASE_MIGRATIONS.md`. (2) `SyncTransport.pendingCount` implemented by `SupabaseTransport` (RPC `qt_pending_count`, zod-checked), `InMemoryTransport` and `FlakyTransport`. (3) Pure `shared/sync/progress.ts` (`SyncProgress`, `progressLabel`, `progressPercent`, `createProgressTracker`, `isProgress`, `PROGRESS_MIN_TOTAL = 3`). (4) Push worker: optional `progress` dep; total = outbox pending at the start of the run, `done` = rows sent; closing event `active: false` in `finally`. Pull worker: optional `progress` dep; total = `pendingCount` after the first ack flush (a failing count call other than offline / license is ignored: the bar then grows with what arrives, so a server without Phase 89's SQL still works); one step per row handled. (5) Event channel `sync:progress` (whitelisted in `EVENT_CHANNELS`), emitted from `main/index.ts`. (6) Renderer: `syncStatusStore` holds `sending` / `receiving`; `SyncProgressBars` (a `<progress>` and "Sending 120 of 768" / "Receiving 300 of 768") in the Sync status panel; the Dashboard chip shows the same text while a run is going (only for totals of 3 or more).
**Tests (RAN, pass):** `shared/sync/__tests__/progress.test.ts` (4); `main/sync/__tests__/progress.test.ts` (4: 450-bill send and receive with the count taken from the server, empty run emits nothing, server without the count function still receives, offline while counting pauses without losing rows); three `pendingCount` tests appended to `transport.test.ts` (mocked HTTP + in memory). Full `src/main`, `src/shared`, `src/renderer` run: 1976 pass / 8 fail, all known and not from this phase (Phase 77 round-off x5 incl. `roundOffWiring`, `freeCellsModel` diffBills, Rough A5 snapshot, `e2eSuite` seven-flows, and the perf-harness "typing re-renders no rows" test that fails under load). `tsc --noEmit` node + web: no errors in touched files.
**Assumptions / decisions:** (a) progress counts bills, not bytes; (b) the sender's total is the outbox size when the run starts (the run is capped at `PUSH_MAX_BATCHES_PER_RUN`, so a very large queue shows several runs one after another, each starting at 0); (c) no bar for fewer than 3 changes; (d) no jsdom test for the bars / chip (store path and pure model are tested).
**Not run / standing gaps:** `06_pending_count.sql` was NOT run against a real Supabase project (owner: run it after `05`, then `select public.qt_pending_count('<license>', '<branch uuid>');` and expect `{"pending": N}`; add to the live checklist); the look of the bar and chip is unseen; no build / Electron.

### Phase 91 - Milestone 8 QA gate + perf re-run + docs sweep (2026-10-11)
**What was run (all RAN this session):** `npm ci --ignore-scripts` (better-sqlite3 loads from its shipped linux-x64 prebuild), full `vitest run` (172 files), `npm run typecheck` (node + web + integration + e2e projects), `npm run perf:acceptance` (5 s soak), the Phase 24 perf harness (inside the suite). **REAL DEFECT FOUND AND FIXED (Phase 71, would have broken the headline feature):** `electronImagePort.writePng` called `clipboard.writeImage(...)`, which does not exist in Electron 44 (its Clipboard has only `read / readText / write / writeText / clear`; the type check failed). "Copy as image" would have thrown a TypeError on every use. Now writes one `image/png` `ClipboardItem` via `clipboard.write` (the API the grid copy already uses). **Never executed in Electron: the owner must paste a copied image into WhatsApp / Paint once.**
**Test-only corrections (the code was right, the tests were wrong or stale):** Phase 77 round-off totals are stored as 2-decimal money (`'100.00'`, not `'100'`) - 3 tests; Phase 77 / 79 `diffBills` identical-checks built two bills with different generated row ids - 2 tests; `supabaseSql` now lists `06_pending_count.sql`; `e2eSuite` lists the Phase 70 eighth flow; F1 sheet palette-only count 36 (Phase 90 `settings.fresh`); Rough A5 adjustment snapshot regenerated for the deliberate Phase 73 top-align CSS (reviewed: only the `body` and `.q3p-sheet` rules changed); perf harness `typeName` no longer inserts a character after a keydown the controller already handled (it typed every letter twice - harness artefact, not a grid bug); type-only fixes in six test files and `/// <reference types="node" />` in two renderer tests.

### Phase 92 - Fix Settings Sync / License pages (render loop) - DONE 2026-10-11
**Cause (from code reading, matches the owner's diagnostics):** `BootstrapPanel.tsx` used `useBackupStore((s) => s.list.filter(...))`, a new array per store read, so React's external-store check looped ('Maximum update depth exceeded'). `BootstrapPanel` is rendered under Settings > Sync (the License controls live in `SyncSection`, same page), so the whole page crashed.
**Fix:** select the stable `s.list`, derive `imported` with `useMemo` (one edit, `components/settings/BootstrapPanel.tsx`).
**Audit:** grepped every `use*Store(` selector in `src/renderer` (single-line and destructured `useXStore()` forms). `BootstrapPanel` was the only fresh-array/object selector; the `GridCell` `.find(...)` / scalar selectors return existing references or primitives. Whole-store destructuring (`const {a,b} = useXStore()`) is stable (returns the state object) but re-renders on any change; left alone, out of scope.
**Tests:** new `renderer/components/settings/__tests__/phase92Selectors.test.ts` (13): a lint-style scan of `src/renderer` that fails on any `use*Store((s) => ...)` selector using filter/map/slice/sort/spread/object-or-array literal/`?? []`; `BootstrapPanel` mounted with imported + auto backups (no console error, only imported shown); `Settings` mounted for sync, company, branch, print, behaviour, appearance, shortcuts, audit, updates, fresh, about with the bridge mocked, failing on 'Maximum update depth' / getSnapshot errors. Confirmed the tests FAIL (3) with the old selector restored and pass with the fix.

No new columns or features.

History + Customers suites: 71 passed.
**Verification actually run:** `tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.web.json` 0 errors; `vitest run src/renderer/components/history src/renderer/components/customers` 71 passed. **Not run:** full suite, other tsconfig projects, build, dev server, Electron.

### Phase 94 - Page navigation model: Dashboard is the root - DONE 2026-10-11
**Before:** `shared/routes.ts` kept a 50-deep stack; every `navigate` pushed and Esc popped one entry, so Esc walked the whole visit history.
**Now (all in place edits):** `navPush(s, path, opts?)` keeps the stack at most `[dashboard, page]`; navigating to the Dashboard resets it to `[dashboard]`; `opts.returnToCurrent` (only honoured when the current page is History or Customers) gives `[dashboard, history|customers, create]` so Esc from a bill opened there returns to it, then to the Dashboard. `navBack` is a no-op on the Dashboard. `initialNav('/settings')` etc. seed `[dashboard, page]`, so dev routes also Esc to the Dashboard. `NAV_STACK_MAX` 50 -> 3 (safety cap). `routerStore.navigate(path, opts?)` passes the option; `openBillForEdit` (History/Customers open) passes `returnToCurrent: true`. The Create leave-guard in `routerStore` is untouched (still fires when leaving Create, now typically toward the Dashboard).
**Not changed [A]:** frozen shortcut registry/labels (`nav.back` still Escape/'Back'), so no frozen shortcut test was edited; the F1 sheet is registry-generated so it needs no hint edit. Restore-revision and draft-recovery still `navigate(create)` plainly, so Esc from those goes to the Dashboard (assumption).
**Tests:** `shared/__tests__/routes.test.ts` rewritten for the new model (every page one step to Dashboard, seed, hop no-growth, bill-from-History/Customers return, option ignored elsewhere, Dashboard reset, Esc x20 from every route ends at Dashboard, cap).
**Verification actually run:** a Node `--experimental-strip-types` script exercising `routes.ts` directly (the same assertions) passed. **Not run:** Vitest (no node_modules in this session; skipped install/build per owner), `npm run typecheck`, the History/Customers/Dashboard suites (their `setState({nav:{stack:[dashboard, X]}})` fixtures already match the new model, by reading), Electron.
**Standing gap:** real Esc behaviour in a window (including the Create guard then Dashboard) is unconfirmed; run `npm test` + `npm run typecheck` locally first.

### Phase 95 - Esc guard: no stale 'already done' popups - DONE 2026-10-11
**Reproduce-first note:** the sandbox has no window, so these are source-derived repro paths (the owner's exact popup/page was not given - [A], please confirm which popup you saw). Suspected causes found by reading `guardStore.ts`, `useHotkeys.ts`, `Dialog.tsx`: (1) **held Esc**: auto-repeat keydowns - the first opens Save/Discard/Cancel, the next closes it (Dialog Esc = Cancel), the next re-opens it from the window handler => popups flicker for actions already answered; (2) **Cancel while a Save was in flight**: Esc closed the dialog but the pending leave was lost, then the Save finished silently, so a later Esc asked again; (3) **Discard left `dirty = true`** on the store (only `billId` was nulled), a stale flag that `updateStore`, drafts and any later check read; (4) a second request while the dialog was open was handled by an ad-hoc check inside `requestLeave`.
**Changes (in place):** `shared/dirtyGuard.ts` - new pure `leaveDecision({billId, dirty, guardOpen})` -> `proceed | ask | ignore` and `ignoreEscRepeat`; `needsPrompt` kept. `stores/guardStore.ts` - `requestLeave` and `isBillDirty` use `leaveDecision`; `chooseDiscard` marks the discarded bill clean before dropping it and is ignored mid-save; `chooseCancel` is ignored mid-save. `hooks/useHotkeys.ts` - a repeated Escape is swallowed (only a fresh press navigates / asks).
**Rule now:** ask only when a bill is open AND differs from its saved state; never after Save (clean), after Discard (no bill), for a fresh/clean bill, or while the dialog is already open. With Phase 94 the Create leave-guard fires once, on the way to the Dashboard (or back to History/Customers).
**Tests:** `shared/__tests__/dirtyGuard.test.ts` - state table (fresh / saved / discarded / discarded-with-stale-flag / dirty / resumed / dialog-open / answered) and held-Esc cases.
**Verification actually run:** Node `--experimental-strip-types` script on `leaveDecision` / `ignoreEscRepeat` passed. **Not run:** Vitest (no node_modules; install/build skipped per owner), typecheck, existing guard/Create suites (read-only check: none assert the removed behaviours, but unconfirmed).
**Standing gap:** actual popups in a window; if the owner still sees a stale popup, send which page and which dialog text.

### Phase 97 - Theme-aware scrollbars - DONE 2026-10-11
**Change:** one global block appended to `styles/ui.css`: `*::-webkit-scrollbar` (size, track), `-thumb` (token colour, fully rounded, 2px track-coloured border + `background-clip` so the thumb looks thin and floats), `:hover`/`:active`, `-corner`. Applies to every scroller (grid, History, lists, dialogs, Settings, shell) so no per-component edits and every current/future theme restyles automatically. Chromium (Electron) ignores `::-webkit-scrollbar` when `scrollbar-color`/`scrollbar-width` are set, so those standard properties sit inside `@supports not selector(::-webkit-scrollbar)` as a fallback only (deviation from "plus", on purpose - otherwise the rounded style would be lost). Thin + rounded is [A]; size is `--scrollbar-size` (10px default, clamped 6-16px).
**Perf:** purely paint styling on the scrollbar; no layout/JS change; the grid scroll harness was NOT re-run (no runnable app here) - standing gap.
**Test:** new `src/__tests__/scrollbarTheme.test.ts` (4): rules use the tokens, thumb rounded, no literal colours in any stylesheet scrollbar rule, both built-ins define the tokens. Existing hex-literal test already scans ui.css.
**Not run:** Vitest, typecheck, window. **Standing gap:** real look/size of the bars on Windows and grid scroll perf unconfirmed.

**Non-neumorphic:** the block overrides `--shadow-raised/-inset/-pressed` (otherwise derived from the paired light/dark offsets): raised = 1px cyan ring + outer dark glow (outer only); inset = 1px dark ring + inner shade (inner only); pressed = 2px cyan inner ring. **Background:** `--bg-image` is six url-free radial gradients (4 faint stars, 2 nebula glows) painted behind `.q3-shell` (Phase 99 wiring). `--glass-blur` stays 0 (no blur in Space); Glass-only chrome rules are not applied.

### Phase 104 - Extra columns: Down key extends the rows - DONE 2026-10-11
**Cause:** with extra columns the grid always shows one blank entry tail row below the last stored tail row (`displayedTailCount`), but the reducer's plain Down was `clamp(row + 1, 0, lastRow)`, so Down on that bottom row did nothing (only Enter / Tab / Right opened another row).
**Change:** `GridShape` gained optional `isExtraCol?(c)`; in `navReduce`, plain Down (no Shift/Ctrl/Alt) on `row >= lastRow` in an extra column now emits `ENSURE_ROW {index: rowCount}` then `MOVE` into it (same effect pair Enter already uses; arrives Append, caret at 0 like Enter). The grid controller supplies `isExtraCol` from `GridColumn.isExtra`; its existing `ENSURE_ROW` handler raises `tailShown` by one. Every other column, Shift/Ctrl/Alt+Down, Up, and smooth arrows ON vs OFF are unchanged (Down is independent of `leftUnlockPresses`).
**Decisions [A]:** (1) The added row is display-only (UI `tailShown`), exactly like Enter's existing behaviour, so it creates no history entry by itself; the content typed into it is the one undo step (the cell commit), and an empty one is never stored (`minimal()` trims trailing empties on every write, Phase 79) and never counts toward paper rows (Phase 81). I did not add a separate "add row" undo entry because there is no stored state to undo; if the owner wants literal one-undo-per-added-row, that needs a stored empty row, which conflicts with the trim rule. (2) Down from the last item row goes into the footer free rows (`fx:itemsTotal` ...) when extra columns exist - this is how `freeNavIds` already ordered rows; now pinned by a test. In non-extra columns those footer rows are navigable but not typable (unchanged).
**Tests:** `nav/__tests__/cellNav.test.ts` "Phase 104" block (extra vs normal column x last vs middle row x smooth ON/OFF, Free-mode caret, no `isExtraCol` = never grows, Shift/Ctrl/Alt+Down and Up unchanged); `__tests__/freeCells.test.ts` "Phase 104" block (Down into footer extras, bottom row is always a tail row, one more shown row adds exactly one, empty added row not stored).
**Docs:** `KEYBOARD_CELL_RULES.md` section 12 marked implemented; `API_CONTRACTS.md` `GridShape.isExtraCol?`.
**Not run:** Vitest, typecheck, render-count harness, window, build. **Standing gaps:** real-window feel of Down in an extra column (caret in the new row, scroll-into-view of the new bottom row), the render-count harness not re-run (the change is in the reducer + one shape callback, no render-path change), undo of text typed into an added row not exercised in a store test.

### Phase 105 - HD image scale actually applies - DONE 2026-10-11
**Diagnosis [A, not reproducible here]:** the owner's log (wanted 4x, expected 2160, got 675, effective 1.25) equals the Windows display scale, so the render stayed at the screen's device scale factor: `webContents.enableDeviceEmulation({deviceScaleFactor})` followed by `capturePage` on a hidden (`show:false`) window does not change the captured surface's scale on that setup (capturePage returns the compositor surface at the display DSF). I could not test on Windows; the cause is inferred from the numbers, not observed.
**Fix:** `main/print/electronImagePort.ts` `capture()` now goes through the DevTools protocol first (`captureViaCdp`): `webContents.debugger.attach` -> `Emulation.setDeviceMetricsOverride` (width/height = plan view size, `deviceScaleFactor` = plan scale) -> two animation frames -> `Page.captureScreenshot` (png, `fromSurface`, `clip` = content rect, clip scale 1). The PNG width is measured (`verifyScale`); if wrong, ONE retry with a calibrated clip scale (`nextClipScale` = current x expected/actual, bounded 0.25-4). The override is cleared and the debugger detached in `finally` (a pooled window is left clean). If CDP is unavailable or still wrong, the old `enableDeviceEmulation` + `capturePage` path runs as the fallback and the closer-to-expected PNG is returned. The service's verify-by-measuring, retry, "Image scale did not apply" warning and `IMAGE_MAX_PX` are untouched; layout, crop and padding rules unchanged.
**Pure (tested):** `shared/print/imageFlow.ts` `cdpCaptureParams(plan, clipScale)`, `nextClipScale(current, check)`, `CLIP_SCALE_LIMITS`; tests in `imageFlow.test.ts` "Phase 105" block (parameters, owner numbers 675 -> 2160 gives x3.2, give-up cases, 125% reproduction).
**Not run:** Vitest, typecheck, build, any Electron window. **Standing gaps (owner check):** that the log no longer shows "Image scale did not apply" and the PNG is 2160 px wide for the 4x default; that `devTools:false` does not block `webContents.debugger` (believed not to; fallback covers it); that a CDP screenshot of the hidden window paints (the two-frame wait is carried over, not proven); memory/time of a 4x capture vs before; the pooled window reuse after a debugger session.

### Phase 115 - Milestone 10 QA gate + perf + docs sweep - DONE 2026-10-11
**Run (first time for Phases 107-114 files):** `npm ci --ignore-scripts` (native binaries not built), `npm run typecheck` (all four tsconfigs), full `npx vitest run`: first run 191 files passed, 2 failed (4 tests); after the fixes below the affected files (src/__tests__, shared/format, shared/items, renderer/components/items, items service, perf) pass and typecheck is clean. I did NOT re-run the whole suite a second time. No build, no dev server, no Electron (owner instruction).
**Defects found and fixed:** (1) `pages/Items.tsx` `onWindowKey` typed `KeyboardEvent` instead of `KeyboardEvent<HTMLElement>` (3 typecheck errors). (2) REAL BUG: `frameRef` was an inline arrow, a new function every render, so React detached/re-attached the ref each render and the virtualised Groups list read a null scroll element (no rows drawn, shown by the Phase 113 jsdom test). Fixed with stable `frameRefs` (useMemo) plus one re-render after mount in `GroupsWindow.tsx`. Whether the blank list ever showed in a real window is unknown (real Chromium has ResizeObserver, which may have masked it). (3) Golden Rule scan: `=== 'gst'` tab-id comparisons in `salesView.ts` / `SalesPanel.tsx` (a UI tab, not a format id) now use the named constant `GST_TAB`; behaviour unchanged. (4) `keyboardAcceptance.test.ts`: its action-id regexes did not accept digits (`items.window1`), and `SalesPanel.tsx` was added to the reviewed double-click list. (5) Test-only: itemsPage title selector, itemsWindows113 `lastDecision` mock (reload wiped the undo label), itemsService "same decision twice" test (the merged-away group is gone, so the repeat is refused).
**Perf (new `main/services/__tests__/itemsPerf115.test.ts`, invented names, 5 000 bills x 12 = 60 000 lines, 2 400 groups, Node + SQLite, this sandbox):** cold open 962 ms, warm open 26 ms, rebuild 931 ms (+ open 56 ms), after ONE save: new spelling 165 ms / rate-only 25 ms, group() 18 ms, sales() 22 ms. Save itself adds nothing (the index syncs on read). Loose ceilings asserted. NOT measured: the renderer (virtualised lists with 2 400 groups / 2 000 sales rows), a real window, a real PC.

### Phase 113 - Items windows 1 and 2: groups and spellings - DONE 2026-10-11
**Built:** (1) the first WRITE channel of the Items page, contract first (`API_CONTRACTS.md`): `items:decide` (zod discriminated union), `items:undo`, `items:lastDecision`; bridge `window.quot3.items.{decide,undo,lastDecision}`; service `decide / undoLast / lastDecision` with injected `RuleOps` (the Phase 109 repo + local branch id, wired in `main/index.ts`, absent on a read-only database = decisions refused). A decision only saves owner RULES (synced by Phase 111); no bill is ever touched. (2) Pure `shared/items/decide.ts` (`planDecision`: group ids / spelling keys -> rule drafts) and `shared/items/groupRows.ts` (family -> variants rows, selection over groups only, spelling + suggestion rows). (3) `GroupsWindow` (virtualised, family header rows, marked item outlined), `SpellingsWindow` (spelling, uses, last used, why; then "Suggested merges"), `windowKeys.ts`, store state/actions, Items page wiring, undo button, status line, CSS (`.q3-items__row*`, tokens only), 7 registry actions + palette handlers.
**Decisions as built [A]:** (1) **Merge** = mark with M, move to the target, M / Enter again (no dialog, keyboard only; Esc cancels). Direction: the marked item merges INTO the target (rule `keyA` = target's most used spelling key, `keyB` = marked one). (2) **Confirm a suggestion** = Enter on its row in Spellings (one key): merges the less used side into the more used side. **Dismiss** = X / Delete = a `split` rule between the two groups, which silences that suggestion for good (the Phase 108 split-pair rule). (3) **Split** = S on a spelling pulls that spelling's original key out. It is refused with a reason when every spelling of the item has the same words (same key). (4) **Move** = V on a spelling, pick the target in Groups, M: saved as TWO rules, split first then merge (a single-key item is just a merge). Rule times are made strictly increasing (`stamp()`), because rules apply oldest first and a tie would be broken by random id. (5) **Not an item** = Delete in Groups: one `ignore` rule per original key of the item. (6) **Undo** reverts the whole last ACTION (a move = 2 steps, an ignore of a multi-key item = n steps): the service keeps an in-memory step count per action; rules from an earlier session undo one step at a time, labelled "the last decision". Ctrl+Z on the page (not inside the search box). There is no redo, and no screen yet to bring back a "not an item" except Undo. (7) Plain keys (M S V X Delete Enter) are registered for the palette / F1 but handled by the focused window only (like Customers), so they never fire while typing in the search box; the page-level listener now only reacts to an explicit list of page actions. (8) Groups: ArrowRight opens Spellings; Spellings: Left = Groups, Right = Sales. (9) Not built: family rename / `family` rule UI (the rule exists in the repo), windows are not searchable beyond the Phase 112 box, the Spellings list is not virtualised (one item has few spellings).
**Tests:** `shared/items/__tests__/decide113.test.ts` (13: merge / split / move / ignore / reject / refusals END TO END through `groupItems` with rules, plus the row builders) - **RUN and all pass** through `tsx` with a tiny `vitest` shim and a `decimal.js` stub (not the real Vitest). WRITTEN, NOT RUN: `main/services/__tests__/itemsService.test.ts` (new Phase 113 blocks: merge writes a rule and leaves bills unchanged, same decision twice = one rule, move = 2 rules + ONE undo, ignore + undo, refusals, read-only, IPC shapes) and `renderer/components/items/__tests__/itemsWindows113.test.ts` (jsdom: rows, key paths for merge / split / move / confirm / reject / ignore / undo, Esc rules, no keys while typing in the search box, selector stability).
**Checked by tsc only partially:** `decide.ts`, `groupRows.ts`, `itemsService.ts` compiled with strict + noUncheckedIndexedAccess without errors in those files (other errors were missing-dependency noise: no `node_modules`). The renderer files (store, components, page) and the test files were NOT type-checked (no `@types/react` here); I re-read them by hand.
**NOT run, honestly:** `npm install`, `npm run typecheck`, the project's Vitest, the build (skipped by owner instruction), Electron. **Standing gaps:** the project typecheck of every Phase 111-113 file; the real look of the two windows (family header rows, the marked-row outline, 1366x768 proportions, all six themes); virtualised-list behaviour in a real window; `itemRules.save` called twice for a move is two transactions (not one): a crash between them could leave only the split (harmless, undoable); whether a nested `.immediate()` is an issue was avoided by not nesting; the quality of the automatic suggestions on the owner's real bills is still the owner's call.

### Phase 111 - Items rules sync - DONE 2026-10-11
**Built:** snapshot kind `item_rule` (`shared/sync/itemRule.ts`), repo hook `onChange` + `applyRemote` (`itemRulesRepo.ts`), `main/sync/itemRuleApplier.ts`, push worker sends a rule when an outbox id is a rule, pull worker decrypts either kind (`kind: 'item_rule'` marker), wiring in `main/index.ts`. Contracts in `API_CONTRACTS.md`, design as built in `SYNC_POSTBOX_SPEC.md` section 11. No migration, no IPC, no UI.
**Decisions [A]:** (1) rule id doubles as the postbox `bill_id` (uuid, so the server column accepts it), revision always 0, outbox row reuses `sync_outbox.bill_id`; (2) LWW = newer `updatedAt`, then greater branch id (rules have no revision); (3) fingerprint dedupe: identical content never rewrites the row (times / maker may differ between branches, content is equal); (4) a bad incoming rule is dropped + logged, never thrown, so it cannot block bills; (5) a pre-111 build cannot read the kind and will skip those rows after 10 runs (unacknowledged until TTL); update all branches; (6) rules existing before activation are not queued (backup import bootstraps them); (7) received rules leave no undo step and no outbox row; (8) a wipe still clears the outbox only, never sends deletes, and leaves `item_rules` alone (carried forward from Phase 109).
**Tests:** `shared/sync/__tests__/itemRule.test.ts` (9, RUN, pass), `main/sync/__tests__/itemRuleSync.test.ts` (13 tests on two/three virtual branches: convergence, same decision twice = one rule, LWW, tie on branch id, tombstone / revive / undo, never re-sent, lossy network, old-branch ignore, applier drops bad rule, wipe sends no deletes). **11 of them were RUN and passed**; the 2 that save real bills (rules + bills sharing the postbox, bill behind a rule on an ignoring branch) were NOT run. Phase 109 `itemRules.test.ts` run again: 10 pass, 2 fail only because my shim lacked `toThrow` / `toBeLessThanOrEqual` (not a code failure, not re-run with real Vitest).
**How they were run (honest):** the sandbox had no network, so the project's `node_modules` could not be installed. I ran the tests with `tsx` plus throwaway shims outside the project: a mini `vitest`, `better-sqlite3` over Node's `node:sqlite`, zod 3 (project pins 4), and stubs for `decimal.js`, `drizzle-orm`, `fuse.js`. Real SQLite, real crypto, real push/pull workers and InMemoryTransport were used; the shims are not the real libraries.
**Not run:** the project's `npm run typecheck` / `npm test`, build, the two bill-dependent tests, `main/index.ts` wiring (needs Electron), a real Supabase. **Standing gaps:** everything above plus: a real `qt_push` accepting revision 0 and these ids (SQL allows `revision >= 0` and uuid, expected fine); behaviour of `.immediate()` nested inside the pull transaction on real better-sqlite3 (applyRemote deliberately opens none); zod 4 vs 3 differences in `itemRuleSnapshotSchema`.

### Phase 110 - Items service: groups and sales read model - DONE 2026-10-11
**Built:** pure `shared/items/sales.ts` (`saleLine`, `buildSalesTab`, `rateStats`, `outlierOf`, `partyKeyOf`; money via `calcRow` and decimal.js, never JS numbers) and `shared/items/api.ts` (DTOs); read-only `main/db/itemsRepo.ts` (`signatures`, `loadBills`, `countLive`, `bill`); `main/services/itemsService.ts` (`overview`, `group`, `sales`, `rebuild(onProgress)`, `openTarget`); `main/ipc/items.ts` (`items:overview|group|sales|rebuild|openTarget`, zod, event `items:progress` added to `EVENT_CHANNELS`); bridge `window.quot3.items.*` (`shared/ipc.ts`, `preload/index.ts`); wired in `register.ts` (`ctx.items`, `ctx.itemsEmit`) and `main/index.ts`. Contract in `API_CONTRACTS.md` (written first). No schema change, no UI, no sync, no write channel (rule decisions arrive with the UI phases).
**How it stays current (decision):** no hooks in save / delete / restore. Every read first compares a cheap per-bill signature (`revision`, `updated_at`, `deleted_at`) with the index and reloads only bills that differ, so edit, delete, restore, purge, legacy import, undo of an import and remote apply all show up, and Save adds NOTHING (budget < 5 ms trivially met on the save path). Grouping (Phase 108, ~0.25 s on the corpus) is recomputed only when the spellings (name, count, last date) or the owner rules changed; a rate-only edit never regroups. `rebuild` clears the index, reloads in chunks of 200 bills with progress, yields between chunks. One rebuild at a time (second call = VALIDATION error).
**GST vs non-GST:** by the bill's FORMAT (`formatHasGst`, data-driven; no format-name branch). A row that still carries a GST % on a non-GST bill stays on the Non-GST tab. GST tab rows carry GST %, tax, line total with tax (`calcRow`).
**What counts as a sale [A]:** a named row with qty, rate or a typed total (kinds item / adjustment). Name-only and blank rows are not sales. Charges (`GST EXTRA`, `LESS`, ...) are indexed but dropped by the Phase 107 non-product rules and listed under `notItems`. Stats per tab: count, priced count, min / median (even count = exact decimal mean) / max, latest rate + date, total qty, parties; last priced rate per party; party filter narrows rows and stats but the median / flags stay the whole tab's.
**Outlier flag [A] (mine, owner may change):** a rate more than 30% above / below the tab's median, only when >= 5 priced sales and median > 0 (`OUTLIER_RATIO`, `OUTLIER_MIN_PRICED`). Rows are capped at 2 000 per tab (`truncated`).
**Tests (all RUN, Vitest 5.0.3, real temp-file SQLite):** `main/services/__tests__/itemsService.test.ts` (14: GST / non-GST never mixed, stats, even-median, last rate per party + outliers + filter, <5 priced no flags, variants share a group, trashed excluded, non-product, incremental after edit / delete / restore / add = cold start = rebuild (overview, families, sales and group detail all identical), rate edit does not regroup, new rule applies on next read, one-bill sync timing, NOT_FOUND / openTarget, index never writes, IPC validation + rebuild progress / busy), `shared/items/__tests__/sales.test.ts` (7), `itemsCorpus110.test.ts` (private corpus, counts only, skipped without the zip). **Corpus run:** 768 bills, 8 945 lines, 3 247 groups; cold read 274 ms, rebuild 218 ms; AMETO SMALL non-GST n=118 over 77 parties, median **82** (spec), GST n=3 (spec: 121 sales, 3 GST) - matches. Its min is 0 (a zero rate exists in the data, shown as is). `tsc --noEmit` (strict, noUncheckedIndexedAccess) over the new source and ALL new test files plus `shared/ipc.ts`: clean.
**Not run (as instructed: no build):** `npm run build`, the project's own `npm run typecheck` / `npm test` (no project `node_modules`; throwaway install, electron types absent so `register.ts` / `index.ts` could only be checked for errors other than the missing-electron ones: none), any Electron window. **Standing gaps:** `main/index.ts` / `register.ts` wiring has never executed (needs Electron); cost of the first read on a 5 000-bill / 60 000-line shop (measured only on 768 bills / 8.9 k lines: signature scan + load is linear, expect ~2 s cold; the page should show a progress state); a one-bill change still pays a full regroup when it adds / removes a spelling (~0.25 s at 4 k spellings); rule writes are not exposed over IPC yet.

### Phase 109 - Item rules schema + repository - DONE 2026-10-11
**Built:** migration `0005_item_rules.sql` (+ journal idx 5, `schema.ts` `itemRules` / `itemRuleLog`); `shared/items/rules.ts` (pure: `itemRuleId`, `checkItemRule`, `toGroupingRule`, types); `main/db/itemRulesRepo.ts` (`createItemRulesRepo(sqlite)`: `list(includeDeleted?)`, `get`, `save`, `remove`, `lastDecision`, `undoLast`, `count`, `transaction`). Tables and id rules are in `DATA_MODEL.md` (as built) and `API_CONTRACTS.md`. No IPC channel, service, sync or UI yet (Phases 110-111).
**Behaviour:** `save` = one decision, one row: same decision again (or merge/split with the pair in the other order and nothing else changed) writes nothing; a changed direction / canonical name / note updates the row (`update`); a deleted decision made again is revived (`revive`, `created_at` kept); invalid input (empty key, merge with itself, nameless family, unknown kind) throws a readable `Error` and writes nothing. Keys are re-normalised with Phase 107 on the way in. `remove` = soft delete (tombstone for later sync). Every change writes an `audit_log` row (entity `item_rule`; actions create / update / revive / delete / undo) and one `item_rule_log` step in the same immediate transaction. `undoLast` reverts the newest step not yet undone by restoring the row as it was before (create -> deleted again); the undo gets a NEW `updated_at` (so it would win a sync); repeated undo walks further back; no redo; the log keeps 200 steps.
**Decisions [A]:** (1) a merge keeps its direction (`key_a` = merged INTO) but its id ignores the order, so choosing the opposite direction updates the same row; (2) saving a split does not delete an older merge of the same pair: both rows stay and the newest wins at grouping time (Phase 108); (3) `item_rule_log` is local-only and not part of any sync snapshot; (4) 'Reset local data' / the wipe does not clear the rule tables (carried forward: add them to `wipeApply` with its test in a later phase if the owner wants a full reset); (5) no derived cache table was added (spec: optional) because nothing needs one until Phase 110 measures.
**Tests (all RUN):** `main/db/__tests__/itemRules.test.ts` (real temp-file SQLite: columns; migration from a DB at 0004 with 3 bills keeps them and takes the pre-migration backup; create/audit/log; same decision twice = one row; swapped direction; invalid input; soft delete + revive; undo of create / update / delete / revive, repeated undo, undo moves `updated_at` forward, log cap; end to end with the Phase 108 grouping), `shared/items/__tests__/rules.test.ts` (ids, re-normalised keys, validation), and `db.test.ts` updated for the two new tables. `src/main/db` + `src/shared/items`: 130 tests pass. Whole `src/main` tree: 512 passed, 28 skipped, 5 test FILES could not load only because my throwaway install lacks `fuse.js` (unrelated to this phase; not a failure in the code). `tsc --noEmit` (strict, noUncheckedIndexedAccess) over the new source AND the new test files with real `better-sqlite3` / `zod` / `decimal.js` types: clean.
**Not run:** the project's own `npm run typecheck` / `npm test` (no project `node_modules`; throwaway install used), build, any Electron window. Native `better-sqlite3` ran under plain Node here, not under Electron's ABI (that is Phase 1's proof, unchanged). **Standing gaps:** migration on a real Phase-91 database file from your PC (only a constructed 0004 database was tested); Electron-ABI run.

### Phase 108 - Vocabulary, typo suggestions, tiers and families (pure) - DONE 2026-10-11
**Built:** `shared/items/vocabulary.ts` (`buildVocabulary`, `typoCandidates`, `autoTypoFix`, `analyseEdit`, `isSafeAutoEdit`, `isBrandToken`, constants) and `shared/items/grouping.ts` (`groupItems(spellings, rules?) => { groups, families, notItems, vocabularySize }`, `soldAtLeast`, types `ItemSpelling`, `ItemRuleInput`, `ItemGroup`, `GroupSpelling` with `why`, `Suggestion`, `ItemFamily`, `NotItem`). Pure, deterministic (input order never matters; tested), no I/O or clock.
**Tiers:** AUTO = a spelling is inside a group (same Phase 107 key, safe typo fix, or owner merge); SUGGESTED = `Suggestion { kind: typo | plural | one-word, fromId, toId, text }` between two groups, never merged on its own; SEPARATE = the rest. Each spelling carries `why { rule: most-used | same-key | typo-auto | owner-merge, text }`.
**Typo rule as built:** candidate = plain alphabetic word >= 4 letters, same first letter, length within 2, one edit (two edits only for words >= 7 letters, suggestion only), target >= 10x as common. AUTO only if exactly ONE target qualifies AND the edit is on the safe whitelist: doubled letter (min length 5), adjacent swap (5), vowel substitution (6), single insert/delete (7); never at the last letter, never consonant-for-consonant, never two edits; AND (typed word <= 2 uses and target >= 30 uses) OR (typed word <= 10 uses and target >= 30x as common). **Deviation from the spec [A]:** the second clause is mine: on the owner's corpus JUPITOR, GRIPER, BUTTTON and SHAKKTI are each used 5-6 times, so the spec's 'count <= 2' would never fix the four typos PHASES.md says must be fixed. The edit whitelist and length floors are also mine [A] (they are what keeps STEAM/SEAM, STRIKER/STICKER, PERAL/PERL, JACKO/JACK, GERMANY/GERMAN, SHAPE/SHAPES out of AUTO; those six are still offered as suggestions when both groups exist).
**Suggestions:** typo (non-auto candidate whose replaced key is an existing group), plural (word vs word+S/ES, both known), one-word (same other words, one different word; needs at least one shared word; skipped when both differing words are colours or both are sizes (`COLOUR_WORDS`, `SIZE_WORDS`, fixed lists [A], not learned); only when the rarer differing word has <= 3 uses). A split rule silences the pair. Word classes are NOT inferred from position/frequency beyond the brand rule (simplification of spec step 5).
**Families:** brand word = >= 5 uses and >= 60% of the time the first word of a name with 2+ words (a one-word name says nothing about position). A group's family = its most used brand word (or the owner's `family` rule). Display only.
**Owner rules (applied last, oldest `updatedAt` first, id as tiebreak, so the newest decision on a pair wins):** `merge` (keyA, keyB, optional canonical display), `split` (pulls keyB's spellings out; silences suggestions for the pair), `ignore` (-> notItems, `byOwner`), `family`; `deleted` rules are skipped (result equals having no rule); rules naming keys that do not exist are ignored. Rule keys are the Phase 107 keys of the ORIGINAL spellings. Group `id` = the group's key (after a safe typo fix; for an owner merge the key of the group merged INTO).
**Corpus run (counts only, `__tests__/corpus108.test.ts`, skipped without the zip):** 4 039 spellings -> 3 240 groups (51 spellings joined by the typo rule), 19 not-items, 686 suggestion pairs, 154 families; **1 197 groups sold >= 2 times covering 6 402 lines** (spec expected ~1 215 / ~6 260 from a different method); the six false matches: 0 automatic; the four named typos: all 4 fixed automatically; runs in ~0.25 s. Nothing in the corpus was printed. Grouping quality on real names is still the owner's call (many auto-merges come from bracket-note stripping, Phase 107).
**Tests:** `__tests__/grouping.test.ts` (edit analysis, vocabulary, the four+ known typos, the six false matches, 10x / 30-use / ambiguity guards, protected tokens, groups + why, notItems, suggestions incl. colour/size guard, families, every owner rule incl. newest-wins, determinism, 6 000 spellings < 15 s) and `corpus108.test.ts`. All 97 tests in `src/shared/items` pass with Vitest 5.0.3 installed in a throwaway folder against this tree's `vitest.config.ts`; `tsc --noEmit` (strict, noUncheckedIndexedAccess) over `normalise.ts`, `vocabulary.ts`, `grouping.ts` is clean (this also covers Phase 107's file, which was unchecked then; the TEST files were not typechecked).
**Not run:** the project's own `npm run typecheck` (no `node_modules`), `npm run build`, the whole suite. No schema / IPC / package / UI change. **Standing gaps:** typecheck of the test files; real-name quality (owner); `one-word` suggestions may be noisy for very rare words (each rare word is offered against every group sharing the rest of the name); performance only measured at 4 k real and 6 k synthetic spellings, not 60 k lines of a 5 000-bill shop.

### Phase 107 - Item normaliser (pure) - DONE 2026-10-11
**Built:** `src/shared/items/normalise.ts` (pure; no Electron / Node / DOM). `normaliseItem(raw) => NormalisedItem { raw, cleaned, note, tokens, protectedTokens, wordTokens, key, nonProduct: { ruleId, label } | null, empty }`; helpers `cleanName`, `splitNotes`, `isProtectedToken`, `nonProductRule`, `itemKey`, `sameItemKey`; data `NON_PRODUCT_RULES` (gst-line, coolie, less, advance, charge, freight, round-off) and `UNIT_TOKENS`.
**Rules as built (spec section 2 steps 1-4):** uppercase (NFKC), `32 x 50` / `32*50` / `32\u00D750` -> `32X50` (also three-part), `1 / 2` -> `1/2`, `12"` -> `12 INCH`, `&` -> `AND`, every other separator/punctuation -> one space (letters and digits of any script kept; `%` and decimal dots kept), bracket notes `()` `[]` `{}` split into `note` (an unclosed bracket keeps its text in the name, nothing is lost), key = tokens sorted by plain code-point order (locale independent). Protected token = has a digit, or is a unit word (`UNIT_TOKENS`: measures, PCS/DOZEN/GROSS only; BOX / SET / ROLL etc. are deliberately NOT units, so a later typo step may still touch them). Non-product rules run on the cleaned name; `ADVANCE` and `PAYMENT` filter only when they are the WHOLE name (so `ADVANCE ZIP` and `PAYMENT SLIP BOOK` stay goods); `LESS ...` / `GST ...` / `... CHARGE(S)` / `COOLIE` / `COURIER` etc. filter.
**Decisions [A]:** (1) the non-product rule list and unit list are my guesses from the spec's examples, not the owner's list: owner-editable later, flagged for review. (2) Duplicate words are kept in the key (`BUTTON BUTTON` != `BUTTON`). (3) Singular/plural (BUTTON / BUTTONS) is NOT merged here; that is a Phase 108 suggestion. (4) A name that is only a bracket note is `empty` (with the note kept). (5) A note never makes a line a charge: the filter looks at the name without notes.
**Corpus run (counts only, owner's 768 files; `__tests__/corpus.test.ts`, skipped when the zip is absent):** 8 474 named lines; 8 445 product lines after the filter (29 lines in 17 distinct non-product names); 4 038 distinct trimmed spellings -> 3 563 cleaned names -> **3 271 key groups**. The spec expected ~3 470 key groups; the same plain method (uppercase, collapse spaces/dashes, sorted words) gives 3 495 here, so the difference to the spec is the new steps: bracket notes stripped (533 lines carry a note: 3 445 groups without that step) and size / dot / punctuation unification (a further ~170). That is more merging than the spec's estimate, all of it rule-based and explainable, but the owner should judge it on real names (nothing inspected here: no name was printed or copied).
**Tests:** `src/shared/items/__tests__/normalise.test.ts` (52: cleaning, sizes, fractions, notes, order-insensitive key incl. all six orderings of a 3-word name, TALCO 2044 = 2044 TALCO, ARIHANT 32X50 != 25X50, MILTON 505 != D7, units, filtered vs kept lines, determinism) and `corpus.test.ts`. Both ran green with Vitest 5.0.3 installed in a throwaway folder against this tree's `vitest.config.ts` (the project `node_modules` was not installed).
**Not run:** `npm run typecheck` (no `node_modules` here; the new file uses only built-in language features and `import type`-free code, but this is NOT proven), `npm run build`, the whole Vitest suite, any Electron window. No schema / IPC / package / UI change. **Standing gaps:** typecheck of the two new files; grouping quality on real names (owner judgement); non-Latin (Hindi / Tamil) names only covered by the letter-class rule, not by a test on real names.

### Phase 105 REGRESSION FIX (owner report: copy-as-image stopped working after Phase 105) - 2026-10-11
**What went wrong:** Phase 105 made the untested DevTools-protocol capture the FIRST path. On the owner's PC copy then failed outright. Cause not observed (no log, no Windows here); most likely a `Page.captureScreenshot` / `Emulation` command on the hidden window that never answers or leaves emulation/debugger state behind. My mistake: an Electron-only path with no way to run it was put in front of the working one.
**Fix:** `electronImagePort.capture()` runs the ORIGINAL `enableDeviceEmulation` + `capturePage` path first again; if its width verifies, that is returned (identical to pre-Phase-105 behaviour). Only when the scale did not apply is the DevTools path tried as an upgrade: every command is time-limited (`CDP_COMMAND_TIMEOUT_MS` 5 s), errors are swallowed, and its PNG replaces the working one only if `cdpResultUsable` (width matches the plan AND more bytes than the fallback, so a blank paint is rejected). After one failed/unusable DevTools attempt it is switched off for the session (`cdpBroken`), so no repeated delay. Tests: `imageFlow.test.ts` "Phase 105 fix" block; typecheck passes; `src/shared/print` + imageService tests pass (130).
**Expected on the owner's PC:** copy works as before (675/930 px, the old warning in the log); on the first copy the upgrade is tried once, and if it works the PNG becomes 2160 px wide. **Still unverified** - owner must report the log line. If copy still fails, the cause is NOT the DevTools path (it can no longer run before the original path) and the log/diagnostics are needed.

### Phase 127 HOTFIX 4 - Professional image still came out as a big white page with the bill small inside (owner, 2026-10-11; Rough was fine)
**Honest status:** the exact reason Professional differs from Rough is NOT known (cannot run Electron; no log lines yet). The guards of hotfix 3 did not catch it, so this fix does not depend on the cause: it checks the PICTURE. New pure `shared/print/imageTrim.ts` (`nonWhiteBounds` on raw BGRA pixels, `isTight` = content fills >= 85 % of both dimensions, `trimRect` keeps the usual 12 CSS px padding), tested in `__tests__/imageTrim.test.ts`. In `main/print/electronImagePort.ts`: (1) an HD upgrade (zoom or DevTools) is accepted only if its PNG is tight (`pngIsTight`); (2) whatever method produced the final PNG, `trimLoosePng` crops a loose one to the content + padding and logs `Image capture: result had white margins, trimmed to the content` with the sizes. So the clipboard image is now always trimmed to the contents; the cost is that a loose HD result can end up at the smaller legacy resolution. If Professional is trimmed but not HD, send the log lines `Image capture` (tier, displayScale, expectedWidth, actualWidth) and the trimmed warning. Not run in Electron.

### Phase 127 HOTFIX 3 - copied image was the whole window, bill small in the middle (owner screenshot, 2026-10-11)
**Reading of the evidence [A, not run]:** the image was as wide as the plan (so Phase 121's width check passed) but the bill filled only ~1/3 of it at the legacy 1.25 display scale, with white all around - i.e. the window was enlarged by the zoom method but `setZoomFactor` did not change the page layout, so `capturePage` returned a big mostly-white surface. **Fix (two guards):** (1) `captureViaZoom` now reads `window.innerWidth` after zooming and gives up (log `Image capture: zoom did not change the layout`) unless the page's CSS viewport is still the planned width; (2) `cdpResultUsable` also requires the planned SHAPE (`sameShape`: PNG width/height within 2 % of the content rectangle's) for BOTH upgrade methods, so a right-width wrong-content image can never replace the cropped one. Worst case the copy falls back to the cropped legacy image (the pre-Phase-121 behaviour, 675 px wide at 125 %). Files: `shared/print/imageFlow.ts`, `main/print/electronImagePort.ts`, test in `shared/print/__tests__/imageFlow.test.ts`. If HD is then still 675 wide, the zoom method is simply not working on that PC - send the `Image capture` log lines. Not run in Electron.

### Phase 127 HOTFIX 2 - Print dialog too narrow, sideways scroll bar (owner screenshot, 2026-10-11)
The shared `Dialog` is 460 px wide; the print dialog holds an info column plus a paper-sized preview (A5 about 400 px, A4 more), so it overflowed and showed a horizontal scroll bar and clipped the preview. **Fix:** `Dialog` gets an optional `className`; `PrintDialog` passes `q3-dialog--print` (`width: max-content; min-width: min(100%, 720px); max-width: 100%`) so the box fits its content; `.q3-print` is `flex-wrap: nowrap`. Files: `components/ui/Dialog.tsx`, `components/create/PrintDialog.tsx`, `styles/ui.css`, `styles/grid.css`. Typecheck pass; create + ui suites 145 pass. NOT seen in a real window (CSS only): on a very small window the box is capped at the window width and could still scroll.

### Phase 127 HOTFIX - 'Copy image' showed 'Invalid request' (owner screenshot, 2026-10-11)
**Cause (found by reading, reproduced by the new test):** `image:copy` / `image:prerender` validated the bill with its own narrower schema (`billPayloadSchema` + `poNumber`) that lacked `roundOff`, while the renderer's `printPayload` sends `roundOff: 1` whenever the bill has round-off on (Phase 120 made that real). The strict zod schema rejected it -> toast 'Invalid request'. `print:*` already used the wider schema, so only image broke. **Fix:** `printBill` is now exported from `main/ipc/print.ts` and `main/ipc/image.ts` uses that same schema (one bill shape for print + image). **Test:** `main/ipc/__tests__/image.test.ts` +1 (round-off, tail rows, footer extras accepted by copy and prerender). `src/main/ipc` suite 37 pass; typecheck pass. Not run on the real app. This is a Phase 120 regression that the Phase 127 gate missed because no test sent a round-off bill through the image channel.
