Decide whether a drag handle is wanted.

### Phase 123 - Row reorder: model (pure) - DONE 2026-10-11
**Built (model only, no UI, no shortcut - those are Phase 124):**
- `shared/rows/moveRows.ts` (pure): `moveDestination(rows, bodyIds, rowId, 'up'|'down'|'top'|'bottom')` -> target body index or null (no-op); `moveItemRow(rows, bodyIds, rowId, toIndex, extraKeys)` -> new rows or null. Same position model as Phase 122 delete: item cells (name runs + per-word formatting, qty, rate, manual total, GST %, item-column highlights) travel; every position keeps its id, idx, extra-column cells, marks and extra highlights. Same length, no input mutation. [A] A move never goes below the last row that has item content (down on the last item row, or 'bottom', stops there; items are never pushed into the trailing blank rows). Adjustment / total rows are not body rows and never move.
- `billStore.moveRow(rowId, target)`: one history entry (label 'Moved row to N'), view/totals rebuilt, returns the new body index or null with NO history entry for a no-op. Units / marked segments follow the positions via the existing engine (extras stay, so they are unchanged by design).
- Sync / diff: no code change needed. `sameContent` compares rows by `idx` and `diffBills` by row id, and because ids stay on positions a move shows up as changed item fields on the affected rows (diff reports them as 'changed', not 'moved'; 'moved' only arises when ids themselves reorder). Tested for both. No schema, IPC or package change.
**Tests:** `shared/rows/__tests__/moveRows.test.ts` (9: up/down, top/bottom, no-ops incl. last item row and blank bill, highlight split, per-word formatting, no mutation + round trip, bad targets, with Phase 122 delete, `sameContent` detects a move); `stores/__tests__/rowMove123.test.ts` (4: extras/ids stay + one undo/redo + dirty, no-op leaves no history, bottom/top keeps the grand total, `toBill`/`normalizeBill` round trip + diff). `src/shared/rows` + `src/renderer/stores` + `src/renderer/components/grid`: 23 files / 271 tests pass. `npm run typecheck` (4 configs): pass. Used `npm ci --ignore-scripts` once.
**Not run:** build (owner instruction), dev server, whole suite, any real window. Nothing visible changes for the owner yet.
**Next (Phase 124):** Alt+Up / Alt+Down call `moveRow` on the active row (check the shortcut-conflict test), optional drag handle, render-count harness, F1 hints, keep the cursor on the moved row.

### Phase 122 - Delete row (extras stay) - DONE 2026-10-11
**What was missing:** the grid had NO row selection at all, so row delete needed it first. Built (all [A], the owner has not specified the gesture beyond "click row number / Shift+Space"):
- **Select a row:** click the Sl.No cell of an item row, or Ctrl+Space from any cell, or Shift+Space **in a number cell only**. Reason (found while designing, would have been a regression): in a text cell Shift+Space is a typed space (people hold Shift for capitals: "ABC DEF"), so it is NOT swallowed there (tested). The selection is a full-width range, so the existing range highlight, Shift+Up/Down extension and Esc work; it is flagged as a row selection (`rowSel`) so that Ctrl+A / plain Shift ranges still use the old rule.
- **Delete:** with a row selection, Delete removes the rows. Delete on a single cell (D-K4) and on a plain range is unchanged (tested). Palette actions `grid.selectRow` / `grid.deleteRows` added (`shortcuts.ts`, `useAppActions.ts`, `gridUiStore` ports).
- **Rule (`shared/rows/deleteRows.ts`, pure):** body rows are positions. Item cells (name runs + per-word formatting, qty, rate, manual total, GST %, highlights of item columns) move up; extra-column cells, their marks / highlights, the row ids and idx stay on their positions; freed bottom positions become blank. Adjustment rows are not body rows: never moved or deleted; total/tail rows cannot be selected as rows ("Only item rows can be selected as a row" notice). Marked segments / units are computed from display order by the existing engine, so they follow the positions automatically.
- **Store:** `billStore.deleteRows(ids)`: one history entry (label 'Deleted row' / 'Deleted N rows'), totals and view rebuilt, trailing blank rows kept as today. The row count of the bill does not shrink by itself (positions stay), so the paper row estimate follows content as before.
**Tests:** `shared/rows/__tests__/deleteRows.test.ts` (7: middle / first / last / blank / only row, several rows, marks stay, highlight split item vs extra, ignored ids and adjustment rows, no input mutation); `components/grid/__tests__/gridRowDelete122.test.ts` (9: Ctrl+Space + Delete + one undo, two rows = one undo, Delete on cell/range unchanged, Ctrl+A + Delete never deletes rows, Shift+Space typed space vs number cell, Esc, Sl.No click, store with extras). Grid + stores + rows + shortcuts + hooks: 22 files / 275 pass; shared/nav/create/ui/router: 34 files / 462 pass. `npm run typecheck`: pass. A wider run (shared + all components + pages in one go) exceeded the sandbox time limit and was not completed.
**Not run:** build (owner instruction), dev server, real window, F1 hints / cheat-sheet text beyond the registry labels, paper-count test (no paper logic touched), whole suite. No schema / IPC / package change.
**STANDING GAPS (owner):** confirm in the real window: clicking the Sl.No number selects the row (the cell may be too small / not clickable at 26 px rows), the selected row's highlight is clear, Delete removes it and the extra-column text stays put, one Ctrl+Z restores. Decide whether Shift+Space should select the row in text cells too (currently it types a space there).

### Phase 121 - HD image really applies on the owner's PC - DONE 2026-10-11 (code + pure tests; NOT run on Windows)
**What the evidence says:** owner toast 675 x 228 = 540 CSS px x 1.25 (Windows 125% display scale): `enableDeviceEmulation` + `capturePage` stays at the display scale. After Phase 105's fix the DevTools upgrade was tried on the first copy, and its failure was swallowed silently (`catch { return null }`), then switched off for the session (`cdpBroken`) - so the owner's log could not say why. **Why the DevTools method fails on the owner's PC is still unknown.** `devTools: false` in the window's `webPreferences` is one suspect (not confirmed: I could not run Electron).
**Built:** (1) A new THIRD method that needs neither DevTools nor emulation: page zoom. `shared/print/imageFlow.ts`: `zoomCapturePlan` (zoom = wanted scale / display scale, e.g. 4 / 1.25 = 3.2; window = CSS viewport x zoom; capture rect in DIP rounded outwards; refuses unknown display scale, zoom outside 0.25-6, or a surface over `MAX_SURFACE_PX` 16 000), `fitToPlanWidth` (ok / shrink to the exact planned width via `nativeImage.resize` quality best / reject; a too-small result is NEVER enlarged), `captureDiagFields`. `electronImagePort.ts`: `captureViaZoom`, `displayScaleOf` (`screen.getDisplayMatching`). (2) Order is now: original path (unchanged, still first) -> measured; if the width is wrong: zoom -> measured; if still wrong: DevTools -> measured; the working PNG is returned unless an upgrade has the planned width AND more bytes (`cdpResultUsable`). Each method is time-limited, errors never throw out of a copy. A method that is merely not applicable (tall bill) is not switched off; one that ran and failed is off for the session. (3) DIAGNOSTICS: every capture now logs `Image capture` {tier, wantedScale, displayScale, expectedWidth, actualWidth, ok}, and the DevTools / zoom failures log their error text (`Image capture: ... method failed`). `createElectronImagePort(log)` receives the main logger. Kept: verify-by-measuring in `imageService` (retry + 'Image scale did not apply' warning), `IMAGE_MAX_PX`, no layout / crop change.
**Tests:** `imageFlow.test.ts` +6 (the 540 -> 2160 case, zoom 3.2 at 125%, 100% and 200% displays, refusals incl. tall surface, `fitToPlanWidth` incl. the owner's 675 = reject, diagnostic fields). `src/shared/print` + `src/main/services` suites: 33 files / 411 tests pass. `npm run typecheck`: pass.
**Not run:** the Electron port (needs a real window; Windows 125% only reproduces there), build (owner instruction), dev server, whole suite. No schema / IPC / package change.
**Decisions [A]:** zoom before DevTools (plain Chromium, no debugger); the zoom method was chosen by reasoning about Chromium behaviour, NOT observed. `capturePage` DIP/zoom arithmetic could still be off by the window-frame / rounding in ways only a real window shows - that is why the result is measured and shrunk to the exact width, and rejected otherwise.
**STANDING GAPS (owner on Windows):** copy an image and read the log (`%LOCALAPPDATA%\Quot3\logs`): lines `Image capture` show `displayScale` (expect 1.25) and which `tier` reached `ok: true`; the toast should read 2160 wide for a 540 CSS px bill at quality 4 (not 675). If still 675: send the log lines - `Image capture: zoom method failed` / `DevTools method failed` carry the reason, and the next step is chosen from them (offscreen window or render at N x CSS size).

### Phase 120 - Round-off actually applies to grand totals - DONE 2026-10-11
**Reproduce-first result (code + jsdom, NOT a real window or the owner's DB):** every path was traced and tested. Working as designed and proven by tests: new bill (flag from `calc.roundOff`, default ON, only an explicit `false` turns it off), every grid edit (`opts()` -> `getBillOptions`), save (main stamps the flag from the setting, never from the client; totals cache + `applySaved` follow it), reload from the DB (`deserializeBill`), TSV / print / image (all call `getBillOptions`), setting OFF (exact totals). **One real inconsistency found and fixed:** opening an EXISTING bill for edit (History / Customers / Items -> `openBillForEdit`) loaded the stored flag. Old bills (made before Phase 77) and imported bills have flag 0, so the grid showed an UNROUNDED grand total although Save would stamp the setting and round it. That matches "round-off still doesn't happen in the grand totals" on any bill that is not brand new. **Cause NOT confirmed as the owner's one:** if the owner sees an unrounded total on a brand-new bill, the setting value on the PC or something I could not run is involved (see gaps).
**Fix (`stores/createSession.ts`, `openBillForEdit`):** the bill is loaded with `roundOff` = the current setting (via `behaviourStore.roundOff`) so the editing session shows what Save will produce. The flag is set BEFORE `loadBill`, so the baseline carries it: opening a bill is not 'unsaved work' (tested). Nothing is written to the DB until Save (old bills in History stay as they were; the stored value of other bills is unchanged). Imported bills keep 0 in the DB until re-saved (unchanged, by design).
**Tests:** `stores/__tests__/roundOffPaths120.test.ts` (5: new bill ON / OFF; old bill + setting ON = rounded, same as TSV copy, not dirty; flag 1 + setting OFF = exact; applySaved follows the stamped flag). With the fix removed 2 fail. Existing round-off suites (`roundOffWiring`, `billServiceRoundOff` incl. real SQLite save) pass; stores, pages, calc: 12 files / 224 tests pass. `npm run typecheck`: pass.
**Not run:** build (owner instruction), dev server, whole suite, any Electron window; print / HD image not exercised (they share `getBillOptions`, covered by the Phase 77 wiring test only). No schema / IPC / package change; no round-off line added.
**Decision [A]:** the on-screen total while editing an old bill follows the setting, not the stored flag (saving would change it anyway).
**STANDING GAPS (owner on the real PC):** (a) a NEW bill, e.g. rate 100.30 x qty 1: grand total must show 100.00 with Settings > round off ON; (b) open an old History bill: total whole-rupee now; (c) save, reopen from History: same; (d) print preview / copy image / copy TSV show the same whole total; (e) if (a) fails, tell me the Settings round-off switch state and whether it also fails after Save - then the cause is outside these paths.

### Phase 119 - Grid clipboard: Ctrl+C / Ctrl+V on ranges and cells - DONE 2026-10-11
**Reproduce-first result:** one cause REPRODUCED in jsdom, one NOT reproducible here (no real window). (1) REPRODUCED: a bare `Control` keydown went through `navReduce`; `Control` is not in `keepsRange`, so a selected range was collapsed (`COLLAPSE_RANGE`) before C was pressed. That is the owner's "Ctrl alone loses the selection". The same happened for Shift / Alt / Meta / CapsLock. (2) NOT reproduced (needs the owner's PC): the copy / paste itself relied only on the browser's native `copy` / `paste` events (copy even through `document.execCommand`). Whether those events fire for Ctrl+C / Ctrl+V in the real Electron window was never proven, so a fallback was added instead of guessing.
**Fix:** `shared/nav/cellNav.ts`: `MODIFIER_ONLY_KEYS` + an early `IGNORE` in `navReduce` (state returned untouched, range kept). `gridController.ts`: bare modifier keydown returns before anything else (no focus change, no notice clear); `copyData` / `finishCopy` factored out of `onCopy` (behaviour identical); `armClipboardFallback`: on Ctrl/Cmd+C/X/V a `CLIPBOARD_FALLBACK_MS` (60) timer starts; the native `copy`/`cut`/`paste` handlers set `clipSeen`; if no native event arrived, copy / cut write the TSV through `clipboard:writeTsv` (cut clears the range only AFTER a successful write; a failed write is reported and clears nothing) and paste reads `clipboard:readGrid` and flows through the unchanged `pasteMatrix`. No fallback in Free mode with no range (native text editing stays). `lastCopy` keeps the Quot3 flavour in memory so a bridge copy then paste inside Quot3 keeps item-name formatting (matched by identical TSV text). Native path and all Phase 16 rules unchanged.
**Tests:** `shared/nav/__tests__/cellNav.test.ts` +10 (bare Control/Shift/Alt/Meta/AltGraph/CapsLock/NumLock inert; Ctrl, C, V keep the range; Free mode untouched); NEW `components/grid/__tests__/gridClipboard119.test.ts` (10: bare modifiers keep range + focus; copy / cut / paste fallback; no double action when the native event arrived; Free mode; failed write; formatting round trip). Checked: with the two fixes disabled 10 of the new tests fail. Grid + create renderer suites: 26 files / 315 tests pass; `src/shared/nav`: pass. `npm run typecheck` (all 4 configs): pass. Used `npm ci --ignore-scripts` once.
**Not run:** build (owner instruction), dev server, whole suite, any Electron window, the real-window e2e (NOT written: needs a real Windows window; Notepad / Excel paste not exercised). No schema / IPC / package change (existing `clipboard:readGrid` / `writeTsv` reused).
**Decisions [A]:** fallback delay 60 ms; Ctrl+Shift+C / Ctrl+K etc. untouched (format keys); text pasted from outside keeps the existing "single value without range = native" rule.
**STANDING GAPS (owner on a real Windows PC):** (a) select a range, tap Ctrl alone: selection stays; (b) Ctrl+C then paste into Notepad and Excel (tab-separated); (c) copy from Excel / Notepad, Ctrl+V into the grid, in Append mode, with a range, and on the last row; (d) Ctrl+X on a range; (e) in Free mode Ctrl+C/V still copy/paste text. If any fails, note whether a toast/notice such as "Clipboard is not available" shows and send the log.

### Phase 118 - Create always opens blank (hard rule) - DONE 2026-10-11
**Reproduce-first result:** reproduced in jsdom, not in a real window. Cause: the Phase 70 subscription in `createSession.ts` (`dirty && billId !== null && currentId === null` -> `currentId = billId`) re-armed the session on ANY later dirty flip, including after the owner had left Create or right after Save/Discard (e.g. a late store write), so the next Create mount took the 'resume' branch and showed the old bill. Tests that fail on the old code: leave to history / dashboard / settings / customers then re-enter, a write after leaving, back-navigation, 'after Discard', and the Phase 70 resume tests themselves (7 of 13 new tests fail with the old subscription restored - checked).
**Fix (`stores/createSession.ts`):** removed the Phase 70 dirty-resume subscription; the session now ends the moment the router leaves Create (`useRouter.subscribe`: previous path Create, new path not Create -> `currentId = null`). Every plain entry (side rail, Dashboard, Ctrl+N, Esc/back, after Save, after Discard) therefore mounts a new clean bill via the existing `createEmptyBill` path (which already takes the machine defaults and the round-off flag). Unchanged: the Save / Discard / Cancel guard on leaving Create; `openBillForEdit`, draft recovery from the Dashboard banner and revision restore set the session just before navigating to Create from another page, so they still open their bill; New bill on Create; a same-visit double mount (StrictMode) keeps the bill.
**Updated in place:** two Phase 70 tests in `unsavedGuard.test.ts` that asserted the superseded resume behaviour now assert the Phase 118 rule (named 'Phase 118 ...').
**Tests:** `stores/__tests__/createAlwaysBlank118.test.ts` (13: every entry path = new id + blank; late dirty flip; back; after Save; after Discard; Ctrl+N; same-visit mount; edit route kept, but not on a later re-entry). Whole renderer suite: 49 files / 560 tests pass. `npm run typecheck`: pass.
**Decisions [A]:** crash-recovery drafts are only offered by the Dashboard banner (already the case; nothing on Create), so no change was needed for 'only at app start'. An abandoned unsaved bill that left Create without the guard (only possible if the guard is bypassed) is not resumed; its autosaved draft remains in the Dashboard recovery list.
**Not run:** build, dev server, e2e (not written: needs a real window), the whole suite outside `src/renderer` + routes. No schema / IPC / package change.
**STANDING GAPS:** owner must confirm on the real PC that Create is blank from every entry; I could not see the owner's actual 'pre-made bill' path, the cause above is the one the code and tests show.

### Phase 117 - Items page: names visible, scroll fixed, wide enough - DONE 2026-10-11
**Cause [A, not seen in a real window]:** the Groups and Sales lists used `@tanstack/react-virtual` on a scroll element owned by the page frame (rows `position:absolute` + `translateY`, Sales also with a `scrollMargin` for its sticky header). The owner's symptoms (blank after scroll, empty rows on Down while the other windows are fine) match the library measuring/updating against that element unreliably. I could not reproduce it in jsdom (jsdom has no layout), so the fix removes the dependency instead of tuning it.
**Built:** `shared/items/windowing.ts` (pure `visibleRange`, `scrollTopFor`, `UNMEASURED_ROWS`; fixed 26 px rows). `components/items/useScrollView.ts` (scroll top / height of the one scrolling content div, re-read on scroll, resize, list-length change; returns `read`). `GroupsWindow` and `SalesPanel` now draw plain in-flow rows between two spacers (no absolute positions, no transforms), scroll the selection / cursor into view with `scrollTopFor` (Sales accounts for its sticky header height), and draw a first screenful when the viewport is not measured yet (never blank). Zebra striping by row index (`q3-items__row--alt`; `nth-child` broke with spacers). Spellings: name column `minmax(0,1fr)`, the 'why' column capped at 5-9 rem (was 1.4fr vs 1.6fr, which truncated names); window widths now 1 : 1.3 : 1.5 (Spellings wider). Names keep their title tooltip. Sales stats: a rate of 0 / blank / non-number is 'no rate' (`positiveRate` in `sales.ts`), so min, median, Low/High flags and 'who paid what' ignore zero lines; the line stays a sale.
**Tests:** `shared/items/__tests__/windowing117.test.ts` (7), `components/items/__tests__/groupsWindow117.test.ts` (4: 3 200 groups, scroll, Down to the end with the selected row drawn / in view / not blank, End). Existing items suites (113, 114, page, sales, corpus) pass: 14 files, 185 tests. `npm run typecheck`: pass.
**Not run:** build, dev server, whole suite, the e2e (NOT written: needs a real window), a jsdom test with thousands of SALES rows (Sales shares the same helpers; covered only by the existing 114 tests). No schema / IPC / package change (`@tanstack/react-virtual` is still used by History and Customers).
**STANDING GAPS:** owner must confirm on the real PC that Groups no longer blanks and Down to the end renders in all three windows; whether the new column widths show full names at 1366 px; owner judgement on 'zero rate = no rate' (a free item given at rate 0 no longer counts as a price).

### Phase 116 - Notification discipline - DONE 2026-10-11
**Root cause FOUND (by reading + reproduced in jsdom):** `SaveFlow` (the only code that raises toasts) is mounted by Create, History AND Customers, and each mount started with `shown = 0`, so every page switch replayed the last announced event (e.g. 'Image copied - 675 x 228 px') as a brand-new toast. Esc through several pages = the stacked identical toasts in the owner's screenshot. Test `a page that mounts SaveFlow later does NOT replay...` fails on the old code's logic and passes now.
**Notices that can reach a toast (all via `useSaveUiStore.announce`, none other raise toasts):** save outcomes, copy TSV / image, print / PDF, history / trash / revision / customers / items actions, open-bill failures. Owner has not supplied exact texts; this is the code-derived list.
**Built:** (1) `SaveFlow` seeds `shown` with the existing event seq. (2) `Toast.tsx`: identical tone+message replaces in place and restarts its timer; `MAX_VISIBLE_TOASTS = 2` [A]; timers tracked and cleaned; `ToastApi.clear()`; pure `addToast`. (3) `components/RouteToastClear.tsx` (mounted in `App`) clears all toasts on every navigation (`navCount`). (4) `components/create/QuietStatus.tsx` in `BottomBar`: 'ok' announcements show as quiet text for 5 s instead of a toast; warn/danger always toast; if no quiet line is mounted (e.g. Customers) an 'ok' falls back to a toast. `saveUiStore` got `quiet`, `quietHosts`, `addQuietHost`. CSS `.q3-bbar__quiet` (tokens only).
**Decisions [A]:** cap 2; ALL 'ok' notices (incl. 'Saved PO...' and image/TSV copy) are quiet when the bottom bar is visible - the owner may want save results to stay toasts; undo/redo raise no notice today (nothing to move).
**Tests:** `components/ui/__tests__/toastDiscipline.test.ts` (11: 10 identical = 1, cap, timer restart, clear, page change clears, no replay on mount x3, quiet routing, fallback). Found and fixed my own bug (timer cancelled by effect deps) via these tests. Existing `create`, `stores`, `pages` suites: 13 files / 177 tests pass. `npm run typecheck` (all 4 configs): pass.
**Not run:** `npm run build`, dev server, whole suite, the e2e for 'Esc across all pages' (NOT written - needs a real Electron window, listed as a gap), any Electron window. No schema / IPC / package change.
**STANDING GAPS:** real Esc walk-through on the owner's PC; whether quiet text is visible enough in the bar at 1366x768 (owner judgement); e2e not written.

**Milestone 11 planning (docs only, 2026-10-11): ✅ DONE.** After running the Phase 115 build the owner reported: (1) stacked, unrelated notifications on every Esc page switch (screenshot: four identical 'Image copied - 675 x 228 px' toasts - which also shows the HD image is still 1.25x); (2) Items page: Groups window blank after scrolling, Spellings names truncated, empty rows when pressing Down while the other two windows still show data; (3) Create sometimes opens a pre-made bill - must ALWAYS open clear; (4) Create: Delete on a row should delete that row but keep the extra-column content; (5) rows must be re-arrangeable (drawbacks to be stated first); (6) round-off to the nearest rupee still not applied to grand totals; (7) Ctrl+C / Ctrl+V do not work on Create, and pressing Ctrl alone with a range selected loses focus; (8) Dashboard: weather tile (default Bangalore, configurable), live INR vs USD and 4-5 other symmetric tiles. **Found by reading the code (not run):** toasts are global, up to 5, never cleared on navigation; the Groups list is a `@tanstack/react-virtual` list with absolutely positioned rows; the grid controller passes bare modifier keys through the navigation reducer; the round-off flag is set at new-bill creation and at save. **No cause was confirmed for 3, 6, 7 - they are reproduce-first phases.** Written: `docs/MILESTONE11_DESIGN.md`, Phases 116–127 in `PHASES.md`, plus in-place additions to `CLAUDE_MUST_READ` (10f), `REQUIREMENTS_ORIGINAL`, `KEYBOARD_CELL_RULES` (section 14), `DESIGN_TOKENS`, `API_CONTRACTS`, `PROJECT_STRUCTURE`, `MASTERPLAN` (section 24), `README`, `ITEM_SORTER_SPEC`. **No code changed; nothing run.** Items marked [A] are unconfirmed assumptions.

**Milestone 12 planning (docs only, 2026-10-12): ✅ DONE.** Input: a handoff of an unresolved decrypt pause between two real computers (License max 5 branches; 1 927 bills and 12 893 customers on both; sending OK, pull paused with `reason: decrypt`) plus a design discussion from another session. Outcome: the encrypted-postbox sync is to be replaced by a shared, versioned Supabase notebook with Supabase Auth email/password per computer, a one-query generator for license + logins, a diff matcher, latest-version-wins, customer unify + Possible duplicates, a Realtime doorbell and a cutover wizard. **Written:** `docs/SYNC_SHARED_STORE_SPEC.md` (new, binding), `docs/MILESTONE12_DESIGN.md` (new), `PHASES.md` (Milestone 12, Phases 128-143), and edits in place to `CLAUDE_MUST_READ.md` (section 10g, section 3 note), `MASTERPLAN.md`, `README.md`, `PROJECT_STRUCTURE.md`, `API_CONTRACTS.md` (planned contract + change-log row), `docs/DATA_MODEL.md`, `docs/SECURITY_MODEL.md`, `docs/SUPABASE_MIGRATIONS.md`, `docs/SYNC_POSTBOX_SPEC.md` (banner), `DEPENDENCY_VERSIONS.md`. **Not done / not run:** no code, SQL, migration or package was changed; nothing was run against Postgres or Supabase; the Free-plan figures are third-party quotes from the discussion, not read from Supabase's own page. **Decisions that overrode earlier planner proposals:** no Main/Branch hierarchy (owner: latest version wins, any branch); read-only branch NOT built. **Assumptions [A] open:** see `MILESTONE12_DESIGN.md` section 4.

## Key decisions log

| Date | Decision | Why |
|---|---|---|
| 2026-10-07 | Electron, not Tauri | Owner has no Tauri/Rust experience; Chromium parity; Node built in |
| 2026-10-07 | No Python wrapper EXE | Second runtime, bigger/slower, higher antivirus false-positive rate |
| 2026-10-07 | Custom DOM grid; no grid library (Glide rejected) | Per-word red/bold, append-caret rule, F2, print parity need full cell control; ≤ ~80 rows is within DOM budget |
| 2026-10-07 | `userData` overridden to `%LOCALAPPDATA%\Quot3` | Electron's default is `%APPDATA%` (Roaming) |
| 2026-10-07 | SQLite (better-sqlite3) + Drizzle; money as decimal strings | Crash-safe, queryable; no floating-point money |
| 2026-10-07 | Formats are registry data; rows are a superset | Lossless switching; new formats without code changes (A4) |
| 2026-10-07 | Supabase = encrypted postbox + license RPCs only; no service key client-side | Owner: "supabase never stores bills data" |
| 2026-10-07 | Assumptions A1–A5 confirmed by owner; A6 (undo scope) proposed | See `REQUIREMENTS_ORIGINAL.md` Part B |
| 2026-10-07 | Vite 7.3.7 + plugin-react 5.2.0 + TS 5.9.3 + Tailwind 3.4.19 | Registry peer constraints: electron-vite 5 ≤ Vite 7; plugin-react 6 needs Vite 8; TS 7 untested; Tailwind 4 incompatible with chosen setup |
| 2026-10-07 | Neumorphism for chrome only; grid flat and high-contrast | Density and legibility at 26px rows |
| 2026-10-07 | Undo/redo is bill-wide with one history (native cell undo suppressed); trash 30 days; 50 revisions | `UNDO_REDO_RULES.md` |
| 2026-10-08 | better-sqlite3 relies on shipped N-API prebuilds; `postinstall` only rebuilds on load failure | 13.x ships ABI-stable prebuilds; works in Electron 44 without VS Build Tools |
| 2026-10-07 | 69 phases, gates at 24 / 40 / 69 | Grid, output and release are the three places where "looks done but isn't" is most likely |
| 2026-10-10 | Milestone 8 = Phases 70–91 added; Phase 69 (release gate) deferred to run after 91; no renumbering | Owner feedback after Phase 68 needed 22 phases of work before a release is sensible |
| 2026-10-10 | Round-off: default ON, nearest rupee, no displayed line, per-bill `round_off` flag (old bills unchanged until re-saved; imported 0) | Owner answers G1–G3 |
| 2026-10-10 | Smooth arrows setting `nav.smoothArrows` default ON; edit mode leaves via Esc/Enter/Tab AND Left-at-start / Right-at-end | Owner answered 'YES BOTH' |
| 2026-10-10 | Legacy import keeps only the number (no old prefix), recomputes totals, checks existing parties, sends to sync with progress UI | Owner answers H1–H4 |
| 2026-10-11 | Milestone 9 = Phases 92–106 added; Phase 69 (release gate) deferred again, to run after 106; no renumbering | Owner feedback on the running Phase 91 app |

### Phase 129 - License + login generator - DONE 2026-10-12
**Built (SQL, owner-run only):** `supabase/sql/14_license_admin.sql`. **Built (SQL only):** `supabase/sql/15_rpcs.sql`. Internal and never granted: `sn_name_norm`, `sn_member_org` (reads `auth.uid()`; order of checks: member Q3A01, then hashed key + active Q3L01, then this seat's branch Q3M01), `sn_history_add`. The spec, `PHASES.md` (M12 section), `MILESTONE12_DESIGN.md`, `API_CONTRACTS.md` section 3f and `SUPABASE_MIGRATIONS.md` were edited in place to the new names. Phase 132's transport must call `sn_*`. The owner-only `qt_create_org` family is unchanged.
**Other decisions [A]:** `base_version` different from the stored version in either direction (including 0 on an existing row, or a version above the stored one) takes the conflict path; the loser's incoming payload is stored in `sn_history` with `version = its base_version` (at least 1) because the stored row takes the next number; a `noop` is decided by `payload_hash` alone as the spec says, so the client's hash MUST include the deleted flag (Phase 133 codec); a takeover removes the seat's old branch row; `sn_branch_name_free` has an optional own-branch argument; an all-noop push does not ring the doorbell.

Phase 132's `InMemorySharedTransport` must pass `PUSH_CASES`. Ran those two files with vitest 5.0.3 from a temp folder: 53 passed. Typechecked the touched TS files alone with tsc 5.9.3 (`--strict --noUncheckedIndexedAccess --noUnusedLocals`, temp folder): no errors.

**STANDING GAPS:** all of file 15 on a real Supabase; a Supabase detail to confirm in the checklist: that `auth.uid()` is available inside these `security definer` functions with an empty `search_path` (it is the documented pattern, unproven here).

### Phase 132 - Client transport v2 + sign-in (in-memory first) - DONE 2026-10-12
**Built (main + shared, nothing wired into the running app yet):** `src/main/sync/sharedTransport.ts` (interface `SharedStoreTransport`; `InMemorySharedServer` + `InMemorySharedTransport` mirroring `15_rpcs.sql`; `SupabaseSharedTransport`, the only importer of `@supabase/supabase-js`, built on a small `SupabaseLike` interface so a fake client can stand in; `createSupabaseClient` makes the real client with `persistSession: false` and `autoRefreshToken: false`), `src/main/sync/activationV2.ts` (`createSharedActivation`: activate / status / deactivate / credentials / `connect()`), an appended Phase 132 section in `src/shared/sync/license.ts` (e-mail, seat-password and branch-name checks, `SHARED_MESSAGES`, `SHARED_SQLSTATE_KIND`, `classifySharedFailure`, `sharedKindToErrorCode`, `sharedLinkStateFor`, `ActivateInputV2`, `SharedLicenseStatus`), `ErrorCode` + `AUTH_FAILED` and `SERVER_PAUSED` (`shared/ipc.ts`). `@supabase/supabase-js@2.117.2` added to `package.json` and `package-lock.json` (see `DEPENDENCY_VERSIONS.md`). Files edited in place or appended; no existing function changed. The v1 transport, `licenseService.ts`, `crypto.ts` and `syncStateRepo.ts` are untouched (v1 stays dormant until Phase 142; v2 keys use the `shared.*` prefix of `sync_state`, so the two cannot collide).
**Behaviour:** sign-in with the seat's e-mail + password, then `sn_activate` (name, PO prefix, takeover), then one `setMany` of: URL, anon key, license (wrapped), `{email,password}` (wrapped), session (wrapped), org id, seat no, branches, registered branch, link. The local PO prefix is set to the registered one after success. Activation is refused when `safeStorage` is unavailable, for a `service_role` anon key, and in a read-only database, all before any network call. Error kinds `auth_failed` / `server_paused` / `offline` / `timeout` are distinct; `server_paused` is HTTP 540 or a "paused" answer [A]. The Supabase transport refreshes the token inside the 60 s margin and tells the activation service (which stores the new session); `connect()` re-signs in with the stored seat password only when the session itself is rejected, never after offline / paused. The renderer-facing `SharedLicenseStatus` has masked hints only.
**Tests (all run, 111 passed across the four files):** NEW `main/sync/__tests__/sharedTransport.test.ts` (38: the whole Phase 130 `PUSH_CASES` table against the in-memory server, history cap 20 incl. 26 chained updates in one call, pull paging / 4 MB cap / tombstones / bad cursors, manifest, activation uniqueness / takeover / branch conflict / rename, org isolation, suspended license, session refresh / stale restore, paused vs offline, two-branch convergence; `SupabaseSharedTransport` against a fake client: wire shape both ways, SQLSTATE mapping without retry, HTTP mapping (540, 5xx, JWT expired, wrong anon key, function missing, fetch failed, DNS), retry then success, timeout, wrong-shape reply, token refresh, doorbell; an import-boundary test that only `sharedTransport.ts` imports supabase-js), NEW `main/sync/__tests__/activationV2.test.ts` (11: activation, nothing secret in storage / status / audit / logs, refusals before any network call, failure leaves nothing stored, clashes and takeover, `connect()` restore / re-sign-in / offline / paused / before activation, `status({check})`, deactivate touches only `shared.*`), NEW `shared/sync/__tests__/licenseV2.test.ts` (checks and failure classification), plus the existing `pushTruthTable.test.ts` (still 11 + 4). How: vitest 5.0.3 run in the repo folder with `node_modules` symlinked to a scratch install (typescript 5.9.3, vitest 5.0.3, zod 4.6.5, vite 7.3.7, supabase-js 2.117.2; the symlink is not in the zip). Typecheck: `tsc 5.9.3 --strict --noUncheckedIndexedAccess --noUnusedLocals --noUnusedParameters` over the five new files and what they import: no errors in them (the only errors are missing `better-sqlite3`, `decimal.js` and `fuse.js` types in files this phase did not touch, because the scratch install does not have them).
**Not run:** `npm install` in the repo, the repo's own `npm run typecheck` (all configs), the whole suite, build, dev server (owner said skip the build). The real `@supabase/supabase-js` client was never run: `createSupabaseClient`, its auth calls (`signInWithPassword`, `setSession`, `refreshSession`), `abortSignal` on `rpc`, and Realtime `postgres_changes` are written against the documented API and checked only through the fake client; the cast `as unknown as SupabaseLike` in `createSupabaseClient` is where a mismatch would show. `better-sqlite3` was not available, so `activationV2` is tested with in-memory fakes of `SyncStateRepo` / `BranchRepo`, not the real SQLite repos.
**Decisions [A]:** (1) `server_paused` = HTTP 540 or a "paused" / "restore" answer with status 500+ (or none); a call with no answer at all is `offline`, because a paused project may simply not resolve. (2) The v2 service uses `shared.*` keys instead of reusing `license.*`. (3) The seat e-mail is stored inside the wrapped login blob; the status shows only `s***@domain`. (4) On activation the LOCAL branch's PO prefix is set to the registered one (`branchRepo.setPrefix`), so numbers cannot collide with another branch; the local branch NAME is not changed (the repo has no setter; the registered name lives in `shared.registeredBranch` and the server). (5) Retries: 3 tries with 0.4 s / 0.8 s backoff for offline, timeout and 5xx only; 20 s per try. (6) `deactivate` makes a best-effort sign-out and removes only `shared.*` keys. (7) The in-memory server mirrors one SQL detail the app must know: the server only trims the branch name, so the app collapses inner spaces first (`validateBranchName`). (8) `sn_now` has no membership check in SQL, so the in-memory `now()` also answers an unsigned caller (the grant to `authenticated` is what protects it).
**Carried forward / found:** the IPC `license:activate` channel, the renderer form and `sync:status` fields are NOT changed (Phases 140-142); nothing in the running app calls the v2 service yet. Phase 133 must hash the deleted flag into `payloadHash` (see Phase 130). `package-lock.json` lost one `"dev": true` flag on a package that supabase-js now needs at run time, which is expected.
**STANDING GAPS:** everything against a real Supabase and the real supabase-js client (sign-in, session refresh, RPC calls, Realtime); whether Supabase's paused-project answer really is HTTP 540 / contains "paused" (unconfirmed, owner can check in the checklist step on pause); `npm install` + repo-wide typecheck of the new files with all repo configs. Next: Phase 133 (local schema 0006, codec v2, set-aside).

### Phase 133 - Local schema 0006, snapshot codec v2, version stamp, set-aside - DONE 2026-10-12
**Built:** migration `0006_shared_sync_records.sql` (+ journal entry; tables `sync_record_state(kind, rec_id, base_version, base_hash, server_seq, synced_at)` and `sync_aside(kind, rec_id, version, min_reader_ver, payload_json, seen_at)`, both PK `(kind, rec_id)`; `parties.merged_into` nullable) and the matching Drizzle definitions in `schema.ts`. NEW pure `src/shared/sync/codecV2.ts`: `encodeRecord` / `decodeRecord`, `READER_VER = 1`, `SCHEMA_VER = 1`, `PAYLOAD_V = 2`, per-kind schemas (`bill`, `party`, `party_merge`, `item_rule`; loose, so unknown nested fields survive), content hash `hashPayload` (pure `sha256Hex`, no Node import) that ignores revision / updatedAt / createdAt / totals cache / syncState and INCLUDES the `deleted` flag (the Phase 130 / 132 carry-forward), `unknownJson` preserved and re-emitted, `min_reader_ver` never lowered when a record is pushed again, `asideEnvelopeJson` / `rawFromAside`. NEW `src/main/db/syncRecordRepo.ts` (state + aside SQL) and `src/main/sync/asideService.ts` (`ingest`, `retryAside`, `assertEditable`). `ErrorCode` + `RECORD_ASIDE`. `createBillService` got an optional `isAside(billId)`; `save` and `delete` refuse an aside bill; `main/index.ts` creates the repo and passes it. Existing tests edited in place: `db.test.ts` (table list), `itemRules.test.ts` (its "old DB" journal filter now drops 0005 and 0006). Docs updated in place: `API_CONTRACTS.md` (3f + change log), `docs/DATA_MODEL.md`, `PROJECT_STRUCTURE.md`.
**Behaviour:** the version check comes first and never reads the payload: `min_reader_ver > READER_VER` (or an unknown kind) -> `aside`; the record is stored whole in `sync_aside` (a newer set-aside copy replaces an older one, never the reverse), nothing is applied or overwritten, and the cursor is the caller's to advance. A readable record arriving later clears any aside row for it. `retryAside()` decodes every aside row with the current reader version: readable -> `apply` callback then row removed in one transaction; still too new -> stays; damaged JSON or invalid -> counted and kept; `apply` throwing -> kept, retried next time. `invalid` (damage from a same-or-older writer) is reported, never set aside.
**Tests (all run):** NEW `codecV2.test.ts` (15: sha-256 vs known vectors and node crypto, hash rules, deleted flag in the hash for every kind, round trip for all four kinds, unknown top-level and nested fields preserved and re-emitted, unknown bag cannot override known or reserved keys, aside / unknown kind / invalid cases, envelope round trip), NEW `syncRecords.test.ts` (4: a database at 0005 with bills and a party upgrades, new tables empty, `merged_into` NULL, pre-migration backup taken; column and primary-key shape; repo state and aside behaviour), NEW `asideService.test.ts` (8: set aside stores the payload untouched, readable passes through, invalid reported, local save and delete of an aside bill blocked through the real `createBillService`, retry before and after the reader version rises, apply failure, damaged row, newer copy wins). Also green: whole `src/main/db`, `billService`, `backupService`, `wipeService`, `bootstrap`, all `src/shared/sync` tests (vitest 5.0.3, better-sqlite3 13.0.3 in a scratch install, `node_modules` symlinked for the run only, not in the zip). `tsc -p tsconfig.node.json` shows no error in any file touched here (the remaining errors in the scratch run are the missing `electron` / `supabase-js` / `decimal`-type packages that the scratch install lacks).
**Not run:** `npm install` in the repo, the full `npm run typecheck` (all configs), the whole test suite, build, dev server (owner said skip the build). Nothing in the running app calls the v2 repo or service yet except the `isAside` guard in `bills:save` / `bills:delete`, which is always false until Phase 135 fills `sync_aside`.
**Decisions [A]:** (1) `sync_aside.payload_json` holds the whole record envelope, not only the payload, so a retry keeps `edited_at`, `schema_ver`, `app_ver` and `server_seq` (DATA_MODEL note). (2) The content hash ignores `createdAt` too, and for `party_merge` ignores `by` / `at`, for `item_rule` ignores `branchId` and the timestamps, so two branches making the same decision give the same hash. (3) An unknown `kind` is set aside, not rejected (the server's check list can grow). (4) Payload `v` other than 2 is `invalid`; a future format must raise `min_reader_ver`. (5) Bill rows are sorted by `idx` before hashing. (6) Only the local edit of a BILL is blocked in code now (save, delete); parties / rules get the same `assertEditable` hook when their writers are wired (Phases 135-137).
**Carried forward:** the Phase 134 diff matcher must compare against `hashPayload`; the Phase 135 applier must call `asideService.ingest` for every pulled record, advance the cursor for aside ones too, store `sync_record_state` for applied and aside ones, and show the count from `countsByKind()`; the UI line "Update the app to see N bills" and a call to `retryAside()` after an update are Phase 136 / 140 work.
**STANDING GAPS:** the codec against records written by a real newer app (none exists yet); the real `payload_hash` interplay with the server (the SQL stores the client's hash as given; checked only through the in-memory server in Phase 132); repo-wide typecheck with all configs and the full suite.

### Phase 134 - Diff matcher (pure) - DONE 2026-10-12
**Built:** NEW `src/shared/sync/diff.ts` (pure, no I/O, linear time): `planSync(local, remote) -> { skip, upload, download, conflict }` implementing the table of `SYNC_SHARED_STORE_SPEC.md` section 7 (skip items carry the server version / seq so `base_version` can be recorded; upload items carry the `baseVersion` for `sn_push`; conflict items carry both hashes and the remote version), `explainPair` (reason per record), `planCounts`, `resolveConflict(a, b)` (later skew-corrected `edited_at`, tie -> greater branch id, unparseable time loses, fully equal -> `a`), `offsetFromServerTime(serverIso, requestStartMs, responseEndMs)` (server time minus round-trip midpoint, null for unusable input), `CLOCK_WARN_MS = 120000`, `isClockSkewLarge` (strictly above 2 minutes, either direction), `correctedEditedAt`. The content hash is the Phase 133 `codecV2.hashPayload` (no second hash notion); no change to existing files.
**Tests (run):** NEW `shared/sync/__tests__/diff.test.ts` (15): each row of the table, no-base cases never overwrite silently, tombstones, one-bucket-per-record and determinism, codec hash feeding the plan, the owner's shapes with invented hashes (1 927 identical = all skip, 761 only-local = upload, 40 same-id-different = conflict, 12 893 customers planned in well under 500 ms), a seeded property test (60 seeds x 40 records: plan, apply both sides, resolve conflicts -> identical on both sides, no record lost), conflict tie-break, clock offset and a slow / fast Windows clock not deciding a conflict once corrected. `tsc -p tsconfig.node.json`: no error in `diff.ts`.
**Not run:** `npm install` in the repo, the full `npm run typecheck`, whole suite, build, dev server (owner said skip the build).
**Decisions [A]:** (1) base known = `baseVersion > 0` and a non-empty `baseHash`; "local unchanged" needs the base hash, "server still at base" needs only the version. (2) When both of those hold with different hashes (inconsistent state) the record is downloaded, which cannot lose a local change. (3) A server row missing for a local record that has a base is still treated as "only local" (tombstones are kept forever, so this should not occur). (4) Duplicate ids in an input list: the last one wins. (5) The manifest has no `edited_at`, so a conflict is only RESOLVED later, after the remote record is pulled (Phase 135/136); `planSync` only detects it.
**Carried forward:** Phase 135/136 must build `LocalEntry` from SQLite + `sync_record_state`, correct `edited_at` with the offset, pass conflicts to `resolveConflict`, and store the loser as a local revision.
**STANDING GAPS:** none new beyond Phase 133's (real Supabase, repo-wide typecheck).

### Phase 135 - Push/pull workers v2 + first compare - DONE 2026-10-12
**Built (main + shared; NOT wired into the running app: v1 stays live until Phase 140):** `src/main/sync/pushWorkerV2.ts` (`createPushWorkerV2`: takes unsent outbox rows oldest first, builds each record at send time with `codecV2`, sends `sn_push` batches of at most 50 rows / 4 MB with the `base_version` from `sync_record_state`; `accepted` / `noop` / `won` store the returned version + hash and settle the row; `lost` keeps the local version as a local revision and settles the row; connection / paused / login / license trouble pauses without blaming a row; a batch the server refuses is retried one row at a time and the culprit is blamed, 10 attempts -> `failed`), `pullWorkerV2.ts` (`createPullWorkerV2`: pages of 200 by `server_seq`, at most 10 pages per run, cursor saved after every page; `asideService.ingest` first; already-held version skipped; apply + `sync_record_state` in one transaction; a failing record never blocks the cursor: kept under `shared.pullFail.*` with the record itself, retried at the start of every run, set aside after 10 failed runs; a bill held because the editor has it open is retried without counting), `recordApplierV2.ts` (`createRecordApplierV2`: bill and item_rule; party / party_merge return `unsupported`), `compareService.ts` (`createCompareService`: first "Compare and sync" with `sn_manifest` + `planSync`, resumable per kind, progress events), `billRecordV2.ts` (`encodeLocalRecord`, `createLocalCompareSources`), `sharedState.ts` (`shared.cursor`, `shared.clock`, `shared.compare`, `shared.compareDone`, `shared.pullFail.*`; `ensureClockOffset`). Appended in place: `shared/sync/push.ts` (`SharedBlock`, `classifySharedWorkerFailure`, `sharedBlockFor`, `CLOCK_REMEASURE_MS`), `shared/sync/pull.ts` (`cursorAfterCompare`, `SHARED_PULL_PAGE_SIZE`, `SHARED_PULL_FAIL_CAP`). Small additions: `RemoteApplyRepo.heads()`, `OutboxRepo.dropUnsent(billId)`, `activationV2.deactivate` now removes EVERY `shared.*` key (cursor, clock, compare, retry list) so a new activation cannot inherit an old cursor.
**FIX of Phase 133 (decided, not asked):** `codecV2` content hash now also ignores `bill.partyId`. Reason: the party link is a per-computer id (a bill received on B is linked to B's own customer row, found or created by exact normalised name), so the same bill hashed differently on two computers and the first compare called 60 of 60 identical bills a conflict. This is the same notion as `sameContent` (which never looked at `partyId`); `partySnapshotJson` (the name on the bill) is still in the hash. One new codec test.
**Behaviour decisions [A]:** (1) A conflict found by the compare is NOT resolved by fetching the remote copy (the manifest has no `edited_at`): the record is queued with this computer's `base_version` and the SERVER decides by the later `edited_at` (tie: greater branch id), the loser goes to `sn_history` / a local revision, and the cursor is set low enough that the winner is pulled. (2) A received version is stored with a NEW local revision number (`local revision + 1`), so no local revision is ever deleted; the bill's `revision` column is not part of the hash. (3) Local UNSENT change vs received version: the later skew-corrected `edited_at` wins (the server's rule, applied early); a losing local change stays in the revision list and its outbox rows are settled; a winning one stays queued and its remembered `base_version` moves to the received version, so the next push is a plain `accepted`. (4) After the compare the pull cursor jumps over what the FIRST manifest fetched (bills) already showed, and starts just below the lowest record that still has to be downloaded or decided; taking the highest sequence over all kinds could jump over a record another computer wrote while the compare ran. Cost: records of other kinds with a higher sequence are pulled once more and skipped by hash. (5) `lost` does not write `sync_record_state` (the winner arrives with the next pull and replaces the local version); `won` / `noop` / `accepted` do. (6) Clock offset is measured against `sn_now()` at most every 10 minutes and shared by push, pull and compare; a failed measurement keeps the old value. (7) Records of kinds without an applier (party, party_merge) are counted `unsupported`, remembered nowhere, and the cursor passes them: nothing pushes them yet, and the cutover compare (Phase 136-140) must run again once parties are wired. (8) Item rules ride the same outbox (the row id says which); rules have no revision list, the server keeps the loser in `sn_history`.
**Tests (all run):** NEW `main/sync/__tests__/workersV2.test.ts` (26: save on A appears on B and nothing echoes back, own record skipped on the sender's pull, edit chains, tombstone, item rule; both edit - A first / B first / wrong clock 15 min fast / unsent local earlier / unsent local later, loser kept as a revision every time; 761 bills upload once and B downloads them; the page cap and cursor between runs; a new third branch gets everything from 0; replay after a lost cursor changes nothing and writes no revisions; compare: 59 skip + 25 upload + 1 conflict decided by the later edit, cursor jump with nothing to download, new empty computer = all download, resumable after a connection failure; failing record retried / set aside after the cap with the cursor passing it; held bill; newer-writer record set aside whole; offline / paused / suspended pause without blaming rows; refused batch isolated to the culprit; no license key in `sync_state`), NEW `sharedSim.ts` (N virtual branches on real temp SQLite over one `InMemorySharedServer`), NEW `shared/sync/__tests__/sharedFlow135.test.ts` (3), `codecV2.test.ts` + 1. Ran with vitest 5.0.3: `src/main/sync` + `src/shared/sync` + `src/main/db` = 34 files, 375 tests passed. `tsc -p tsconfig.node.json` shows no error in any file touched here (the remaining errors in a scratch run are missing `electron` / `electron-vite` / `exceljs` packages).
**Not run:** `npm install` in the repo (a scratch install with `node_modules` symlinked for the run only, not in the zip), the whole suite (a run of all of `src/main` + `src/shared` timed out in this sandbox; the touched areas above were run), `npm run build`, dev server (owner said skip the build). Nothing here ran against a real Supabase or the real supabase-js client; nothing runs in the app yet.
**Carried forward:** Phase 136 wires the applier into the app (open-bill banner via `isOpen` / `bills:remoteChanged`, party records, the History "Update the app to see N bills" line using `asideService.countsByKind()`, `retryAside` after an update) and must re-run the compare once party kinds have sources; `RecordApplierV2` already takes `isOpen` and `branches`. Phase 138 calls `pull.kick()` on the doorbell. Phase 140 wires `createPushWorkerV2` / `createPullWorkerV2` / `createCompareService` in `main/index.ts` and runs `compare.run()` when `compare.needed()`. The party link on a received bill is still found / created by exact name (v1 rule); Phase 137 unifies ids.
**STANDING GAPS:** everything against a real Supabase and real supabase-js; the workers inside the running Electron app (not wired); the owner's real shapes (1 927 identical, 761 only-local) were modelled with generated bills, not the owner's data; whole-suite run and repo `npm run typecheck`; a bill payload from an OLDER writer that lacks fields this build writes (e.g. `roundOff`) hashes differently from its local copy after it is applied, so a later compare would upload it once (harmless: the server stores a new version with the full content).

**Decisions [A]:** (1) "Keep mine" moves the remembered server version to the received one, so the next push from this computer builds on it and wins even when its `edited_at` is earlier: it is the owner's explicit choice. ### Phase 137 - Customers: exact-name auto-merge, Possible duplicates, synced merges - DONE 2026-10-13
**Built:** `shared/customers/duplicates.ts` (pure): `partyRecordId(orgId, nameNorm)` / `mergeRecordId`, `chooseSurvivor` (most bills, tie oldest, tie smaller id), `fillBlanks`, `planUnify` (equal normalised names only, idempotent), `findPossibleDuplicates` (rules `same-core` Ltd/plural/punctuation, `one-letter`, `starts-with`; digit-bearing words must match; blocked by the first 3 letters of the first word, so 12,000 customers finish fast; `notSame` pairs left out). Migration `0007_customer_merge` (`party_merge_log`, `party_not_same`). `partyRepo`: merge SQL, log, not-same list, `allIds` / `mergeIds`. `customerService`: `duplicates`, `merge`, `notSame`, `undoLastMerge` (newest batch; a unify is one batch), `unify`; optional deps `newPartyId`, `emit`; `billService` / `partyService` take optional `newPartyId` + `partyCreated` / `partyChanged`. Sync: `billRecordV2` encodes `party` and `party_merge` for push and lists them in the compare (customers and merges FIRST, so a bill never precedes its customer); `pushWorkerV2` takes `parties`; `recordApplierV2` applies `party_merge` (and its undo), resolves a received bill's customer through `merged_into`, and folds a bill-auto-created (`source = 'sync'`) customer into the real record when it arrives; `remoteApplyRepo`: `resolveParty`, `applyPartyMerge`, `restorePartyMerge`, `adoptSyncParties`. IPC `customers:{duplicates,merge,notSame,undoLastMerge,unify}` + preload. UI: `DuplicatesPanel`, `duplicatesStore`, Customers page header button and keys. Shortcuts `customers.duplicates` Ctrl+D, `customers.merge` M, `customers.notSame` N (M / N act only while the section is open and no text box has the key), `customers.undoMerge` Ctrl+Shift+U; `customers.unify` palette only.
**Tests:** NEW `shared/customers/__tests__/duplicates.test.ts`, `main/services/__tests__/customerMerge.test.ts` (real SQLite), `main/sync/__tests__/customerMergeSync.test.ts` (two branches: same name = one customer id, unify converges and a second unify plans nothing, merge + undo move bills on both, a late bill links to the survivor), `renderer/.../duplicatesPanel.test.ts` (incl. selector stability). Counts and ids only; no real names. Edited existing tests: `db.test.ts` (two new tables), `syncRecords.test.ts` and `itemRules.test.ts` (skip 0007 with 0006; upgrade is +2 migrations). `sharedSim` gained an opt-in `parties: true` so older suites keep their counts.
**Decisions [A]:** (1) "Not the same" is a local list, never synced. (2) Two branches merging the same customer into different survivors: the first merge received stays; running unify again converges them (the survivor rule is deterministic). (3) A remote undo moves bills back by matching the bill's saved customer name to the restored customer. (4) Bill links stay a local fact: merges re-point `bills.party_id` with no revision and no outbox row. (5) Similar names are never merged automatically; a first-word typo in its first 3 letters is not suggested (blocking limit). (6) The survivor's record is always sent with a merge (it may never have been pushed).
**Carried forward:** Phase 140 wires `newPartyId` (`partyRecordId(orgId, ...)`), `emit` (outbox capture) and `parties` for the push worker / compare in `main/index.ts`, runs `customers.unify()` once after the first compare and after pulls, and re-runs the compare once party kinds have sources; Phase 138 `pull.kick()`.

### Phase 138 - Realtime doorbell + poll fallback - DONE 2026-10-13
**Realtime limits re-read (2026-10-13), Supabase's own page `supabase.com/docs/guides/realtime/limits`, Free plan:** 200 concurrent connections, 100 messages per second, 100 channel joins per second, 100 channels per connection, 256 KB payload. That page gives no monthly message allowance, so the old "about 2 million a month" is NOT confirmed (pricing page not read). Recorded in `SYNC_SHARED_STORE_SPEC.md` section 10 and `SUPABASE_MIGRATIONS.md` item 19. Project pause and no-backups facts were not part of this page and are still "not checked".
**Built:** `shared/sync/pull.ts`: `SHARED_POLL_MS` (60 s), `DOORBELL_DEBOUNCE_MS` (500 ms), `doorbellReconnectMs` (2 s doubling, cap 5 min), `pollDelayMs(failures, baseMs)` (`pullDelayMs` unchanged), `deliveryText` ("Live" / "Checking every minute"). NEW `main/sync/doorbell.ts` `createDoorbell({ connect, orgId, kick, log, onMode }, { setTimer, clearTimer, now })`: subscribes through the existing `transport.subscribeDoorbell`, one debounced `kick()` per burst of rings, ignores everything a dropped subscription says afterwards, reconnects with backoff, cancels the retry when the socket recovers, kicks once when it becomes live again, does nothing without an organisation, `stop()` clears everything. `pullWorkerV2` takes `pollMs` (default 30 s, so the v1-shaped tests are unchanged). `SyncOverview.delivery` / `deliveryText` (+ optional `live` input) in `shared/sync/manager.ts`. The Supabase `subscribeDoorbell` (written in Phase 132) is unchanged: it was already main-only, filtered to the organisation, and reports `SUBSCRIBED` as live. No renderer, CSP or preload change.
**Tests (all run):** NEW `main/sync/__tests__/doorbell.test.ts` (8): burst of rings = one pull after 500 ms; Live / Checking every minute and the catch-up kick; backoff 2 s / 4 s / 8 s, reset on success, a dead subscription's rings ignored, a recovered socket cancels the retry; no organisation, `stop()`; poll is 60 s with backoff and the 30 s default intact; overview words; against the in-memory server a push on A rings B and ONE debounced pull brings the bill; with rings dropped the 60 s poll brings it. `sharedSim` gained an optional `pullOpts`.
**Not run:** whole suite, `npm run build`, dev server (owner: only required tests), anything against a real Supabase (Realtime delivery, the socket really dropping, token refresh on a long-lived socket).
**Decisions [A]:** (1) A ring waits up to 500 ms and then the pull worker adds its own 250 ms start delay, so the worst case from ring to pull is about 750 ms. (2) The poll is the worker's timer, not the doorbell's, so it keeps running when the doorbell is stopped or never connects. (3) "Live" means the socket reported `SUBSCRIBED`; it says "Checking every minute" until then and whenever the socket is down. (4) Reconnect re-subscribes from scratch; supabase-js's own rejoin is welcome too (a late `SUBSCRIBED` cancels the pending retry).
**Carried forward:** Phase 140 wires it in `main/index.ts`: `createPullWorkerV2(..., { pollMs: SHARED_POLL_MS })`, `createDoorbell({ connect, orgId, kick: pull.kick })` started after sign-in and stopped on sign-out, `onMode` into `deriveOverview({ live })`, `pull.notifyOnline()` on network regain, a pull after Save; Phase 139 renders `deliveryText` on the Sync status page.
**STANDING GAPS:** Realtime against a real Supabase; project pause / backups facts; everything is unwired in the running app.

### Phase 140 - Cutover from v1 (wizard, safety backup, first compare) - DONE 2026-10-13 (service + wizard built and partly tested; NOT wired into the app)
**Built:** NEW `shared/sync/cutover.ts` (pure): `CUTOVER_KEYS` (all `sync_state` keys `cutover.*`), `cutoverStep(facts)` (backup -> signin -> compare -> unify -> done, DERIVED from what is stored, so an interrupted wizard, a restart or a second window resumes in the right place), `cutoverNeeded`, wizard words (the backup step says why: the Free plan has no server backups), counts-only compare sentence, `CutoverBridge` contract. NEW `main/sync/cutoverService.ts` (`createCutoverService`): `begin()` makes a fresh local backup FIRST (kind `manual`) and only after it exists writes `cutover.backupFile` + `cutover.v1Inert` in one write and stops the v1 workers (a failed backup changes nothing and v1 keeps running; a second `begin` makes no second backup); `compare()` needs backup + sign-in, runs the existing resumable `compareService`, calls `afterCompare` once when it finished, and is a no-op once finished; `unify({ skip })` needs the compare, runs the exact-same-name unify (or skips), then records the cutover as finished and the v1 license as abandoned (`cutover.v1Abandoned`, audit `cutover_finished`, counts only). The v1 `license.*` keys stay readable and untouched (Phase 142 removes them). The service has no v1 transport, so v1 rows are never read. `syncManager.ts` (in place): `inert()` and `suspend()`; while inert the v1 workers never start, `overview()` reads as not activated, `syncNow` / `setEnabled` are refused. NEW `main/ipc/cutover.ts` (`cutover:status / begin / compare / unify`, strict zod; written, not registered). Renderer: NEW `stores/cutoverStore.ts` and `components/settings/CutoverWizard.tsx` ("Step n of 4"; step 2 embeds the normal `SharedSyncSection`; skip button on the customer step; focus moves to the step's button); `SyncSection.tsx` shows the wizard only when `window.quot3.cutover` exists (otherwise exactly as before).
**Tests (all run):** NEW `shared/sync/__tests__/cutover.test.ts` 7/7 and NEW `main/sync/__tests__/cutoverService.test.ts` 16/16 (fakes for the state table, backup, compare and customers: backup before pause, failed backup changes nothing, no second backup, gating, paused compare resumes, re-run is a no-op, unify / skip, restart resumes, no key / password / token in logs or audit). They ran under a throw-away stand-in for vitest (outside the repo) driven by `tsx`, because `npm install` crashes in this sandbox again ("Exit handler never called", twice; one of them the single allowed retry; `node_modules` removed, lockfile byte-identical to the one in the zip). Global `tsc` 6.0.3 (strict, noUncheckedIndexedAccess, noUnused*) over `cutover.ts`, `cutoverService.ts`, `syncManager.ts`, `ipc/cutover.ts` and `cutoverService.test.ts`: no error in my code (only "cannot find module zod / vitest" from the missing install, and one `exactOptionalPropertyTypes` complaint in an old `syncManager` line that the repo's own tsconfig does not enable).
**Written but NOT run:** `main/sync/__tests__/cutoverSim.test.ts` (the simulation the phase asks for: two computers holding the same bills plus one-sided bills end identical after the wizard on each, shared bills stay on server version 1, re-running is a no-op, an interrupted wizard resumes after a restart and after a connection loss in the compare, the customer step; default sizes are a TENTH of 1 927 / 761 / 1 166, `Q3_CUTOVER_FULL=1` runs the real sizes); needs `better-sqlite3`. `renderer/components/settings/__tests__/cutoverWizard.test.ts` (6 jsdom cases: all four steps, failed backup message, skip, resume, not needed, no bridge); needs react / jsdom. One new case appended to `main/sync/__tests__/syncManager.test.ts` (inert); needs better-sqlite3. Also not run: the whole suite, `tsc -p tsconfig.node.json`, `tsc -p tsconfig.web.json`, `npm run build`, dev server, Electron (all skipped as instructed or impossible here).
**Run these on your PC:** `npm install`, then `npx vitest run src/shared/sync/__tests__/cutover.test.ts src/main/sync/__tests__/cutoverService.test.ts src/main/sync/__tests__/cutoverSim.test.ts src/main/sync/__tests__/syncManager.test.ts src/renderer/components/settings`, `npx tsc -p tsconfig.node.json --noEmit`, `npx tsc -p tsconfig.web.json --noEmit`. Likely trouble spots if the sim fails: the content hash of the same bill saved on both computers (must be equal), and `syncOne` (the loop until the outbox is empty).
**Decisions [A]:** (1) The safety backup uses the existing `manual` kind (no new backup kind, so the frozen backup tests and the auto-keeper are untouched; a manual file is never pruned). (2) The wizard has no stored step; the step is derived from facts (backup file, v2 signed in, compare done, unify answered, finished). (3) The v1 workers are stopped by an "inert" flag in `sync_state` that `syncManager` reads, not by deleting v1 settings; Phase 142 deletes them. (4) "Suspend / abandon the v1 license note" is recorded as `cutover.v1Abandoned` + an audit row; nothing is sent to Supabase about it. (5) Step 2 reuses `SharedSyncSection`; the wizard moves on by itself when sign-in succeeds. (6) The customer unify is optional and says "on ONE computer, after both have compared"; the service does not check the other computer. (7) No new shortcut or palette entry: the wizard is a one-time flow inside Settings > Sync, every control is a normal focusable button (a registered shortcut would also edit frozen shortcut tests). (8) Old unsent v1 outbox rows are not deleted: the v2 push worker builds each record at send time and the compare re-queues the same ids, so they are harmless; this is [A] and untested against a real stuck queue.
**Carried forward (IMPORTANT, the app is not connected yet):** nothing creates the service or the v2 engine at start-up. Still to do, in one wiring phase (suggested "140b", before Phase 142; Phase 141 does not need it): in `main/index.ts` build `activationV2` (`makeTransport` -> `SupabaseSharedTransport`), `compareService` with `createLocalCompareSources`, the v2 push / pull workers, `recordApplierV2`, `heldService`, `asideService`, `doorbell` (start after sign-in, stop on sign-out), `customers` with `newPartyId = partyRecordId(orgId, ...)` and `emit`, `syncManager.suspend` as `stopV1`, `backup.create` as the backup, `customers.unify`; outbox capture must be on while EITHER the v1 or the v2 activation is on; register `cutoverHandlers` and the Phase 139 `syncShared` handlers in `ipc/register.ts`, add both bridges to `shared/ipc.ts` and the preload, feed `serverPaused` / `signedOut` / `clockOffsetMs` into `deriveOverview`, send the transport `kind` in error envelopes (Phase 139 carried this to 140; it is still open), run `compare.run()` when `compare.needed()`, `pull.kick()` on the doorbell and after Save. Phase 142 removes `LegacySyncSection`, the v1 code and keys. Until then the owner's computers still run v1 exactly as before.
**STANDING GAPS:** everything above that was not run; real window, real Supabase, real backup file on Windows, real two-computer cutover with the owner's 1 927 / 761 / 1 166 bills (the simulation uses invented bills); safeStorage; that stopping v1 and signing in on a real project works end to end. The owner's real cutover order (backup on both, generate license + two logins, computer 1 then computer 2, unify on one) is `MILESTONE12_DESIGN.md` section 6.

### Phase 141 - Simulation harness v2 and convergence tests - DONE 2026-10-13
**Built:** (1) `main/sync/flakyTransport.ts` (appended in place): `FlakySharedTransport`, the Phase 60 hostile-network idea for the v2 `SharedStoreTransport`: seeded, loss before the server, loss of the answer (work done, reply lost), DUPLICATE delivery (push / pull replayed, the caller gets the first answer), a `down()` switch, virtual delays, `heal()`, per-call `only` filter, metrics. Sign-in / session / doorbell calls are never lost. (2) `main/sync/__tests__/sharedSim.ts` (in place): new option `wrapTransport(t, branchIndex)`; nothing else changed, the other suites are untouched by it. (3) NEW `main/sync/__tests__/simHarnessV2.ts`: `createHarnessV2({ seed, branches 2..4, skewMs, parties, loss, billPool })` = N real temp-SQLite branches (real bill / trash services, outbox, v2 push / pull workers, applier, compare) over ONE `InMemorySharedServer` through a `FlakySharedTransport` each; two seeded generators (edits, network); random `step()` = edit / delete / restore / push / pull / network flip with the virtual clock moving 0..3 s first; `converge()` (heal, network up, jump 15 min over any back-off, settle); `snapshotOf` / `assertConverged` (identical content on every branch, nothing queued, server and branches agree on deleted flags; failures print seed + counts, never content). Windows-flake note for the soak is written in that file's header and below. (4) NEW `main/sync/__tests__/harnessV2.test.ts`.
**Tests (all run):** `harnessV2.test.ts` 19/19: wrapper itself (2), six seeded 3-branch property seeds 101-106 (60 ops, lossy: 12 % lost before, 10 % answer lost, 15 % duplicated), 4 branches with customers travelling (seed 201), "the harness really injects trouble" guard, same-seed replay gives identical content, a second full sync changes nothing (also server versions), 40 ops with the network DOWN then up, customer merge + undo across 3 branches while others edit, version skew (old reader sets aside, keeps its copy, counts it, other bills keep flowing, applies after `setReaderVer`; server record untouched), clock offsets (A +10 min fast, B -10 min slow, B's edit 30 s later in real time wins in both push orders on every branch, the loser is kept on the server history / local revision) + same-skew control, and the 1 000-operation soak (seed 20261013, 3 branches, pool 30). Soak timing recorded on this sandbox: ops {edit 427, delete 117, restore 66, push 137, pull 168, netflip 85}, run 1.3-1.4 s, converge 0.08 s, 334 transport calls, 16 lost before, 19 answers lost, 24 duplicates, 154 refused while down, 30 server records; whole file about 7-8 s. Also run: `applyV2`, `customerMergeSync`, `doorbell`, `harness` (v1), `workersV2` = 72/72 together with the new file (these use `sharedSim.ts` / `flakyTransport.ts`); `cutoverSim` 4/5 (below). `tsc -p tsconfig.node.json --noEmit` clean (strict).
**Environment note:** `npm install --ignore-scripts` worked this time (once); the native `better-sqlite3` was built for Node 22 with `node-gyp rebuild --nodedir=/usr` (no prebuilt binary for this sandbox), outside the zip. `package-lock.json` is byte-identical to the one received. `node_modules` is not in the zip.
**Not run:** `npm run build`, dev server, Electron (skipped as instructed); the whole suite; `tsc -p tsconfig.web.json` (no renderer file changed); the jsdom `cutoverWizard.test.ts` and the `syncManager.test.ts` inert case from Phase 140 (not touched by this phase; still owed); the soak on Windows; `Q3_CUTOVER_FULL=1` (real 1 927 / 761 / 1 166 sizes).
**Changed frozen / earlier files (smallest edits):** `cutoverSim.test.ts` line 133 had a missing `;` (Phase 140 wrote it without running it, so the whole file failed to load); fixed. Fixed file result: 4 of 5 cases pass (backup once and before v1 stop, re-run is a no-op, interrupted wizard resumes, customer step). **ONE assertion still fails (Phase 140 test, not fixed here):** in "same bills + one-sided bills", everything converges (identical ids and content, nothing queued) but the final check "shared bills are still on server version 1" gets 2. Likely cause [A, not confirmed]: the test seeds the same bill id on both computers through each computer's own `bills.save` (own PO prefix AA / BB, own timestamps), so the content hashes differ and the compare treats it as a conflict (later edit wins, version 2) - a test-fixture artifact, not a data-loss bug; the real joined-by-backup computers hold byte-equal copies. Phase 140 itself named "the content hash of the same bill saved on both computers" as the likely trouble spot. Next step if you want it green: seed one computer, copy rows to the other with the same PO number and timestamps.
**Findings:** (a) A trashed bill that arrives from another computer is NOT linked to a customer (`recordApplierV2` links only live bills, by design), so a trashed bill's customer link differs between computers until it is restored; the harness snapshot therefore ignores the customer of trashed bills. [A] (b) With customer sync OFF (the default in `createSim`) each computer makes its own customer ids, so the harness compares customer NAMES only there and ids only when `parties: true`. (c) If the first `now()` call (clock measurement) is lost on a lossy network the first push goes out with an uncorrected `edited_at` until the next re-measure; the property seeds use no skew, the clock tests use no loss. Worth a look in Phase 140b wiring, not changed here. (d) No convergence failure was found in the real engine by any seed.
**Decisions [A]:** (1) The harness lives in `__tests__` (test-only), `FlakySharedTransport` lives next to the v1 `FlakyTransport` as the phase's key-files list says. (2) Fixed seeds 101-106, 201, 301, 401, 501, 601, 602, 777, 20261013. (3) "Duplicate delivery" = request replayed, first answer returned. (4) Soak size 1 000 operations on 3 branches with a 30-bill pool (many conflicts). (5) 4 branches is the harness maximum (`createSim` has four PO prefixes). (6) Random merges are NOT part of the random op mix (concurrent merges of the same pair on two computers are a design question, not a convergence proof); a merge + undo is a separate deterministic test. (7) No new shortcut, palette entry, IPC channel or contract: nothing user-facing changed, so `API_CONTRACTS.md` is unchanged.
**Windows-flake note (soak):** the soak uses virtual time only and a seeded generator, so its result does not depend on the machine; the only environment risk is a slow disk making the file exceed vitest's timeout (the soak case sets 280 s, the file normally takes under 10 s). A red soak on Windows = rerun with a larger `--testTimeout` first; only if `assertConverged` throws is it a real bug, and the printed seed + counts replay it exactly.
**Carried forward:** the v2 engine is STILL not wired into `main/index.ts` (see the Phase 140 log; suggested phase "140b" before 142). The red cutoverSim assertion above. Phase 140's owed jsdom / syncManager tests.
**STANDING GAPS:** everything is in-memory: the real Supabase (RLS, RPC SQL, Realtime, token refresh, timeouts) is not exercised; real Windows clocks; real two-computer behaviour; the in-memory server rules are a second writing of `15_rpcs.sql`, so a rule that differs in SQL is invisible here until the owner runs `docs/SUPABASE_MIGRATIONS.md` and `91_selftest_v2.sql`.
