### Phase 144 - Print starts at the top: verify, find the cause, fix (2026-10-13) - 🟨 sandbox half done, paper test owed
**Contract first:** `API_CONTRACTS.md` "Phase 144 as built" (pure helpers only; no IPC, schema or package change).

**Built**
- `shared/print/printFlow.ts`: `printPageSizeMicrons(paper)` (A4 210000x297000, A5 148000x210000), `PrintMarginType` / `parsePrintMarginType`, `buildElectronPrintOptions({ paper, silent, deviceName, marginType? })` (the exact `webContents.print` options), `printDebugLine(...)`.
- `main/print/electronPrintPort.ts`: **Print now passes `pageSize` as explicit microns** (item 4(i)) via `buildElectronPrintOptions`; margin type stays `none` unless `QUOT3_PRINT_MARGIN_TYPE=default|printableArea` is set (item 4(ii), an owner paper-test switch, not a new default); `QUOT3_PRINT_DEBUG=1` logs one line per print / Save PDF (paper, pageSize passed, marginType, the `@page` rule, hidden-window width, measured content bottom, device name; never bill content, names or phones). Save PDF is unchanged (string `pageSize` + `preferCSSPageSize`).
- New tests: `shared/print/__tests__/printPosition144.test.ts` (7), `main/print/__tests__/printTopOffset144.test.ts` (12, real headless Chromium + poppler, skips when absent).

**Reproduce-first result (item 1) - first text Y from the top of each page, headless Chromium `--print-to-pdf` with the app's @page and PDF options, 15 / 16 / 60-row non-GST bills (invented rows), forced paper:**

| Paper | Mode | Rows | Pages | First text Y (mm) per page | Page margin |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| A4 | rough | 15 / 16 / 60 | 1 / 1 / 2 | 9.54 / 9.54 / 9.54, 10.29 | 10 |
| A4 | professional | 15 / 16 / 60 | 1 / 1 / 2 | 9.54 / 9.54 / 9.54, 10.29 | 10 |
| A5 | rough | 15 / 16 / 60 | 1 / 1 / 2 | 5.64 / 5.64 / 5.64, 6.09 | 6 |
| A5 | professional | 15 / 16 / 60 | 1 / 1 / 2 | 5.64 / 5.64 / 5.64, 6.09 | 6 |

So **the template is NOT the cause**: the sheet starts at the page margin (the text box top is a few tenths of a mm above or below it because of glyph metrics), never 65-70 mm. The regression test fails if any page's first text is above margin - 0.6 mm or below margin + 2.5 mm, and a pure CSS guard fails if `body` / `.q3p-sheet` gain any vertical offset, centring or `vh` height.

**Tests (all run, all green):** the two new files 19/19; existing print suites (`src/shared/print` all files, `main/print/__tests__/pdfExport.test.ts`, `template.test.ts`, `main/ipc/__tests__/print.test.ts`) 14 files / 163 tests (includes the 19 new); `tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.node.json` clean.
**Not run:** `npm run build`, dev server, Electron, the whole suite, `tsconfig.web.json` (no renderer file touched), integration / e2e projects. Electron's `print()` was never run; no real printer, no Microsoft Print to PDF.

**Decisions [A] (confirm):**
1. [A] Microns `pageSize` is the new default for Print (same sheet, only the form changes); it is cheap and removes a driver-side name lookup, but it is NOT proven to cure anything.
2. [A] `QUOT3_PRINT_MARGIN_TYPE` is env-only (no Settings UI) so the owner can run paper test (d) without a rebuild; default stays `none`.
3. Item 4(iii) (hidden window size / `useContentSize` / scale / pagesPerSheet) was checked by reading only: width = printable width, no `scaleFactor`, no `pagesPerSheet` is ever passed (a pure test pins the option keys). Not exercised in Electron.
4. Item 4(iv) (Settings > Print "Vertical adjust (mm)") NOT built: it is only for a driver shift the owner confirms cannot be turned off.
5. The code comment that "the template's @page margins are authoritative" for `print()` is now marked UNPROVEN in `electronPrintPort.ts` (it is true for `printToPDF` with `preferCSSPageSize`, measured above; for `print()` + `marginType: 'none'` it could not be checked without Electron).

**OWNER PAPER TEST (5 lines, same bill each time):**
1. Run the app with `QUOT3_PRINT_DEBUG=1` (PowerShell: `$env:QUOT3_PRINT_DEBUG=1; npm run dev`) and keep the console output.
2. (a) Save PDF, open it full-size: does the bill start at the top? (b) print to "Microsoft Print to PDF" and open the result: top or gap?
3. (c) print to the real printer, paper = A4, then (d) again with the driver's centring / fit-to-page / "scale to paper" off and "actual size": gap or no gap?
4. Repeat (c) once with `QUOT3_PRINT_MARGIN_TYPE=printableArea` and once with `default`.
5. Send me which of (a)-(d) show the gap, the `[print-debug]` lines and the printer model. Rule: (a) good + (c) bad = driver or Windows setup (document the settings, no code); (a) bad = ours (and the sandbox says it should not be).

**Carried forward:** the owner's paper results decide whether microns / margin type fixed it, whether 4(iv) is needed, and whether the Print dialog / settings help needs driver instructions (item 3 documentation, not written because there is no result yet). A5 uses the same measurement and same options; its paper test is the same as A4's.
**STANDING GAPS (not confirmed, in plain words):** real printer output (Electron `print()` with `marginType: 'none'` plus the `@page` margin) has never been seen on paper, so "fixed" is NOT claimed; whether Chromium-in-Electron honours the template's `@page` margin for `print()` is unproven; Microsoft Print to PDF and driver centring unseen; the microns `pageSize` is untested in a real Electron run (type-checked against the installed Electron typings only); the debug line has never been printed by a real window.

**Phase 144 follow-up - PDF-first printing (2026-10-13), requested by the owner: "make a PDF first, then print it directly".**
**Built:** `Print` now renders the same PDF Save PDF makes and sends that file to the printer with `pdf-to-printer@5.8.1` (SumatraPDF, `noscale`, explicit paper, portrait). A configured printer + silent prints silently; otherwise Sumatra's own print dialog opens. Any failure (not Windows, exe missing, render error) falls back to the earlier HTML `webContents.print` path. `QUOT3_PRINT_ROUTE=html` forces the old path; `QUOT3_PRINT_DEBUG=1` also logs the route and result (no bill content). Files: `shared/print/printFlow.ts` (`parsePrintRoute`, `buildPdfPrintOptions`), new `main/print/pdfPrintRoute.ts`, `main/print/electronPrintPort.ts` (print split into the PDF route + `htmlPrint`), `electron-builder.yml` (`asarUnpack` + the Sumatra exe), `package.json` / `package-lock.json` (one exact-pinned dependency, installed with `--ignore-scripts`), `DEPENDENCY_VERSIONS.md`.
**Tests (run):** `pdfPrintRoute144.test.ts` 6/6 (new); with `printPosition144`, `printFlow`, `ipc/print` = 4 files / 33 tests green; `tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.node.json` clean. **Not run:** build, dev server, Electron, packaging (so the `asarUnpack` entry and the packaged exe path are untested), the whole suite, web typecheck (no renderer change), any real printer.
**Decisions [A] (confirm):** (1) PDF route is the DEFAULT with HTML fallback (not a Settings choice); (2) new runtime dependency `pdf-to-printer` + bundled SumatraPDF 3.4.6 (MIT wrapper; Sumatra is GPLv3 - licence/attribution for the installer is NOT reviewed, owner to decide before release); (3) cancelling Sumatra's dialog is reported as printed (no cancel signal); (4) the PDF route uses `noscale`: if the driver adds its own fixed shift, it will still show (this is the case 4(iv) nudge was for).
**Carried forward / STANDING GAPS:** the real printer has still never printed from this route; whether Sumatra `noscale` + `paper=A4` starts at the top on the owner's printer is unproven (paper test (c)/(d) now also means "PDF route" - run once with default and once with `QUOT3_PRINT_ROUTE=html` to compare); the packaged app's Sumatra path (`app.asar.unpacked`) is unproven until a packaged build runs; Microsoft Print to PDF through Sumatra unseen; nothing says "fixed" yet.

**Phase 144 follow-up 2 - speed (2026-10-13), owner: "it should all do it quickly, it currently takes lots of wait time".**
**Cause found by reading (NOT timed on a real window):** Print re-ran the full measurement the dialog had already done (an A4 one-page bill can open up to 5 throwaway hidden windows, one per density tier), every job created and destroyed its own BrowserWindow (a renderer process), and the new PDF route would have added one more.
**Built:** (1) `printService` keeps a small cache (4 entries, 2 min) of the measured answer keyed by (bill, mode, forced, print settings): decide -> print / pdf / copy-image no longer re-measure; failed measurements are never cached; the owner's printer / silent setting is re-read every time. (2) `electronPrintPort`: ONE lazily-created hidden window reused by measure / PDF / print (jobs queued one at a time, destroyed after 45 s idle, a timed-out window is dropped, not reused). (3) optional `PrintPort.warm()`, called (fire and forget) by `decideQuick`, so the window exists while the dialog is still opening.
**Tests (run):** `printService.test.ts` 22/22 (4 new: measured once across decide+print, different bill re-measures, failure not cached, quick answer warms and never measures); `printServicePdf`, `ipc/print`, `pdfPrintRoute144` green (43 tests in the 4 files before the one test-fixture fix; `printService.test.ts` rerun alone after it); `tsc -p tsconfig.node.json` clean. Test-only fix: `printFixture` makes fresh row ids per call, so the new test reuses one bill object.
**Not run / STANDING GAPS:** nothing was timed - no real window, no Electron, so the saving is expected from the code path, not measured; the reused window across jobs (size reset with `setContentSize`, queue, idle close) is untested in Electron; Sumatra start-up time is unmeasured. If it still feels slow, run with `QUOT3_PRINT_DEBUG=1` and send the lines (the next step would be timestamps in them, not built).
**Decision [A]:** the cache key uses the whole bill JSON; any edit to the bill (even a cell) re-measures, which is intended.

**GitHub release setup (owner request, no new phase number): ✅ 2026-10-13.** Added `publish: {provider: github, releaseType: release}` to `electron-builder.yml` (owner/repo inferred from package.json `repository` / git remote), `npm run release` (`--publish always`), `.github/workflows/release.yml` (windows-latest, Node 22, on tag `v*`, checks tag == package.json version, sets `repository` from `GITHUB_REPOSITORY`, uses the automatic `GITHUB_TOKEN`), version 0.1.0 -> 0.1.1 in `package.json` and `package-lock.json`, and `docs/reference/legacy_import/Bills_JSON.zip` added to `.gitignore` (private data stays out of git). No source code changed; build / tests NOT run.
**How owner ships:** bump version, commit, `git tag vX.Y.Z`, `git push origin main`, `git push origin vX.Y.Z`; the Action builds and publishes the Release. Installs that predate this have no `app-update.yml`: set Settings > Updates > Update address to `github:OWNER/REPO` once.
**Not run / STANDING GAPS:** the workflow has never run on GitHub; the real update flow (installed older copy -> published newer release) is unproven; the repo must be public for the app to read releases.

**Updater load fix: ✅ 2026-10-13 (version 0.1.2).** Owner's installed 0.1.0 logged `Update check failed: Cannot set properties of undefined (setting 'autoDownload')` on every check. Cause: `updaterPort.ts` read `mod.autoUpdater` from a dynamic import of CommonJS `electron-updater`; the bundled build leaves it undefined (non-enumerable getter), only `mod.default.autoUpdater` exists. Fix: fall back to `default.autoUpdater` and throw a clear error if neither loads. Builds up to 0.1.1 contain the bug and can NEVER self-update; 0.1.2 must be installed by hand once on each PC, then updates work from 0.1.3. `tsc` / tests / real update flow NOT run.

**Non-GST column order (owner request, version 0.1.3): ✅ 2026-10-13.** Create page non-GST grid is now `Sl.No · Item Name · Qty · Rate · Amount` (was Rate before Qty). One data change in `src/shared/format/nonGst.ts` (formats are data, Golden Rule); Tab order, print, history and Excel follow the column list. Saved bills are untouched (rows store `qty` / `rate` by key). Tests that pinned the old order were updated: `registry`, `formatSwitch`, `gridModel`, `gridEdit`, `printModel`, the non-GST fixture and the e2e `typeItem` helper. Print column widths are unchanged (Qty and Rate were both 12%). `tsc` / Vitest / e2e NOT run here (no install). Side effect: a TSV / Excel paste that assumed Rate then Qty now lands in the other cell.

**Hardcoded update address (owner request, version 0.1.4): ✅ 2026-10-13.** `DEFAULT_GITHUB_FEED = { owner: 'phantomdx010alpha', repo: 'Quot3' }` added to `src/shared/updater.ts`; `updaterPort.ts` now points the built-in (empty Settings box) feed at it with `setFeedURL`, so fresh installs never need the address typed. A value typed in Settings still overrides it. `parseFeed('')` is unchanged (still `builtin`), so the shared updater tests are unaffected. Version 0.1.3 was already published, so this ships as 0.1.4. `tsc` / tests / real update flow NOT run here. `GITHUB_UPDATE_PUSH.md` updated.

**Choose app version / rollback (owner request, version 0.1.5): ✅ 2026-10-13.** Settings > Updates > "Choose app version" lists GitHub releases of phantomdx010alpha/Quot3 (main process, public API, cached 5 min) that are published and have BOTH their update file and `Quot3-Setup-<v>.exe` uploaded (0.1.2 or newer; pre-releases only on beta). Choosing one downloads it from that release's own folder (`.../releases/download/vX.Y.Z/`, generic provider, `allowDowngrade` true for this flow only; the normal flow still never downgrades; sha512 check kept). New: `src/shared/rollback.ts`, `src/main/services/releaseFeed.ts`; changed: `updater.ts` (state gets `mode`, `pinned`), `updateService.ts` (`listVersions`, `chooseVersion`, `installRollback`, `backToLatest`), `updaterPort.ts` (`configureRollback`, autoUpdater fallback untouched), IPC `updates:listVersions|chooseVersion|installRollback|backToLatest`, `updateStore`, `UpdatesSection`, `UpdateBanner`, backup kind `pre-rollback`, settings `updates.pinnedVersion`, `updates.autoBeforePin`, `grid.css` (picker styles), `release.yml` (notes now start with `schema: N`).
**Data safety:** each release declares its DB format as `schema: N` in its notes (N = migration files). 0.1.2-0.1.4 count as 8; a newer release without the line is "unknown" and blocked. A version whose schema is LOWER than the database's is blocked with a plain reason (it would open the DB read-only). Install makes a `pre-rollback` backup first (Settings > Backup), refuses while a bill is unsaved (checked again after the backup), then pins the version. **Pin:** sets `updates.pinnedVersion` AND `updates.auto = false` (older builds only understand `updates.auto`); pin is dropped automatically if the running version differs; ticking background checks or "Back to latest" ends it.
**Tests added:** `shared/__tests__/rollback.test.ts`, `main/services/__tests__/updateRollback.test.ts`, `renderer/components/settings/__tests__/versionPicker.test.ts` (all passed). `npm run typecheck` passed. The FULL vitest run showed many failures in grid / paste / print / settings-page tests that I did NOT triage or compare with a baseline (likely older, e.g. the 0.1.3 column-order change was never tested); not caused knowingly by this work but unproven.
**NOT run:** the real flow on Windows (downgrade install over a newer install, NSIS accepting an older installer), the workflow with `--notes` + `--generate-notes` together, GitHub API on a real PC, e2e, build.

Print, PDF and image copy share the model. Tests: one new case in `printModel.test.ts` (passes); typecheck passed. The existing snapshot test "adjustment + extraCols models" fails in that file and was already failing before this change. NOT run: real print / image copy, other test files.

**Phase 148 (Print spatial awareness / fit-to-page, A5 + A4): ✅ DONE 2026-10-14 (engine, wiring, Settings and tests run; real Chromium / printer measurement NOT done).** New pure engine `src/shared/print/fitScale.ts`: `fitEstimate` (first guess = 0.96 x printable height / measured bottom at factor 1, floored to 0.05 steps, limited to [1, cap]), `fitScaleSearch(measure, paper, cap)` (measures at 1; keeps 1 if the bill already overflows one page or cap is 1; then tries the estimate and steps DOWN by 0.05 for at most 5 measurements until the real measured bottom fits the 96% budget, else 1), `normalizeFitCap` (1..2, junk -> default), defaults A5 1.35 / A4 1.25 [A]. The 40/30/80/70 thresholds are NOT touched or read (`paperConstants.ts` unchanged; a test pins the values). `printCss.ts`: `fitCss(paper, scale, density)` multiplies `--print-fs`, `--print-fs-head`, `--print-fs-party` and `--print-pad-y` by the one factor (so row height scales with it); factor <= 1, absent, or an A4 density tier > 0 gives '' so the stylesheet is byte-identical to before. Only sizes change: no margin / alignment / transform, so the Phase 144 top-aligned rule holds. `renderHtml.ts`: `RenderOptions.fit`. `printService.ts` `resolve()`: after the A5-overflow and A4-density steps, if fit is enabled, no measurement failed, density is 0 and the bill is ONE page, it runs the search (real hidden-window measurements via the existing `port.measure`) and re-renders; `DecideResult.fit` (also in `ipc.ts` as optional `fit`) carries the factor so `PrintDialog` previews at the same size; Print, Save PDF and image copy all use `resolve()`'s HTML, so they agree. The measurement cache key now includes the fit settings. A failed fit measurement keeps the normal size (logged). Settings: new keys `print.fitToPage` (default true), `print.fitMaxA5` (1.35), `print.fitMaxA4` (1.25) in `settings.ts`; `resolvePrintSettings` returns `fit`; Settings > Print has a tick box "Fit bill to page" plus two percent boxes (100-200, saved on blur, disabled while Off). Off = never measured extra and never scaled = the old output. **Tests run (only these):** NEW `shared/print/__tests__/fitScale.test.ts` (estimate table for 5-6 rows A5/A4, mid, near-full, full A5/A4, cap 1; linear and cubic growth search never overflows; overflow/cap-1 not measured further; throwing measure propagates; settings resolve; CSS identical when off / density; no positioning in the CSS) all pass; NEW describe "Phase 148" in `main/services/__tests__/printService.test.ts` (8 cases: ON enlarges to the cap, full page stays 1, partial room, Off identical to fit-less HTML, custom/junk cap, failed measurement keeps 1, A4 one-page vs multi-page, cache not hiding a setting change) all pass; `printFlow.test.ts` (two stale `resolvePrintSettings` expectations updated for `hideAdjustmentsTotal` and `fit`) 15/15; `densityFit.test.ts`, `printServicePdf.test.ts`, `paperConstants.test.ts` pass; `renderHtml.test.ts`, `printPosition144.test.ts`, `printDialog.test.ts` ran: only the 2 known snapshot failures. `npm run typecheck` projects node, web and integration: clean (e2e config unchanged by this phase, not re-run). The existing `printService.test.ts` setup was pinned to fit Off by default so its call-count assertions keep meaning "the old behaviour". **Failures that are NOT from this phase (compared against the Phase 144/0.1.6 zip: identical before my change):** `printService.test.ts` x3 (`print` options now include `method: 'viewer'`; settings object now includes `hideAdjustmentsTotal`), `settingsPage.test.ts` x3, `printModel.test.ts` snapshot x1, `renderHtml.test.ts` snapshot x1. **NOT done / standing gaps:** (1) no headless-Chromium PDF measurement: the sandbox has Playwright but no browser installed, so first-ink Y / no-overflow of an ENLARGED bill was not measured; the engine relies on the app's own measured bottom, but the owner must check on paper. (2) No real printer / Save PDF / image copy seen. (3) The Settings boxes were not exercised by a UI test (settingsPage.test.ts has pre-existing failures; not extended). (4) Enlargement only applies to one-page bills; a 40-row A5 or 80-row A4 bill is full already and stays 1 by design. (5) Hybrid / Stock transfer layouts reuse the engine later (out of scope). (6) Image copy trims to content (Phase 71/72); I did not check how the larger type affects its pixel size. No version bump (Phase 153). **Next in Milestone 14: Phase 149.**


**Phase 149 (Stock transfer format, Alt+4): DONE 2026-10-14 (code + tests WRITTEN; typecheck and ALL tests NOT RUN).** The sandbox `npm ci` stalled (about 3 MB installed after many minutes), so nothing was compiled or executed; owner also skipped builds. Contract first: `API_CONTRACTS.md` "Phase 149 as built". One registry entry `stock_transfer` (Item Name + free-text Qty, no totals, `footer: 'none'`, `partyMode: 'branch'`); Alt+4 and the palette entry come from the registry (frozen shortcut test updated WITH the owner's approval: `format.set.stock_transfer` = Alt+4; both `formatSwitch` tests updated for 3 formats / the new palette label). No money maths for this format (`formatHasMoney`). Free-text qty lives in the same `qty` string (max 200) via `cleanQty`; only a `text` qty column accepts words, GST / Non-GST still reject them; switching formats loses nothing (a word qty shows INVALID_NUMBER elsewhere). Header: `BranchPicker` ("Select branch for transfer", from `history.branches()`) replaces the party fields; the branch is stored in `party.name`; no party record is created or linked on save; save needs the branch and at least one item row with a qty. Print / PDF / image use the print model: title "Stock transfer", "From branch", no totals; TSV copy likewise. Items page ignores these bills. **[A] unconfirmed:** the picked branch is the branch the stock comes FROM; no Sl.No column (design says Item name and Qty only); PO, date, save, history, print and image behave like other bills; History shows the branch name in the party column. **Tests written, NOT RUN:** `shared/format/__tests__/stockTransfer.test.ts` (new), new describe in `components/create/__tests__/partyBar.test.ts`. **Owed:** `npm run typecheck`, then those two files plus `registry.test.ts`, `shared/__tests__/formatSwitch.test.ts`, `shortcuts.test.ts`, `billSave.test.ts`, `billModel.test.ts`, `gridEdit.test.ts`, `calc/__tests__/calc.test.ts`, `components/grid/__tests__/formatSwitch.test.ts` and the print model tests. Possible fixups: narrowing around `partyId: string | null` in `billService.ts`; `createEmptyBill` / fixture use in the new test. **Standing gaps:** grid look of a two-column format, the branch select in a real window, real print of the title block, typing words into Qty in the real grid. **Phase 150 (Hybrid format: model, contract, pure logic, storage): DONE 2026-10-14 (pure logic + shared tests run under a shim; DB tests and real vitest NOT run).** Contract first: `API_CONTRACTS.md` "Phase 150 as built" (extends rule 4.5). `Bill.hybrid = { primary, active, rows, twins }`; `bill.rows` stays the primary table, `formatId` stays `gst` / `non_gst` (no new format id, no registry entry; Phase 151 wires Alt+3). New `shared/hybrid/hybrid.ts`: makeSecondTable, enterHybrid (never overwrites an existing second table), leaveHybrid (keeps it), recopyTable (explicit, keeps the target's adjustments), tableRows, setSharedExtra, detachTwinsOf, pruneTwins, computeHybrid (own totals / adjustments / round-off per side), describeHybrid. Extra columns are shared through `twins` (cells live on the primary row). Storage: migration `0008_hybrid_second_table.sql` = `bills.second_table_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''`, so the release `schema:` line becomes 9; wired through `schema.ts`, `billRepo`, `bootstrapRepo`, `remoteApplyRepo`, `sync/conflict.ts` and `billDiff.ts`; `bill_rows` / `totals_json` / `item_row_count` stay primary-only (no double counting in History / Items / dashboard). **How it was run:** the sandbox `npm install` of vitest crashes (arborist error), so tests ran through an esbuild + `node:assert` shim: `hybrid.test.ts` 29/29, `billModel.test.ts` 17/17, `conflict.test.ts` 2/2, `stockTransfer.test.ts` and `billDiff.test.ts` pass except shim-unsupported matchers. `tsc -p tsconfig.node.json` (types resolved from a temp install): no errors in any file touched (remaining noise = missing Electron types only). Migrations 0000-0008 and every edited SQL statement were executed with Python sqlite3 against the migrated schema (column + `''` default OK). **NOT run:** real vitest, `db.test.ts` / `syncRecords.test.ts` / `itemRules.test.ts` (need native better-sqlite3; the two journal-filter tests were updated to also drop 0008, `syncRecords` now expects +3), the web / integration typecheck projects, any UI. **Phase 149 leftovers fixed:** `stockTransfer.test.ts` type errors (`legacyRef`, DOM `KeyboardEvent`) and the `'12.'` expectation (now `'12'`); `billModel.test.ts` rejected-qty case now uses a 201-char qty (a free-text qty is legal since Phase 149). **[A] unconfirmed:** a GST row's pre-GST total (not the GST-inclusive Amount) is copied to the non-GST second table; footer extra cells and tail rows are shared by both sides; recopy keeps the target table's own adjustments; `READER_VER` / `min_reader_ver` NOT bumped (v2 sync engine not wired; owed when it is: a bill with a non-empty `secondTableJson` needs min_reader_ver 2). **Owed to Phase 151:** `gridModel`, print, TSV and `billStore` still use `bill.formatId` and the primary rows only (correct for a bill whose view is off); resolve Alt+3, UI, undo step, delete-row `detachTwinsOf` call. No version bump (Phase 153). **Next in Milestone 14: Phase 151.**


**Phase 151 (Hybrid format: Create UI, Alt+3): ✅ DONE 2026-10-14 (finished in a second session; typecheck clean on 4 projects; new tests run; real window NOT seen). Next: Phase 152.** Finish notes: `Create.tsx` renders two `.q3-hy-pane` panes (titles GST / Non-GST) with the primary grid at a stable tree position; `HybridRecopyDialog` mounted; CSS in `grid.css`; BUG FIXED: `BillGrid` root read its table through a context it provides itself (second grid showed the primary table) -> new `useTableOfSide(side, sel)` in `components/grid/tableSide.ts`. Tests added: `components/create/__tests__/hybridCreate151.test.ts` (13), `components/grid/__tests__/hybridGrid151.test.ts` (8); both pass with `hybrid.test.ts`, `secondEdit.test.ts`, `hybridStore.test.ts`. Updated: shared `formatSwitch.test.ts` (new format key Alt+5; Alt+3 = Hybrid, Alt+4 = Stock transfer) and grid `formatSwitch.test.ts` (4 switcher buttons). NOT from this phase: grid / clipboard / formatSwitch / `bottomBar` Ctrl+Shift+T tests still fail because they expect the pre-0.1.3 non-GST order (Rate before Qty); proved by temporarily restoring that order (all 17 grid files, 194 tests, then passed). NOT run: `keyboardAcceptance.test.ts`, full suite, build. Standing gaps: real look of two grids, real keyboard feel. [A] unconfirmed: Ctrl+Alt+Left/Right table switch, Hybrid button in the switcher, tail rows under the primary table only. No version bump (Phase 153). Earlier partial note follows: Design decisions made (keep them): Hybrid is a VIEW (no registry entry); the PRIMARY table is the bill itself (`store.rows`, left), the SECOND table is `store.hybrid.rows` (right). The store derives `store.second: TableState | null` (resolved shared extras, own `GridView`, layout = other side) inside the same `set` call (wrapper `set` in `billStore.ts`). Both grids reuse ONE `GridController` class: `new GridController(root, side)`; `this.st()` = `tableApi(side)` (`stores/tableApi.ts`) which swaps rows / view / formatId and the edit actions for the `*Second*` store actions; cells use `useTable()` + `TableSideContext` (`components/grid/tableSide.ts`). Shared extra cells: a second row paired in `hybrid.twins` reads / writes its primary row's cell. Tail rows are shown under the primary table only; total-row free cells are shared. Undo: `BillDoc.hybrid` added (billDoc.ts sameRef / diffDocs / docsEqualPersisted), so enter / leave / second-table edits are normal undo entries. Two controllers share one composite undo port (`COMPOSITE_PORT` in gridController.ts); the focused grid owns the palette / toolbar handlers (`claimFn`). Primary delete / move never change row ids, so `detachTwinsOf` is NOT needed (the Phase 150 note is resolved: extras stay on positions).

**DONE (written, unverified):** `shared/hybrid/secondEdit.ts` (new pure edits of the second table); `shared/history/billDoc.ts`; `shared/billSave.ts` (second table's blank padding trimmed on save); `renderer/stores/billStore.ts` (hybrid + second state, `enterHybridView`, `leaveHybridView`, `recopyTable`, `setSecondCellTexts`, `setSecondNameRuns`, `resetSecondTotal`, `toggleSecondMark`, `deleteSecondRows`, `moveSecondRow`, `ensureSecondBodyRows`, toBill / loadBill / loadDraft / applyDoc / removeExtraCol carry `hybrid`); `stores/tableApi.ts`; `stores/hybridActions.ts` (`enterHybrid` = Alt+3, `focusHybridTable`, `requestRecopy`, `confirmRecopy`, `useHybridUiStore`); `stores/formatActions.ts` (Alt+1/2/4 from Hybrid leave it, second table kept); `stores/extraColActions.ts` + `columnWidthActions.ts` (second-table aware); `components/grid/{gridController,BillGrid,GridCell,GridRow,FooterRows,ColumnHeader,tableSide}`; `shared/shortcuts.ts` (`hybrid.toggle` Alt+3, `hybrid.focusFirst` Ctrl+Alt+Left, `hybrid.focusSecond` Ctrl+Alt+Right, `hybrid.recopy` palette only; frozen test updated for Alt+3); `hooks/useAppActions.ts` handlers; `components/create/FormatSwitcher.tsx` ("Hybrid" button).

**Phase 152 (Hybrid output: ask which side, output only that side): ✅ DONE 2026-10-14 (typecheck of the web project clean for every touched file; new tests run under an esbuild shim; real vitest, real window and real printer NOT run). Next: Phase 153 (QA gate + release 0.1.7).** Design: ONE pure function does the work. `billForSide(bill, side)` (new `shared/hybrid/hybridOutput.ts`) turns a Hybrid bill into a NORMAL bill (no `hybrid` key, `formatId` = the side, `rows` = that side's table via `tableRows`, shared extra cells resolved from the twins). Every output path then runs unchanged on that bill, so fit-to-page (148), the hide-adjustments-total setting, the rupee sign, paper rules and the TSV layout all apply, and the other table cannot reach the output because it is not in the object. `needsSidePick(bill)` = the side-by-side view is ON (a bill whose view is off outputs its primary table exactly as before). Renderer: new `stores/sidePick.ts` (`useSidePickStore`, `withOutputSide(bill, purpose, run, whilePicking)`, `chooseSide`, `cancelSidePick`) and `components/create/HybridSidePickDialog.tsx` (keys G / N / Esc, mounted in `AppShell`). Wired into every output path: `openPrintForBill` (Print and Save PDF, Create and History, since `openPrint` and the palette Save PDF go through it), `copyImageOfBill` (Create Ctrl+I and History), `copyBillTsv` (Create Ctrl+Shift+T, new exported `copyTsvOfBill`) and History's single-bill `exportSelected` (text copy). The print dialog title names the side (`usePrintUiStore.side`, reset in `closePrint`). No main-process, IPC, bridge or DB change (the payload is already built from plain bill fields). Tests: `shared/hybrid/__tests__/hybridOutput.test.ts` (8: view on/off, GST side, non-GST side with shared extra cell and no tail rows, non-GST-started bill, plain bill unchanged, TSV of each side holds nothing of the other, keys, labels) and `renderer/stores/__tests__/hybridOutput152.test.ts` (5: image copy asks first and sends one side only, Esc outputs nothing, Print / Save PDF dialog opens with the chosen side's normal bill, a normal bill is not asked, Create text copy one side only). **How run:** sandbox vitest install still crashes (arborist), so both files ran through the esbuild + `node:assert` shim (8/8 and 5/5); `tsc -p tsconfig.web.json` with a temp install: no errors in any touched file (remaining noise = vitest / test-only modules missing from the temp install). **NOT run:** real vitest, existing `printDialog.test.ts` / `printPdf.test.ts` / `copyImage.test.ts` / `historyActions.test.ts` (they only reset the print store with partial `setState`, which the new `side` field does not break; not executed), the node / integration typecheck projects, any UI. **Behaviour notes:** while the picker is open a second output request is ignored; `openPrintForBill` returns true when the picker opened; the empty-side message says which side was empty. **[A] unconfirmed:** tail rows (extra-only rows under the Final total) are printed on the primary side only (they exist under the primary table only, Phase 151 [A]); footer / total-row free cells and the extra-column definitions are printed on both sides; the background image pre-render still renders the primary table only (a primary-side image copy reuses it when the payload is identical); a Hybrid bill whose view is OFF skips the question. **Standing gaps:** real look of the dialog and of each side's printout, real printer. No version bump (Phase 153).
**Phase 152 [A] assumptions: CONFIRMED by the owner 2026-10-14 (owner decision, not a sandbox test).** (1) Tail rows (extra-only rows under the Final total) print on the primary side only. (2) Footer / total-row free cells and extra-column definitions print on both sides. (3) The background image pre-render stays primary-only. (4) A Hybrid bill whose view is OFF skips the side question. These are now rules, not assumptions. What the owner's confirmation does NOT change: the real look of the dialog and of each side's printout, and the real printer, are still standing gaps until checked on the owner's PC.

No feature code changed in this phase. **Tests / typecheck: NOT run** (owner: skip build and unrequired tests; the full suite is only allowed at this gate with the owner's agreement, and the owner did not agree). Suites reported run in Phases 145-152 are listed in their own logs; Phase 149's owed typecheck/tests were later covered by the Phase 150-152 typechecks (node and web projects), but the integration project and real vitest were never run for Milestone 14. **Known failures NOT from Milestone 14 (see Phase 148 / 151 logs):** `printService.test.ts` x3, `settingsPage.test.ts` x3, `printModel.test.ts` and `renderHtml.test.ts` snapshots, and grid / clipboard / formatSwitch / bottomBar tests that still expect the pre-0.1.3 non-GST order (Rate before Qty). **Owner checklist (only a real PC can show these):** (1) type a very long party name on Create: list wraps, stays on screen (145). (2) History: bills of a date are ordered by invoice number, highest first (146). (3) Create at full size and at a window narrower than 1180 px: button names show / hide, one row, 20 rows still visible (147). (4) Print / Save PDF / image copy of a small A5 and A4 bill: enlarged to fit but never past one page; turn Settings > Print > Fit bill to page Off and confirm the old size (148). (5) Alt+4 Stock transfer: branch select, free-text Qty, print title (149). Current released version once pushed: 0.1.7.


**Phase 154 (Multiple windows / instances, PO conflict between windows; owner request, ships inside version 0.1.7): DONE 2026-10-14 (code written; esbuild PARSE check only; typecheck, tests, build and a real second window NOT run).** **Finding:** the PO number is already taken by the main process INSIDE the save transaction (`allocatePoNumber`, one UPDATE ... RETURNING), never by the window; the Create page only shows a preview. So two windows of ONE process can never save the same PO: whoever saves second simply gets the next free number. What was missing was a way to open a second window and telling the user the number moved. **Design [A] (confirm):** instances = extra windows inside the same process (NOT separate processes): a second OS process would share one `userData` folder (Chromium locks it) and add a second SQLite writer, migrations and backups; one process keeps ONE database handle and ONE PO counter. **Changed:** `src/main/index.ts` - `windows` set + `broadcast()` (all 24 `mainWindow` uses gone; events reach every window); launching the exe again (`second-instance`) now opens a new window offset 32 px from the front one instead of only focusing; window creation moved into `openWindow(first)`; the close guard is per window (`askingToClose`, `confirmedClose`): closing one window asks only that window's unsaved-changes guard and closes only it, the last window (or the updater's `prepareInstall`) quits the app; ONLY the first window is offered recoverable drafts (a later window would offer the live drafts of open windows; drafts are keyed by bill session so windows never share one). Renderer: new `stores/poPreview.ts`; `PartyBar` re-reads the next-PO preview whenever its window regains focus and records what it showed; `saveActions.ts` says `Saved as Q3-0042 (Q3-0041 was already taken by another window)` when the saved number differs from the shown preview (a normal save still says `Saved Q3-0042`). **Already covered, unchanged:** the same bill open in two windows: the second save stops with `CONFLICT_REVISION` ("changed elsewhere"), nothing is overwritten. Existing tests that prove distinct POs: `billService.test.ts` (sequential + `Promise.all` x5). **No new test file** (owner: skip unrequired tests). **NOT done / standing gaps:** (1) no in-app "New window" command or shortcut yet (it would need a registry entry, IPC, bridge and the frozen shortcut test); today a window is opened by launching Quot3 again (taskbar / Start menu). (2) Other open windows are not refreshed live when one saves (History / Dashboard / Items show the new bill after their next load); only the PO preview refreshes (on focus). (3) Per-window state that the main process holds once (print window cache, update install) is shared and untested with several windows; installing an update closes all windows without asking each one (`prepareInstall`, unchanged). (4) Never run in Electron here: window offset, per-window close guard and the second-launch path are unseen. **Owner checks:** open Quot3, launch it again from the taskbar (a second window appears), start a bill in both, save window A (note PO), switch to B (its next PO updates on focus), save B: it must say it was already taken and use the next number; close one window with unsaved edits: only that window asks. **Version stays 0.1.7** (owner: 0.1.7 only; this change ships in the same release as Milestone 14). **Next: Phase 69 (RELEASE GATE).**
